Chapter 2693
Sword Comprehension Liquid
Wang Shaoqi said, "Yes, it's Sword Comprehension Liquid! Using it can increase one's compatibility in a short time … "
"Spirit liquid that increases compatibility?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He stared at Wang Shaoqi and asked, "Do you have it?"
Wang Shaoqi was extremely unwilling, but he didn't dare to disobey Luo Zheng. He trembled as he took out a small jade bottle from his spatial ring and said, "I only have this bottle!"
Luo Zheng took the small jade bottle and gently removed the cork on top.
This small jade bottle emitted a pure Sword Fate Eternal True Meaning aura. That aura soared into the sky and was extremely sharp.
"Someone integrated the sword intent into the water." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Yue Baicheng said, "This true intent aura is very similar to brother Luo Zheng's true intent aura."
These disciples had all fought with Luo Zheng before. Through the battle, their souls had felt Luo Zheng's true intent aura, leaving a deep impression. This aura was very similar to the aura of this Sword Comprehension Liquid.
Luo Zheng began to understand the situation.
If one's compatibility wasn't high, then one had to borrow the true intent aura of others. This was also a method.
And from the looks of it, Great One Sky Palace still didn't care about this cheating method …
"Can this Sword Comprehension Liquid be made by oneself?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Wang Shaoqi shook his head and said, "How is that possible? The person who made this Sword Comprehension Liquid has perfect compatibility with the sword intent of Eternal Sword Cloud. How can an ordinary person make a genius that can comprehend the dao with a single thought? This Sword Comprehension Liquid was bought by my Wang family at a high price … "
"If there is perfect compatibility, can you make it yourself?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"I don't know." Wang Shaoqi continued to shake his head.
Sword Comprehension Liquid wasn't easy to obtain. The key was that this thing could only increase the compatibility of the sword intent for an instant. Adding on the high price, ordinary people couldn't afford it. Most people would only use it once when crossing the sword wall.
"How many god crystals does this bottle cost?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Twenty thousand," Wang Shaoqi replied.
Hearing this number, the disciples of the academy were speechless.
The Qiu Clan and the Yue Bai Clan were great clans that had established the foundations of the Dao Sword Palace, but in the face of such a high price, it was likely that they would not be able to afford it.
Those disciples were originally interested in the Sword Comprehension Fluid, but now they gave up on the idea. They couldn't expect to spend 20,000 divinity crystals on each disciple, could they?
There were close to a hundred disciples here. If each of them had a bottle of Sword Enlightenment Liquid, it would be two million Divinity Gems. This was an astronomical figure!
"I'll take this bottle of Sword Comprehension Liquid. Are you okay with that?" Luo Zheng said.
The corner of Wang Shaoqi's mouth twitched. Even as the young master of the Wang family, this bottle of Sword Comprehension Liquid made his heart ache.
But he did not have the ability to refuse. Wang Ze had died here, but the Greater One Heavenly Palace did not pursue the matter, and Wang Shaoqi had not even climbed over the sword wall. He was insignificant to the Greater One Heavenly Palace.
"No, no objection …" Wang Shaoqi shook his head and said.
After giving the Sword Comprehension Liquid to Luo Zheng, Wang Shaoqi turned to leave with his people.
"Did I say you could leave?" Luo Zheng said lightly.
Wang Shaoqi had nowhere to vent his depression, but he had no choice but to be manipulated by Luo Zheng. Thinking that Luo Zheng was already a dead man, he comforted himself and turned his head to ask, "Is there anything else …"
"Everyone here has the same Sword Comprehension Liquid as you?" Luo Zheng asked again.
In the open square below the sword wall, there were close to 30,000 people gathered. Most of them exuded ordinary Qi of Truth. In Luo Zheng's opinion, the chances of these people passing the sword wall were very low. But it was impossible for everyone to have such pure Sword Comprehension Liquid, right? Ordinary people could not take out so many Divinity Gems.
"Of course not. The Sword Comprehension Liquid has its own purity. The diluted Sword Comprehension Liquid is not expensive," Wang Shaoqi said.
"Do you have diluted Sword Comprehension Liquid? Let me take a look, "Luo Zheng said again.
Wang Shaoqi wailed in his heart. This guy could not keep extorting him, right? After thinking for a while, he gritted his teeth and said, "I do. If I give it to you, will you let us go?"
"Sure," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
Wang Shaoqi winked at someone beside him. One of them came forward and handed his small jade bottle to Wang Shaoqi. Wang Shaoqi handed it to Luo Zheng and looked at him eagerly.
Luo Zheng waved his hand casually. Wang Shaoqi then ran away with his people as if he had been pardoned.
"The Qi of Truth in this small bottle is much weaker …" Luo Zheng opened the bottle and felt the Sword Comprehension Liquid exuding a faint Sword Intent of Eternity.
Lai Huabei and Yue Baicheng also gathered around.
"Brother Luo Zheng, according to our talents, this sword wall is nothing. But the Sword Comprehension Liquid is so expensive. I'm afraid it's a waste," Lai Huabei said.
Most of the disciples already had thoughts of giving up when faced with this sword wall. Seeing Luo Zheng worry for them, they were touched. But Luo Zheng had sacrificed too much.
But Luo Zheng never gave up on something he set his mind on.
He smiled and said, "Although I don't know the formula of this Sword Comprehension Liquid, I think it must be used to absorb the Sword Intent of Eternity through some kind of spirit liquid. I don't have this spirit liquid, but I do have this!"
After saying that, he put his hand on the mouth of the small jade bottle. A stream of Qi formed into a spiral. That was the Sword Intent of Eternity that Luo Zheng was brewing.
The Qi of Truth spiraled in his palm and went into the small jade bottle. Even the Sword Comprehension Liquid inside started to spin.
He didn't know what this spirit liquid was, but it actually merged with the Qi of Truth!
"It's similar to what I thought …" Luo Zheng felt the changes in the bottle and the corners of his mouth curled up.
The Sword Comprehension Liquid was really expensive because it had a perfect compatibility with the Qi of Truth. The spirit liquid itself was not difficult to concoct. After concocting this spirit liquid, one had to find geniuses to infuse their Qi of Truth into it and merge it with the spirit liquid.
Those who could comprehend the Dao with a single thought were all very valuable. The price to get them to do it was naturally huge. Naturally, the price would be expensive.
"This bottle is for you." Luo Zheng threw the small jade bottle into Lai Huabei's hand. At the same time, he said, "It should be similar to Wang Shaoqi's bottle."
"It's about the same as your bottle …" Lai Huabei was stunned and said excitedly, "But it's too, too expensive. I can't use it. Brother Luo Zheng, you should sell it. It's worth twenty thousand Divine Crystals!"

Chapter 2694
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Opening of the Sword Wall
"Take it. I don't lack divine crystals," Luo Zheng said, shaking his head.
He still had a huge sum of money on hand. Divine crystals were nothing to him.
However, after he handed the bottle of Sword Comprehension Liquid to Lai Huabei, the other disciples' gazes became heated.
They did not expect to pass the Sword Wall. However, with this Sword Comprehension Liquid, the possibility became infinitely high. After all, Wang Shaoqi's talent was not high.
Although Luo Zheng could perfect the ordinary Sword Comprehension Liquid, he did not have any extra liquid.
The disciples were all eager. Although they did not speak, their thoughts were written on their faces.
Luo Zheng looked at the crowd waiting not far away and had an idea.
He casually walked towards a group of people. The scene that Luo Zheng had just witnessed under the sword wall had left a deep impression on these people. When they saw Luo Zheng, there was naturally a trace of fear in their eyes.
"I want to make a deal with you," Luo Zheng said.
"What, what deal?" a middle-aged man asked.
Luo Zheng held onto Wang Shaoqi's bottle of Sword Comprehension Liquid, "This is a bottle of Sword Comprehension Liquid with a perfect True Meaning aura. If you want to exchange this for diluted Sword Comprehension Liquid, the more diluted the better."
As he spoke, he opened the jade bottle and let the aura rush out.
When the others saw the jade bottle, their eyes lit up!
Sword Comprehension Liquid was a very deceptive item. The diluted Sword Comprehension Liquid was only one percent of the original Sword Comprehension Liquid. The difference in price was like heaven and earth!
"How many bottles do you want to exchange?" the middle-aged man asked.
Luo Zheng thought for a while and said, "Name your price."
The middle-aged man thought for a while, then turned his head and said, "Give me your Sword Comprehension Liquid. We will give up on the Sword Wall for today!"
The 30 people behind him were obviously from the same faction. When they heard the middle-aged man's words, they did not have any objections. They all took out the jade bottles in their hands.
Luo Zheng noticed that the jade bottles were all the same. He guessed that all the Sword Comprehension Liquid was bought from the same place. He did not know which faction sold the Sword Comprehension Liquid.
If he had the chance, he could also make Sword Comprehension Liquid and sell it.
The 30 or so people gathered their jade bottles in the hands of the middle-aged man. The middle-aged man then placed the bottles in front of Luo Zheng and said, "These are all of our Sword Comprehension Liquid. There is not a single bottle left. How about we trade with you?"
The middle-aged man also knew that Luo Zheng was at a disadvantage in this deal, so he didn't have much confidence. He could only express his sincerity. He really couldn't take out more.
"Sure. that." Luo Zheng smiled.
The middle-aged man was overjoyed. After taking Luo Zheng's jade bottle, he left with his men without another word. There were chances to climb over the wall every day, but such a bargain was not every day. If he sold Luo Zheng's bottle of Sword Comprehension Liquid, he could earn at least 70% of the profits.

"Hu …"
The aura of True Intent spiraled and went straight into the small bottle.
Soon, a bottle of Sword Comprehension Liquid was "created" by Luo Zheng. With a smile on his face, he walked towards another group of people...
After three rounds, Luo Zheng had exchanged for more than 100 bottles of Sword Comprehension Liquid. The only thing left was to perfect the diluted Sword Comprehension Liquid.
The disciples gathered in a circle and looked at Luo Zheng. It was as if he was performing a magic trick.
"Brother Luo Zheng, using his Qi of True Intent to make money. I wonder how much he can make?"
"In a few days, he will become a millionaire!"
"This is using talent to make money. No one can take it away from him..
The disciples of the academy were all envious of him.
Yue Baicheng said, "Brother Luo Zheng can make Sword Comprehension Liquid for us to use. However, it will be difficult to sell it in large quantities."
"Why?" Qiu Yi asked.
"These small bottles come from the same workshop, which means that everyone comes to this workshop to buy and sell. I'm afraid that they have long monopolized the market. I'm guessing that Greater Yi Heavenly Palace is the one selling it. Otherwise, how could the Heavenly Palace tolerate such an unfair situation? If Brother Luo Zheng takes it out to sell, there will be problems. "Yue Baicheng analyzed.
Luo Zheng released his True Intent of Dao and nodded. "Yue Baicheng is right. I think so too."
He had already guessed this, but Yue Baicheng was more comprehensive than him. Luo Zheng agreed that it was Greater One Heavenly Palace that was selling the Sword Comprehension Liquid.
At least for now, Luo Zheng's impression of Greater One Heavenly Palace had been greatly reduced. They would definitely do such a thing.
"Yours."
"Yours."
"Yours..
. "Luo Zheng threw bottles of Sword Comprehension Liquid to the crowd.
The heavy jadeite bottles made every disciple overjoyed. One bottle was worth twenty thousand God crystals. They would never have such an expensive item in their lifetime.
After the Sword Comprehension Liquid was distributed, they began to ponder.
"How do we use this?"
"Rub it on the sword?"
"We should drink it directly..
. "Luo Zheng did not know how to use it, but since so many people here had Sword Comprehension Liquid, they should know how to use it.
Everyone waited patiently for an hour. At this time, the giant wall not far away suddenly trembled.
"Rumble..
. "As the giant wall trembled, the people waiting suddenly became excited.
"It has begun!"
"God bless me. I must pass this time!"
"This is the most important challenge in my life!"
There were people old and young, men and women under the sword wall. Some of them had been trapped on this side of the wall for countless years. Every time it happened, it was a great challenge for them.
As the sword wall continued to tremble, the swords that were originally attached to it fell off.
"Ding ding dang dang..
. "The swords collided with each other and fell. After falling to a certain distance, all the swords seemed to have a mind of their own. They flew towards the wall like a rain of swords.
In the blink of an eye, the swords that were attached to the wall disappeared completely.
At this time, a white-robed youth walked out of the sword wall and said in a low voice, "The sword wall has been opened. Those who are interested in joining the Heavenly Palace, hang your swords on the wall and you will be able to cross this sword wall."
Hearing the white-robed youth's words, the disciples were stunned. The rules were much simpler than they had imagined.
"It should not be difficult."
"Do not let your guard down. It is not that easy to leave a sword on the wall."

Chapter 2695
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
Banner Lord
When the other disciples saw this, they followed suit and drank their Sword Enlightenment Liquid in one gulp.
The Sword Comprehension Liquid in the hands of the disciples was much purer than the others. After drinking the liquid, the Sword Comprehension Liquid emitted a strong aura of True Meaning.
Most of the disciples had yet to fully comprehend the Sword Essence of Eternity. However, after drinking the Sword Enlightenment Liquid, they could feel a surge of Sword Essence rushing into their minds. They felt that their understanding of the Sword Essence of Eternity had reached a new level!
If the Sword Comprehension Liquid could be provided without limit, everyone would be able to enter the Sea of True Meaning in a short time.
"Let's go," Luo Zheng said calmly.
Then, some of the disciples followed Luo Zheng and went straight to the Sword Wall.
When Luo Zheng was a hundred feet away from the Sword Wall, a strong repulsive force rushed toward Luo Zheng.
Not only Luo Zheng, but everyone's footsteps also slowed down.
"Hmm?"
In the face of this repulsive force, Luo Zheng only needed to twist his body slightly to adapt to it. His speed immediately returned to normal.
Most people began to stagger. On the contrary, the disciples were in a much better situation because of the Sword Comprehension Liquid …
Tens of thousands of people approached the Sword Wall together. Ninety-nine percent of them slowed down. Only a few stood out, and Luo Zheng was one of them.
Other than Luo Zheng, there were two women walking behind Luo Zheng. On the right was a young man in plain clothes. They were as relaxed as Luo Zheng, as if they could not feel the pressure from the Sword Wall at all.
The human race is very powerful. Every day, tens of thousands of people try to enter the Sword Wall. There are always some treasures. I wonder if these people have taken the Sword Comprehension Liquid, Luo Zheng thought to himself.
For people like Luo Zheng, they could cross a distance of a hundred feet in a few breaths.
When they were ten feet away from the Sword Wall, the two women behind them could not bear the pressure and slowed down. On the other hand, the young man in plain clothes on the right was exactly the same as Luo Zheng. He maintained the same pace and looked very relaxed.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was sizing him up, the young man in plain clothes greeted him, "Hey …"
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. "What's the matter?"
"Did you drink the Sword Comprehension Liquid?" the young man in plain clothes asked.
The young man in plain clothes was curious when he saw that Luo Zheng was as relaxed as he was.
"No," Luo Zheng replied simply.
Hearing this answer, a look of surprise appeared on the young man's face. "It seems that your compatibility is very high."
Luo Zheng just smiled faintly.
"Perhaps you can also become a Banner Lord," the linen-clothed youth said.
"Banner Banner Banner Lord? What's that? "
Luo Zheng thought of the white-clothed youths inside the sword wall. They all addressed the old man as Banner Master.
The linen-clothed youth found it strange that Luo Zheng didn't even know this, so he said, "Those who climb over the Sword Wall don't have any authority. Everyone has to rely on a Banner Lord, so after crossing the Sword Wall, you either rely on a Banner Lord or become a Banner Lord yourself."
"Is that so?" Luo Zheng's eyes flickered. "How do you become a Banner Lord?"
"Here, hang your sword on the uppermost layer," the plain-clothed youth said.
The sword wall was very tall, but it was divided into six layers.
Luo Zheng had also noticed that the sword wall was densely covered with swords, but the higher he went, the fewer swords there were.
"At the top, right?" Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
Previously, Luo Zheng had only wanted to climb over the sword wall, but now that the young man in plain clothes had mentioned the "Banner Master", he had changed his mind.
Thinking of the guy named "Wang Xiao", Luo Zheng didn't plan to join any of the Banner Lords, so he could only make himself a Banner Lord.
After making up his mind, Luo Zheng had already arrived in front of the sword wall. He took a deep breath, and his body spread out like a giant roc. Then, he rapidly ascended upwards!
"This speed …" The plain-clothed youth was shocked at this scene.
The plain-clothed youth was originally very proud. He came from a large family in the northern part of the Center God Province, and his compatibility with the Eternal Sword Intent had reached 70%. He originally thought that Luo Zheng might be a little weaker than him, but seeing Luo Zheng's figure fly upwards, he couldn't help sucking in a breath of cold air.
"Xiu!"
The sword wall was a wall used by Greater One Heavenly Palace to screen the general public, and not to screen out geniuses.
Although the higher Luo Zheng flew, the greater the pressure from the sword wall, but this pressure itself was derived from the Eternal Sword Intent, so it didn't pose much pressure on Luo Zheng.
In the blink of an eye, he had reached the top of the sword wall.
"This speed …" The plain-clothed youth stared blankly.
At the top of the sword wall stood a row of white-clothed youths. Zhao Hao, who had previously retreated, was among them.
Seeing Luo Zheng fly to the top of the sword wall, his eyes revealed a faint killing intent.
"Looks like this kid is planning to become a Banner Lord?"
"Heh heh heh, a Banner Lord without a background, isn't he courting death? If he defected to another Banner Lord, it might be a little troublesome, but becoming a Banner Lord by himself is courting death! "
"… …"
Although Banner Lords enjoyed more power, they also had to pay more responsibilities.
Unless they had a great background, most people would obediently submit to another Banner Lord.
"Enough!"
Luo Zheng reached the top of the sword wall, and the long sword in his hand suddenly turned, slamming towards the sword wall.
"Clang!"
With a crisp sound, the long sword in his hand ruthlessly slammed into the sword wall.
This sword wall was like a giant magnet, firmly attracting the long sword to it.
Luo Zheng flipped over, standing firmly on top of the sword wall, and at the same time looking down.
The plain-clothed youth and the other two girls had also begun to climb, and it seemed that they weren't having an easy time.
As for the others, their performance was even worse. Most of them were already struggling when they were three hundred feet away from the sword wall. On the contrary, the disciples of the academy were performing better than the others because they had consumed the Sword Comprehension Liquid.
Qiu Yi and Yue Baicheng were already close to the sword wall, and Qiu Siyuan and the rest were following closely behind. Even Lai Huabei was only seven to eight feet away from the sword wall.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng let out a long sigh of relief. He estimated that all the disciples of the academy would pass.
He wondered what Qiu Hanyan's expression would be when she saw this.
Then, Luo Zheng turned his head and looked inside the sword wall. Behind the sword wall was a wide open space, and there were hundreds of people gathered in this open space. These people were all Nirvana Realm masters.
From the aura they emitted, they should be people who had just entered the Nirvana Realm.
In their hands, each of them held a metal flagpole. On the flagpole, there was a flag that was unfurled in the wind, and on the flag, there were different surnames.
"Yao!"
"Chen!"
"Mu!"
These people were the so-called Banner Lords?

Chapter 2696
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
The Banner Masters were depressed.
The other Banner Masters were also looking at Luo Zheng. Some of them had looks of interest in their eyes.
Luo Zheng was the first person to climb over the Sword Wall. He was definitely a delicious cake in the eyes of the Banner Masters. If they could recruit him and train him, he would definitely have a bright future.
"Whoosh!"
The dozen or so Banner Masters suddenly raised the flagpoles in their hands and headed straight for the Sword Wall.
"Newcomers to the Sword Wall need to choose a Banner Master to join me. I guarantee you a high position and wealth!"
"I am the Banner Master of Zhuotang. If you choose me, no one here will dare to touch you!"
"Under my banner …"
The Banner Masters all sent out invitations to Luo Zheng, afraid that they would be one step too slow and the other Banner Masters would beat them to it.
At this moment, Zhao Hao flew down from the Sword Wall and said calmly, "Banner Master Wang Xiao said that whoever dares to recruit this brat will become Banner Master Wang's enemy. He hopes that all the Banner Masters will reconsider!"
When the Banner Masters heard Zhao Hao's words, worry appeared on their faces.
There were strong and weak Banner Masters. Among the Banner Masters, Wang Xiao was considered one of the stronger ones. Moreover, Wang Xiao had an even stronger backer.
If they recruited Luo Zheng in exchange for Wang Xiao's enmity, some of the Banner Masters would have to reconsider.
Seeing the Banner Masters retreat, Zhao Hao looked at Luo Zheng with a smug smile. "I already said that if you offend Banner Master Wang, you will definitely regret it!"
However, as soon as he finished speaking, two other Banner Masters stepped forward.
One of them was wearing golden armor. He had a big mouth and a wide forehead. He was more than ten feet tall. He shouted in a rough voice, "Little guy, join me, Hao Lin. I am not afraid of Wang Xiao!"
The other Banner Master was a woman with a slim figure. She said calmly, "You can also join my banner."
There were so many Banner Masters. There were bound to be some who weren't afraid of Wang Xiao. Not only were these two Banner Masters not afraid of Wang Xiao, they were even his enemies. Naturally, they wouldn't take Zhao Hao's words to heart.
The other Banner Masters looked regretful. With these two people stepping forward, the other Banner Masters didn't need to fight.
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng cupped his hands toward the two Banner Lords. "Banner Lords, I do not intend to join anyone's banner."
Hao Lin was full of confidence. Originally, he thought that Luo Zheng would agree. After all, he would not accept just anyone. Since this little guy had won first place and had a special affinity with him, he naturally liked him.
Luo Zheng's answer surprised Hao Lin. "You don't plan to join the banner?" He thought for a moment, then asked, "You plan to be a banner leader?"
"That's exactly what I was thinking." Luo Zheng nodded.
This time, it wasn't just Hao Lin. Even the other Banner Masters' expressions turned strange.
Reaching the sixth level of the Sword Wall did indeed give one the qualifications to become a banner master, but most people would not do so.
Banner Masters indeed had greater authority and wouldn't be restricted by other Banner Masters. However, at the same time, they would face more trouble and even conflicts between other Banner Masters. Especially for people like Luo Zheng, who had a conflict with Wang Xiao, the first thing they should do was to seek protection under other Banner Masters.
"In the past, there were many Banner Masters like you who couldn't reach the Nirvana Realm, but it's rare these years. You have a conflict with Wang Xiao. Aren't you afraid of death if you become a Banner Master yourself?" the female Banner Master asked calmly.
Luo Zheng replied calmly, "I haven't reached the Nirvana Realm yet, but I'll be able to in a few days."
"You'll be able to reach the Nirvana Realm in a few days? How confident …" The female Banner Master smiled.
The other Banner Masters also had strange smiles on their faces.
No matter what era it was, the Sea of True Meaning was a challenge for True Gods. However, this young man spoke as if he could pass it as soon as he thought about it.
Those who could easily pass the Sea of True Meaning were probably only the geniuses at the top of the Heavenly Palace. If Luo Zheng really had such talent, he wouldn't be climbing over the Sword Wall here.
"Haha, then I can only wish you good luck!" Hao Lin laughed.
While they were talking, the plain-clothed young man who had been with Luo Zheng also climbed over the Sword Wall. He let out a long sigh.
Hanging the longsword on the fifth level had already taken him a tremendous amount of effort. After he flipped over the sword wall, he looked at Luo Zheng with a faint look of admiration on his face. This fellow had reached the sixth level of the sword wall without any difficulty at all. He even felt that even if the sword wall was three times higher, this fellow would still be able to flip it over.
After the plain-clothed young man had climbed over, the other Banner Masters turned their attention to him. However, the plain-clothed young man flew down and cupped his hands towards Hao Lin. "Uncle Hao Lin, I'm here. However, I didn't get first place …"
If it wasn't for Luo Zheng, the plain-clothed young man would have gotten first place. He had previously promised that he would climb over the Sword Wall with first place.
"Haha, don't blame him. Xiaochen has already performed well." Hao Lin waved his hand.
The other Banner Masters had originally wanted to invite the plain-clothed young man. However, the plain-clothed young man and Hao Lin were family, so they could only give up.
The plain-clothed young man glanced at Luo Zheng and advised, "Friend, joining my Uncle Hao Lin's banner is definitely the right choice. If you choose to be a Banner Master yourself, it will be too difficult. Furthermore, Banner Masters must have at least 50 subordinates. I'm afraid you won't be able to recruit that many people in such a short time."
"He has great ambitions and is not afraid of death. Then let him be," Hao Lin said calmly. Hao Lin was never willing to force people who didn't want to submit to him. However, if Luo Zheng encountered trouble, Hao Lin didn't mind stepping on him.
No one who could become a Banner Master was easy to deal with!
Not long after, more and more people climbed over the Sword Wall. After the plain-clothed young man, it was the pair of women.
After this pair of women climbed over the Sword Wall, they walked towards the beautiful woman who had tried to recruit Luo Zheng. Clearly, they were familiar with each other …
The next ones to climb over were Qiu Yi and Yue Baicheng!
Qiu Yi and Yue Baicheng's talents were extremely outstanding in the Dao Sword Palace, but here, they could only be considered above average. However, with the help of Luo Zheng's Sword Comprehension Liquid, their compatibility was greatly enhanced. Their speed was also much faster than the others.
The Banner Masters hadn't managed to recruit the first four people, so naturally, the fifth and sixth people weren't willing to miss this opportunity.
However, as they opened their mouths to invite them, Qiu Yi and Yue Baicheng looked towards Luo Zheng.
"I choose to be a Banner Master myself. Are you willing to join me?" Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
"What do you think?" Qiu Yi smirked and walked towards Luo Zheng with a smirk.
Yue Baicheng didn't consider it at all. This choice wasn't even a choice.
Without Luo Zheng, they wouldn't have been able to reach this step.
Although Qiu Yi had always been jealous of Luo Zheng, when he truly recognized the difference between him and Luo Zheng, this feeling disappeared without a trace. Moreover, he wasn't someone who forgot his roots. Naturally, he wasn't willing to choose another Banner Master to join.
The Banner Masters were extremely depressed. The fifth and sixth people had finally appeared, but they actually chose to join Luo Zheng!

Chapter 2697
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
Receiving a banner
Although Qiu Siyuan, Yue Bai Hao, and the others belonged to the Condensing Sword Academy, it did not mean that their talents were bad.
In fact, their talents were second only to Qiu Yi and Yue Bai Cheng. Apart from them, there were about ten other academy disciples from the Sword Burial Palace and the Demon Sword Palace. Their talents were close to Qiu Yi and Yue Bai Cheng's.
Seeing more than ten people climb over the sword wall, the Banner Masters' thoughts became active again. They were thinking that they could get a share of the loot this time, right?
As the Banner Masters surrounded them, Qiu Siyuan and the others did not even look at them. Instead, they all walked toward Luo Zheng.
Those Banner Masters were also dumbfounded.
"No way?"
"These guys are all following that kid?"
"That guy's background is not ordinary, right? Which great family does he come from? "
It was a coincidence that Qiu Yi and Yue Bai Cheng were following Luo Zheng. However, it was a different story if more than ten people at the top were following Luo Zheng.
Just as the Banner Masters were puzzled, another hundred people flew over the sword wall.
This time, the Banner Masters were even more depressed.
Among the hundred people, only sixteen or seventeen people did not walk toward Luo Zheng. The other eighty or so people continued to follow Luo Zheng. No matter how the Banner Masters tried to persuade them, the academy disciples did not even look at them.
Since the academy disciples were following Luo Zheng, they did not have any other thoughts!

The Supreme Sword Codices each had their own advantages. Almost all of them demonstrated the Way of the Sword to the extreme. The further in one went, the higher the price.

"After all, the remnant was painstakingly created by Donghuang. Because it's a remnant, the price is only 1.5 million Divinity Gems," the voice said.

But these supreme sword mantras were most likely not the pinnacle of the Sword Dao. The sword mantras that could guarantee the establishment of the Great Yi Heavenly Palace could not be measured using divine crystals. For example, the Taiyi Sword Mantra that Ling Shuang mentioned was the ultimate technique of the Taiyi Mountain Lord. It was impossible for the Taiyi Sword Mantra to appear in the True Meaning Sword Pavilion.
Luo Zheng opened the first page of the Soul-Melting Sword Codex. When he saw the first line of words, he was slightly stunned. It was not a sword formula, but a series of self-answering questions.
"What is truth?"
"It is a principle that applies throughout the ages and throughout the world!"
"Merging with the Dao is a dream?"
"All things in the world have their principles, converging together to obtain the beginning and the end!"
"…"
This was not a divine ability. Instead, it was more like Donghuang's personal understanding of the Dao Fusion.
"Heh heh heh, this guy was quite ambitious when he compiled the Soul-Melting Sword Codex. He really thought he could comprehend the Sword of Truth …" 9527, who had been silent all this time, took the opportunity to speak.
"It seems that the Dao Fusion was very popular back then," Luo Zheng said.
"Of course, if you could really merge with the Dao, you could comprehend all the truths in the unified energy, surpassing all the Ancient Gods of Chaos, and even surpassing all the past and time. That would be the real immortality. Who could refuse such a temptation?" 9527 laughed. "Unfortunately, they all gave up in the end. Now I guess you are the only one who hasn't given up …"
Luo Zheng shook his head and ignored 9527. Even if he was faced with failure in the end, he had no intention of giving up.
What's more, the Dao Fusion energy was not weak. Practicing the Soul-Melting Sword Codex could make full use of its strengths.
Continuing to read the Soul-Melting Sword Codex, Luo Zheng was gradually attracted by the content, and his eyes became brighter and brighter!
There were only three moves recorded in the Soul-Melting Sword Codex, namely "Three Burions Sword," "Six Burions Sword," and "Thousand Burions Sword." Reading like this, Luo Zheng was also very excited.
As expected of the creator of the Taiyi Heavenly Palace, his thinking was very clear. He divided the Dao Fusion energy into three stages, and the power of each stage was different. It almost brought the Dao Fusion energy to the extreme.
In contrast, Luo Zheng's "Sword of Dao Fusion" was like using gold as a stone. Every time he integrated a Divine Path, his power would increase a little, but it was only relying on the power of the Dao Fusion energy.
In addition to the Three Swords of the Soul-Melting Sword, the Sword of Truth was also mentioned at the end. But the Sword of Truth was just Donghuang's speculation, so the Soul-Melting Sword Codex could only be called a remnant.
"This Soul-Melting Sword Codex still needs to be comprehended calmly …"
Today, Luo Zheng just skimmed through it. No matter how talented he was, it was impossible for him to master it in a short time.
Unknowingly, an hour had passed. When he closed the Soul-Melting Sword Codex, a ray of starlight shot out from the Dragon City again. The starlight kept splitting, and finally turned into thousands of silver swords that fell down.
After putting away the Soul-Melting Sword Codex, Luo Zheng closed his eyes and silently recited the Eternal Sword Essence. His soul had already entered the Sea of Essence.
Just as Luo Zheng entered the Sea of Essence, he heard the voice of the Ear Mouse not far away. "I've already given you the divine crystals. Why are you still dragging your feet?"
"What's the hurry? Is ten million divine crystals a lot? "Ling Shuang replied.
It turned out that Ling Shuang had already entered the Sea of Essence. The Ear Mouse was afraid of being stood up, so he kept urging her.
"You're finally here! Squeak squeak! "The Ear Mouse saw Luo Zheng outside the whirlpool and became excited.
Luo Zheng glanced at the Ear Mouse, then said to Ling Shuang, "Wait for me. When the whirlpool opens, let the Ear Mouse go first."
He then left the Sea of Essence.
"Why did he run away again?!" The Ear Mouse was anxious again.
Ling Shuang ignored the Ear Mouse and just stood guard by the side of the whirlpool.
In order to open the whirlpool, Luo Zheng's physical body had to be used. However, his physical body was still stuck at the second line.
During the day, Luo Zheng had asked Yin Yuehuan about it. There were no secret cultivation rooms outside the Dragon City. If he wanted to find a place to hide, he would have to go underground. Many people did that.
After leaving the Sea of Essence, Luo Zheng reached out his hand and pressed lightly on the ground. A huge force spread downwards. With a slight vibration, he dug out a huge pit that was thirty meters deep.
After entering, Luo Zheng set up a restriction and hid his body inside.
The others were either concentrating on comprehending the True Intent or wandering in the Sea of Essence or the other shore. Even if they noticed Luo Zheng's actions, they wouldn't interfere.
After Luo Zheng released his flame, he once again landed on the red Sea of Essence.
Compared to his soul, it was easier to cross the sea with his physical body.
The third line and the fourth line had some resistance, but it didn't affect Luo Zheng much.
After swimming in the Sea of Essence for four hours, he could already see the whirlpool. He could even faintly see the figures of Ling Shuang and the Ear Mouse.
The Ear Mouse only knew that Luo Zheng could open the whirlpool. As for what method he used, Luo Zheng didn't want to reveal it to him. This time, he simply went into the whirlpool from the other side. His whole body was sucked into the whirlpool in the waves of the Sea of Essence.

Dark Domain
Luo Zheng was quickly sucked to the bottom of the sea by the waterspout.

…
In the depths of the endless Dark Domain, a tentacle slowly moved at the bottom of the swamp.

The most valuable thing in the Dark Domain was not the Soul Core, but the light.
Darkness eventually covered this endless swamp. Every time the candle light lit up, all kinds of huge bats, flying mosquitoes, and strange insects would quickly gather.
These were all creatures created by the soul, but they were much more powerful than the ordinary Sun Soul.
However, the candlelight was fatal to these soul creatures. Anyone who approached it would be ignited by the candlelight and burned to a crisp. Even so, these soul creatures continued to advance one after another.

A circle of pale white force field formed around the candle flame, blocking those annoying soul creatures outside.

It was a woman of the human race, but she had a pair of large butterfly wings on her back. In the darkness, the candlelight outlined her graceful figure.
"Lord Suo, why did you summon me?" The woman looked greedily at the candle light and asked softly.
"In one month, two candles have been extinguished," a strange voice came from the bottom of the swamp.
The woman thought for a moment, then asked curiously, "Is there a big storm in the Sea of Moon?"
"There shouldn't be," the strange voice replied.
"That is indeed strange …" The woman couldn't understand. When a Sun Soul entered the Sea of True Intent, it would be completely disintegrated. Who could extinguish the candle? She said again, "Do you need me to send someone to investigate?"
"No need. With so many candles arranged, there will always be some accidents. You … have to speed up your progress," the strange voice continued.
"We also want to speed up, but we don't have enough candles," the woman said helplessly.
"The palace has already rushed to make thousands of candles. I will have them sent over as soon as possible," the strange voice said again.
The woman's face was full of joy, "Yes, Lord Suo!" Then she said, "But Lord Suo … to seal the Sea of True Intent, I'm afraid you will need millions, or even tens of millions of candles. The current number is far from enough, and it can't even cause trouble for them. Those lower life forms have even learned to dye the whirlpools!"
She had also discovered not long ago that the color of most of the whirlpools in the Sea of True Intent had changed. After careful observation, she discovered that the whirlpools had been dyed by someone to prevent people from entering by mistake.
"Hahaha … these are all meaningless actions. It's not just the Dark Realm that placed the candles. You just need to do as I say," the voice said lightly.
"Understood!" The woman bowed.
Then the tentacle suddenly waved, and the candle that had just been lit flew towards the woman. The tentacle then slowly retracted into the depths of the swamp.
The woman impatiently rushed up and held the candle firmly in her hand, staring at the candle's sparks as if it was a treasure.
Light was an eternal luxury here.
As the force field disappeared, the surrounding soul creatures surrounded them again.
This woman was not as gentle as Lord Suo. Facing these disgusting insects that wanted to share the light, she had always killed them without mercy!

Chapter 2711

Sword Training Pagoda
Starting from the fourth section, the pressure of the Sea of True Intent continued to increase.

Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang still maintained their speed.

This time, they still encountered some trouble when they rushed to the end of the fourth section. The waves of the Sea of True Intent became very violent near the fourth section. Luo Zheng's True Intent boat almost flipped over, but he still gritted his teeth and rushed over.

Luo Zheng, who opened his eyes, emitted a stronger aura.
After two days in Dragon City, Luo Zheng had already crossed the third section and the fourth section. Yin Yuehuan was speechless when she saw Luo Zheng's speed of improvement.
Yue Baicheng and the others were full of admiration when they saw Luo Zheng's speed of improvement. After all, they were still wandering around the first section.

"You don't need me to accompany you?" Yin Yuehuan's beautiful eyes flashed faintly.
"No need. I'm familiar with the general rules of Dragon City. I just don't need to enter the Life and Death Pagoda. If I can't make it back in a few days, I'll have to trouble Banner Master Yin to take care of my people," Luo Zheng said.
There were no secret cultivation rooms outside Dragon City, not even a decent cave. If he focused on cultivating the Sword Fate Eternal True Intent, it wouldn't matter. The Sky Palace's arrangement was understandable. After all, most of the outsiders who joined Dragon City first comprehended the True Intent of Dao and crossed to the other side.
However, Luo Zheng had the Fusion God Sword Technique and the Nine Flash Diffraction Sword Technique. He needed to spend time and effort to master them. Naturally, he needed a suitable place to cultivate.
Yin Yuehuan naturally agreed. She only told Luo Zheng to be careful, and then she went straight to Dragon City.
On the other west side of Dragon City, there was a tall tower that looked like a gourd. It was painted dark red, and it was very eye-catching in the snow-white Dragon City.
When he came here with Yin Yuehuan yesterday, Luo Zheng asked about it. This was the Sword Refining Tower of Dragon City and the only place for cultivation in Dragon City.
Luo Zheng secretly noted it down. But at that time, Yin Yuehuan told Luo Zheng that it was best to come here after reaching the other side. As for the reason, she did not explain.
When Luo Zheng pushed the door open, he saw an old man with a mask on his face leaning on one side, seemingly resting with his eyes closed.
The masked old man didn't open his eyes. The moment Luo Zheng pushed the door open, he pointed outside and said, "You haven't even reached the Nirvana Realm. Get out!"
That voice actually contained a trace of unquestionable might, causing Luo Zheng to involuntarily stop in his tracks and frown slightly.
This old man cultivated the Sacred Words Spell, True Meaning of Annihilation, which was rare in Dragon City. Luo Zheng thought to himself. At the same time, he asked, "The Sword Refining Tower of Dragon City is open to the public. I've never heard that it requires the cultivation of the Nirvana Realm to enter."
The masked old man didn't expect Luo Zheng to refute. He opened his eyes lazily and looked at him with a smile. "If you can pay the divine crystals, you can go in. The premise is that you're not afraid of death. I'm doing this for your own good."
"Is this Sword Refining Tower very dangerous?" Luo Zheng asked back.
"For you, it is." The masked old man squinted at Luo Zheng impatiently. "Please go back. Come back when you reach the other side."
Since Luo Zheng was here, he didn't plan to go back. With a flip of his hand, a pile of divine crystals appeared in his hand. He said loudly, "Since the Sword Refining Tower is open to the public, there's no reason to keep people out."
Seeing those divine crystals, the masked old man looked at Luo Zheng in surprise. He shook his head helplessly and muttered, "There are always some little fellows who seek humiliation but aren't afraid of death … Come with me!"
He then brought Luo Zheng into the tower.
The interior of the Sword Refining Tower was very spacious. The rooms inside spiraled upward and were distributed among them. Sword platforms were erected at the door of each room. Above the platforms was a small jade plate.
"Just put the divine crystals on the jade plate. 100 divine crystals can only be opened for one day. However, the time flow in the room is 10: 1. When the time is up, the room will kick you out. If you really can't hold on, wait until the time is up. This room will automatically kick you out to save your life." After the masked old man finished speaking, he chuckled and left with his hands behind his back. In his opinion, Luo Zheng would most likely be kicked out.
As for what he would encounter in the Sword Refining Tower, he didn't say a word.
Luo Zheng stood at the door and observed for a while. He then took out 300 divine crystals and stuffed them into the jade plate one by one.
Luo Zheng had seen a similar jade plate on the Soaring Firmament Flying Boat. After the jade plate absorbed the divine crystals, it rotated slightly and the door of the room slowly opened.
After entering, Luo Zheng looked around and felt a little strange.
The walls were empty. Except for a jade cattail in the center, there was nothing else. This was an ordinary secret cultivation room. A place like this cost 100 divine crystals a day?
The Heavenly Palace was really black-hearted …
Luo Zheng muttered in his heart. He had already sat cross-legged on the jade cattail.
However, the moment he sat on the jade cattail, the jade cattail rotated and the originally empty secret cultivation room changed greatly!
He was already in a huge cave. The interior of the cave was densely packed with red swords. These swords were like weeds. They were densely packed and the tips of the swords were all pointed at him in the center. In front of him was a path to leave.
"These swords …" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows. He was already on guard.
Although these swords were densely packed on the walls of the cave, Luo Zheng felt as if countless people were pointing their swords at him and could stab at him at any time.
He turned slightly on the jade cattail and reached out with one hand.
"Whoosh!"
A sword at the top of the cave was already shooting toward him!
"Cha …"
The speed of the sword was so fast that it brushed past his arm, leaving behind a shallow mark of blood.
"What a powerful sword!"
To be able to cut through Luo Zheng's body, this sword had at least the strength of one Jun of God. It had already reached the full strength of a person who had just entered the Nirvana Realm. If it was another person's arm, it would have been cut off.
These swords could threaten his life!
Luo Zheng finally understood the words of the masked old man. He was right. The Sword Refining Tower was not suitable for people below the Nirvana Realm to enter.
If he couldn't deal with these swords, he could only sit obediently within the range of the jade cattail and wait for the time to come to kick him out.
"30 days … I have to walk out with my own strength …"

The Might of the Samiya Sword
As Luo Zheng began to comprehend the God-Fusion Sword Canon, the entire cave became abnormally quiet.

The swords on the cave walls exuded different sword intents, as if thousands of people were pointing them at Luo Zheng. The sword intents intertwined, calming Luo Zheng's mind.
"So these swords aren't just a test. Using intertwined sword intents can produce this kind of effect?" Luo Zheng thought to himself.
Luo Zheng didn't understand the principle behind it, but Luo Zheng could personally experience the effects.
As these sword intents intertwined, Luo Zheng quickly sank into the God-Fusion Sword Canon.
There were only three forms in the Fusion God Sword Canon, but these three forms were filled with many variations. The first form was also the simplest Triple Burying Sword. The "three" in it didn't represent the fusion of three kinds of divine daos. Instead, it meant that there would be three layers in the process of using fusion dao energy.
Luo Zheng's eyes shone brighter and brighter as he continued to study it.
No matter how you looked at it, the value of the God-Fusion Sword Canon far exceeded that of the supreme sword canon.
The only thing that affected the God-Fusion Sword Canon was its excessively high cultivation threshold. Fusing multiple Divine Daos was a time-consuming and exhausting process, which was why no one cared about the God-Fusion Sword Canon.
For others, this threshold was difficult to cross, but for Luo Zheng, he had crossed it long ago.
When he truly began to cultivate, there naturally wasn't much of a hindrance!

Three days …
Ten days …
Luo Zheng slowly stood up from the Jade Cattail Leaf. With a light wave of his right hand, a sword appeared in his hand.

Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly became sharp!
He suddenly raised his sword, and a thick sword light suddenly slashed out!
Before this, Luo Zheng's Dao Fusion Sword only relied on unstable Dao Fusion energy to unleash a powerful annihilation power. But the Triad Sword was different!
This sword gleam was actually divided into three sword gleams, which meant that there were three Dao-Fusing energies. However, one of them was the main one, while the other two were auxiliary ones!
The moment he slashed out, the three sword auras merged together smoothly. It looked no different from Luo Zheng's Sword of Dao Fusion.
However, the moment the sword-light collided with the longsword, the two auxiliary Dao-fusion energies suddenly exploded, and the originally dazzling sword-light suddenly glowed with a resplendent silver light!
Under the intense pressure of the annihilation energy, the innermost main sword light was activated!
Boom!
A dazzling white light reverberated throughout the entire cave.
The long sword that had been aimed at Luo Zheng had already disappeared without a trace …

The same energy was only released in a different way, but it produced a completely different power. How much power did the Triad Sword have?
More importantly, the Triad Sword consumed much less Dao-fusion energy than the Dao Fusion Sword!
Just as Luo Zheng was absent-minded, more longswords at the top began to loosen up. As these longswords loosened up, they flew toward Luo Zheng from all directions!
Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …

After all, these longswords only had one attack. Moreover, Luo Zheng could predict their trajectory, speed, and power. Naturally, the difficulty was incomparable.

Luo Zheng wanted to try, so he did not immediately return to the Jade Cattail.

Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …
Boom, boom, boom, boom …
Every time the Triad Sword split open, it would produce a dazzling white light. Since he could not see with his eyes, Luo Zheng could only use his divine sense to replace it.
After taking more than ten steps forward, he had already slashed more than ten times.
The dazzling white light dissipated. The longswords that were flying toward him had completely disappeared.
Luo Zheng pursed his lips and smiled. Just as he was about to continue forward, the longswords around the cave walls shook slightly. This time, there were hundreds of longswords that turned into a rain of swords and flew toward him.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng's expression changed slightly. He tapped the ground with the tip of his foot. His body swayed and dragged a series of afterimages back into the Jade Cattail.
Dong, dong, dong, dong …
The hundreds of longswords bombarded the surroundings of the Jade Cattail. They were blocked by an invisible force.
Luo Zheng's expression did not look good. "No wonder the masked old man said that he might die in there. Even those who had just entered the Nirvana Realm would have a hard time dealing with it, let alone a True God …"
Although he would be kicked out of this room after 30 days, he did not want to leave in such a pathetic way.
"Six Cloud Sword …"
The Six Cloud Sword was more powerful than the Triad Sword in terms of power. However, in terms of principle, there was not much difference.
According to the concept of the Fusion God Sword Codex, it was to chain the detonation of the fusion energy to increase its power exponentially. The Six Cloud Sword detonated five layers consecutively.
As for the "thousand" in the Thousand Cloud Sword, it was just an imaginary number. Even if it detonated seven or eight layers, it was also called the Thousand Cloud Sword. However, it was also recorded in the Fusion God Sword Codex that the highest number of layers that could be detonated could reach a thousand.
Luo Zheng could not imagine how powerful that was. However, since the Fusion God Sword Codex was called a remnant, even Donghuang himself might not be able to unleash that level of power.
The power of the Triad Sword was more than enough to deal with these longswords. However, there were too many longswords in this cave. Even if he managed to unleash the Six Cloud Sword, it would be difficult for him to leave …
In the end, Luo Zheng could only focus on the Nine Flash Spectroscopic Sword Codex.
When he chose the Nine Flash Spectroscopic Sword Codex instead of the Sword Pattern Technique, Luo Zheng had some considerations in his heart. The Sword Pattern Technique sounded very wonderful and could be used to kill in one strike. However, the requirements for cultivation were too high.
Moreover, in terms of pure power, Luo Zheng already had the fusion sword, and the Nine Flash Spectroscopic Sword Codex was the best choice in terms of power and speed.
After all, the most suitable one was the best …
In the next twenty days, Luo Zheng still sat in front of the jade cattail and calmly studied the Nine Flash Spectroscopic Sword Codex. To Luo Zheng, this sword codex was more difficult than the Fusion God Sword Codex. If he had any doubts, Luo Zheng could only transform into an inner clone and practice in his inner world.
Thirty days passed in the blink of an eye.
And outside the Sword Training Pagoda, three days had passed.
The masked old man glanced at the rooms of the Sword Training Pagoda and calculated that Luo Zheng would be kicked out soon. He hated those young people who did not listen to his advice and did not know the immensity of heaven and earth. When Luo Zheng was kicked out, he would definitely ridicule him.

Report
