He couldn't deal with the dense swords with just the Triple Burying Sword, but he probably couldn't withstand those swords with just the Nine Flash Diffraction Sword Codex …
. Anyway, he was he he..
Just as Luo Zheng opened the door, he saw a young man and the masked old man staring at him. Luo Zheng felt his hair stand on end. He asked, "What's wrong?"
"Wen, Elder Wen … Am I seeing things?" The young man asked.
Elder Wen also had a baffled look on his face, but the mask covered his face, so his expression couldn't be seen.
In Elder Wen's opinion, Luo Zheng being kicked out of the room was a sure thing. He was worried about this and was waiting to see Luo Zheng make a fool of himself. Seeing Luo Zheng walk out, he felt strange and did not know how to answer.
The young man did not understand the situation and continued to ask, "Elder Wen, this guy has not reached the Nirvana Realm. His Qi of Trueness is only at the fourth or fifth level. I-I …"
Not only the young man, but even Elder Wen had never seen such a strange thing.
Not only did he not reach the Nirvana Realm, but his cultivation was only at the middle level in the Sea of Trueness. How did he pass the Secret Cultivation Room of the Sword Training Pagoda?
He had been guarding the Sword Training Pagoda for so many years. There were tens of millions of people entering and leaving Dragon City. He had never seen such a thing.
"Did you … cheat?" Elder Wen asked Luo Zheng in a low voice.
"Cheat?" Luo Zheng was stunned. "How?"
The cave was surrounded by sword thickets and there was only one way out. Where could he have cheated?
Elder Wen also felt that his question was wrong. It was impossible to cheat in the design of the Sword Training Pagoda. He asked again, "How did you … open the door?"
"I opened it directly." Luo Zheng asked, "What's wrong? Is it against the rules? "
"Haha …" Elder Wen laughed awkwardly.
The young man covered his head. It was obvious that he was hit hard.
Among his peers, he had always regarded Lin Huaian as his goal. Now, looking at him again, this guy in front of him was almost like a monster. He could open the door at the Nirvana Realm. Those geniuses on the mountain could not do it, right?
"What's your name? Where are you from? Please tell me one by one! "Elder Wen asked seriously.
Luo Zheng was a little vigilant. "What's the use of telling me these?"
"Use …" Elder Wen glanced at Luo Zheng. It seemed that this guy did not know much about the rules of Dragon City. "Of course it's very useful. For example, I can send you to the place of your dreams."
Seeing Elder Wen so serious, Luo Zheng only smiled, but still reported his origin and name to him.
This old man most likely wanted to send him to the Sky Palace. But in Luo Zheng's view, Elder Wen's action could only be in vain. If the Greater One Sky Palace wanted to accept him, they would have already acted. It was impossible for them to remain silent until now.
But it was not bad to stay in Dragon City. With sufficient divine crystals, he could calmly cross the sea of True Meaning. He could also strengthen his shortcomings, so he was not that interested in the upper levels of the Sky Palace.
Elder Wen was not clear about this. After Luo Zheng left, he also left the Sword Training Pagoda and ascended along the Greater One Peak.
People like Lin Huaian had barely reached the standard to be sent to the Sky Palace, but they still needed to be inspected for a period of time. But Luo Zheng's performance made it impossible for him to stay in Dragon City. He naturally would not delay for a moment.
When he was halfway up the mountain, the surrounding scenery had become illusory and blurred.
In the seven-coloured fog, there was a spotless palace. Elder Wen entered the palace, went straight to the lobby, and said to the guard on duty, "Wen Si requests an audience with Lord Gu..."
Just then, a voice sounded, "No need to request an audience, Lord Gu will not agree."
Wen Si turned around and saw Qiu Yinhe. He was slightly startled, and hurriedly cupped his hands in greeting. This was the Heavenly Festival! He was an expert in the Sky Palace who could guard an area!
"Lord Qiu... why..." Wen Si asked, puzzled.
Qiu Yinhe smiled indifferently, "You want to report a young man called Luo Zheng, right?"
"Yes, this person passed the Sword Training Pagoda! Although it's only the first floor, but... "
Before Wen Si could finish, Qiu Yinhe interrupted him, "But he hasn't crossed the other shore, right?"
"How does Lord Qiu know..." Wen Si was puzzled.
"This person is a disciple of the Dao Sword Palace I established. He can be considered as someone I introduced to the Sky Palace," Qiu Yinhe said with an unnatural expression.

Chapter 2714
Buy a Sword
Although the Great Yi Mountain Sect and Greater One Mountain Sect were on the same side, there was still competition between them.
This time, the Great Yi Mountain Sect swore that Xiao Shuang had known Luo Zheng for a long time, not to mention that Luo Zheng was saved by Lin Zhanting.
However, Qiu Yinhe still had seventy percent of reason. Luo Zheng was a disciple of the Dao Sword Palace, and the Heavenly Palace had sent people to pick him up, but they had failed due to an accident.
Qiu Yinhe could owe Lin Zhanting a favor for saving Luo Zheng, but Luo Zheng was undoubtedly a member of the Heavenly Palace.
Under Qiu Yinhe's insistence, the Great Yi Mountain Sect finally chose to give in.
"Since Lord Qiu brought him into the Heavenly Palace, why did you let him stay in the Dragon City?" Wen Si was puzzled. This kind of person should have been sent up the mountain long ago.
Qiu Yinhe stroked his beard and said, "I have my own arrangements. If he wants to borrow your Sword Training Pagoda, please take care of him."
Wen Si naturally agreed to Tian Jiedu's request.
… …
In Dragon City, the Banner Masters walked briskly. The flow of people was like shuttling shuttles.
If they wanted to be promoted to Alliance Master faster, they had to expand the number of people under their banner or accept all kinds of missions. Other than that, they also needed a large amount of time to cultivate. Therefore, most of the Banner Masters were very busy.
The most difficult part of the process after leaving the secret cultivation room was not Luo Zheng's problem, but the problem of his weapon.
He had changed three swords in total.
These three longswords were top-grade grandmist artifacts in the God Domain, and one of them even had the ability to repair itself. Even so, it couldn't withstand the force of Huang Xiaolong's attack and shattered into pieces.
With the toughness of the Tengshe Sword, there was no problem, but once he used it, there was a risk of it being exposed.
Before he could confirm the Great Yi Heavenly Palace's attitude towards the Li Tribe, he could not use it. Therefore, he needed a suitable sword.
Fortunately, he had a bountiful harvest this time. The divine crystals in his hand were enough for him to squander for a while.
After walking for a while, he finally stopped in front of a pavilion.
"The World's Famous Sword Tower!"
Seeing the signboard of the pavilion, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. Perhaps only Greater One Heavenly Palace dared to give such a name.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to enter the attic, he frowned slightly and stepped back. He stared at the young man not far away and asked, "Why are you following me all the way?"
This young man was the one who had been kicked out of the Sword Training Pagoda. Seeing that Luo Zheng had discovered him, the young man smiled awkwardly and said, "I … just wanted to ask if you have any unique secret manuals."
Luo Zheng thought for a moment and said, "Are you referring to the room outside the Sword Training Pagoda?"
"That's right!" The young man's eyes lit up.
"Yes," Luo Zheng answered.
The smile on the young man's face widened. "Is there really such a technique? I, I can pay … "He was also embarrassingly short of money. It was not easy for him to enter the Sword Training Pagoda, but he still gritted his teeth and said," I will pay 100 divine crystals. "
"You don't need to pay with spirit crystals." Luo Zheng smiled. "You are better than me in terms of technique. I guarantee that you will be able to leave the Sword Training Pagoda."
"What, what? Do you mean that I am weaker than you now? "The young man widened his eyes.
Although he had just stepped into the Nirvana Realm, the Nirvana Realm Token he had obtained was not weak.
The difference in cultivation was obvious. Even if Luo Zheng had left the secret cultivation room with his own strength, he did not believe that Luo Zheng was better than him. He always thought that Luo Zheng had used some special method to pass through the cave.
"Yes, that's what I mean." Luo Zheng answered coldly. He turned around and stepped into the World's Famous Sword Tower. He thought that he could get rid of this young man, but the young man actually followed him into the tower.
"Hey, hey …" The young man shouted a few words from behind. Only then did he realize that he had followed into the Sword Training Pagoda. He immediately forgot about his previous question and asked, "Do you want to buy a sword?"
"Yes," Luo Zheng answered.
"I can be your reference! My name is Su Kuan! Have you heard of the Family Su of the Center God Province? The famous Weapon Refining Aristocratic Family! Most of the swords in the Heavenly Palace are provided by the Family Su … "Su Kuan followed behind Luo Zheng and said.
No matter what Su Kuan said, Luo Zheng ignored him. His eyes swept over the sword shelves on the wall.
The long swords were placed on the shelves, head to tail. Something was smeared on the long swords, giving off a faint fragrance.
"Although all the disciples of the School have a Qingfeng Sword, I should also prepare one for them. Not only that, but those who join us in the future should also receive the same treatment." Luo Zheng thought to himself.
"This sword …" Luo Zheng pointed at one of the swords. Just as he was about to ask the waiter in the Sword Pavilion, Su Kuan beside him said, "Spring Forged Sword, made with the spring water of the Nirvana Realm Token 'Crane Spring'. The price of our Family Su is 300 Divine Gems. In Dragon City, it's 800 Divine Gems …"
The waiter beside him had a bad look on his face. However, this young man was so professional that he didn't dare to say anything more.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. His eyes swept over the other long sword. Before he could ask, Su Kuan said, "Wind Chant Sword, this is a fast sword …"
After Su Kuan introduced more than a dozen long swords, Luo Zheng suddenly stretched out his hand and said, "This one, this one, and this one …"
The waiter and Su Kuan also widened their eyes. Su Kuan even asked, "Don't tell me you want all of them?"
Four long swords cost about 3,000 Divine Gems, which was enough to go to the Sword Training Tower several times.
"Forty of them," Luo Zheng said.
Although there was no lack of rich people in the Sword Pavilion, it was rare to see someone as generous as Luo Zheng. So many long swords were definitely not for his own use. Besides, Luo Zheng hadn't even been to the Nirvana Realm!
Seeing the waiter's doubtful expression, Luo Zheng directly took out the Divine Gems. 120,000 shiny Divine Gems piled up together, exuding pure energy that was dazzling.
The waiter, who was originally tense, also burst into laughter at this moment. Su Kuan's eyes were wide open.
As a member of a refining family, Su Kuan had seen a lot of Divine Gems before. However, he had gone against his Family Su's wishes to enter Dragon City this time, so he was now very poor.
Seeing Luo Zheng buy more than 100 long swords in one breath, Su Kuan was naturally shocked.
"Do you have any long swords of a higher grade?" Luo Zheng asked the waiter.
When the waiter heard that Luo Zheng still wanted to buy weapons, how could he refuse? He hurriedly said, "Please follow me!"
Therefore, under the guidance of the waiter, Luo Zheng walked forward and entered the cabinet. Obviously, Su Kuan wanted to rely on Luo Zheng, so he actually followed him in.
However, as soon as Su Kuan entered, he saw a person in the cabinet and wanted to turn around and run away.
However, a person in the cabinet blocked Su Kuan's way and said angrily, "You didn't listen to your father. How long do you want to stay in Dragon City?"
"Sixth Uncle! Please spare me! I won't leave Dragon City even if I die! "Su Kuan begged for mercy.
The Family Su was established in the Center God Province and had a very good relationship with the Heavenly Palace. Naturally, there was someone serving in Dragon City, and this person was Su Kuan's sixth uncle, Su Qiao.

Chapter 2715
Third Rank Nine
The attendant knew that Luo Zheng was bold and unrestrained. He wanted to remind Su Qiao but stopped himself.
"Sixth Uncle, Sixth Uncle, this is a nouveau riche! Let's talk business first, okay? "Su Kuan, who was grabbed by Su Qiao, wanted to cry but had no tears.
There were not many customers in the Sword Tower's cabinet. Those who were willing to enter were all important guests.
However, Luo Zheng's cultivation was so low that Su Qiao did not take him seriously. But after hearing Su Kuan's words, he temporarily let go of his hand. He stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "The swords in the cabinet are all forged with Family Su's bloodline. If you don't have a million Divine Crystals, we can forget about this business."
The Sword Tower's cabinet did not belong to Dragon City. It was completely controlled by the Family Su. Therefore, they directly transacted with the Family Su here.
"Swords forged with bloodline?" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
A trace of pride appeared on Su Kuan's face. "Yes, my Family Su has the bloodline of the Fierce Gale Shadow Clan. This bloodline is most suitable for forging swords!"
The Fierce Gale Shadow Clan was one of the ancient beast clans. Their skin was like brass and their eyes were like mercury. They grew up in mines and their bodies were full of treasures. Because of this, the Fierce Gale Shadow Clan was completely slaughtered a long time ago.
In the end, only their bloodline was passed down and was inherited by the Family Su.
If it were not for the protection of Taiyi Heavenly Palace, their Family Su would have been slaughtered by the foreign races. After all, their bloodline was extremely precious. Because of this, the Family Su had always served the Heavenly Palace without any complaints.
"May I take a look?" Luo Zheng asked.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was so confident, Su Qiao walked to the side of the cabinet and waved his hand lightly.
That wall immediately caved in and six longswords appeared on the wall. When these longswords appeared, traces of sharpness immediately spread out.
After feeling the sharpness, Luo Zheng's eyebrows raised slightly.
"These six longswords can all be called Arcane Venerable Grade Dao Treasures! Even the cheapest Bird Seizing Sword costs a million Divine Crystals, "Su Qiao said clearly.
He was very satisfied when he saw Luo Zheng's serious expression.
However, what Luo Zheng saw was not the other five swords, but the sword hanging on the leftmost side. The back of the sword was slightly slanted, and there was a faint snake-shaped bow. Its appearance was exactly the same as Tengshe's sword!
"I seem to have seen this sword before. It looks exactly the same as the legendary Sword of Tengshe?" Luo Zheng asked.
Su Kuan chuckled and said, "This is a replica! It was an imitation of the Tengshe Sword. In terms of power, it was naturally not as powerful as the legendary Tengshe Sword! However, you are quite knowledgeable to even recognize Tengshe's sword. Hehe, an average person would not be able to do that! "
How could Su Kuan know that the Sword of Tengshe was in Luo Zheng's hands?
"Then is this sword also a Mystique Venerable One Dao Treasure?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Of course! Longswords that have been tainted with our Family Su's blood can all be called Mystique Venerable One Dao Treasures. Although this replica is only a third-grade Mystique Venerable One Dao Treasure, its power is still quite impressive, "Su Kuan said.
"I'll buy it." Luo Zheng did not hesitate much.
The appearance of this sword alone was enough to pass it off as the real thing.
If he had no choice but to use the Sword of Tengshe at some point, he could still use this sword to conceal himself. This longsword was naturally very suitable.
"You're not even asking about the price?" Su Kuan pursed his lips. He knew that Luo Zheng was quite a tycoon, but who would buy a sword like this?
"This sword is priced at one million and five hundred thousand Divine Crystals." Su Qiao saw how generous Luo Zheng was and smiled.
Luo Zheng paid the Divine Crystals and looked at the longswords again. He said, "These longswords can be called Mystique Venerable One Dao Treasures, but they are not the real Sword of Tengshe. What are they called?"
"The real Sword of Tengshe …" Su Qiao and Su Kuan were also stunned.
They did not know that Luo Zheng did not even know about such common knowledge. Su Kuan smiled and said, "After the Middle Ages, the treasures in the world were divided into three grades and nine grades! Mystique Venerable One Dao Treasures, Nirvana Dao Treasures, and Connate Dao Treasures are divided into three grades. They are divided into first, second, and third grades! "
Su Kuan paused for a moment before saying, "The Sword of Tengshe is a third-grade Connate Dao Treasure!"
"Only third-grade?" Luo Zheng said.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was slightly disappointed, the Family Su uncle and nephew could not help but laugh. Su Kuan even said, "Only third-grade? Hahaha … To forge a Connate Dao Treasure, it must be forged with Connate items from before the chaos was created! There are only a few dozen Connate Dao Treasures like this in the entire Mother World! It's only third-grade because there are fewer Connate items in this sword. Even if it's only third-grade, it's enough to make all races restless! "
"So that's how it is …" Luo Zheng nodded.
As Su Kuan said, a Mystique Dao Treasure was a Heavenly Weapon that was refined with blood, while a Nirvana Dao Treasure contained a Nirvana token. As for the Connate Dao Treasure, it was forged with Connate items from before the chaos. Such a Heavenly Weapon was rare and could only be found by chance.
After having a general understanding, Luo Zheng placed his gaze on the top of the wall.
When Su Qiao presented the six swords, he noticed two of them. One was a replica of the Sword of Tengshe, and the other was this sword.
The appearance of this long sword did not look special, but the bloody light that was hidden in the sword made people shudder. The sharpness that was faintly revealed was as if it wanted to devour people at any time.
"How much is this sword?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Seeing that Luo Zheng mentioned this sword, Su Kuan's face was faintly sorrowful.
From the bloody aura, Su Kuan knew who made this sword. They had squeezed her dry again. Thinking of her miserable appearance, Su Kuan felt an inexplicable heartache.
Su Qiao's reaction was completely different. He raised a finger and said, "Good eye. This sword is called Snow! It's a first-class Mystique Venerable Dao Treasure! It's also the best sword in the Famed Sword Tower! Although this sword does not have a Nirvana token, it's comparable to some Nirvana Dao Treasures. It's priced at eight million Divine Crystals! "
Hearing this price, Luo Zheng was slightly speechless.
However, he had an affinity with this sword. Moreover, the replica of the Sword of Tengshe was not enough to fight the enemy … However, Luo Zheng still wanted to buy that piece of yellow paper to study it with the ten million Divine Crystals that he had reserved.
If he bought this sword, he would have to find another way to earn Divine Crystals.
Gritting his teeth, Luo Zheng said, "I'll buy this sword too."
Su Qiao's eyes widened slightly. He did not think that Luo Zheng would really buy it.
In this cabinet, there were less than three to five treasured swords traded in a year. Luo Zheng bought two swords at one go, and it was a transaction of ten million Divine Crystals. Naturally, he was extremely happy!
"That's great, that's great!" Su Qiao excitedly took down the Snow Sword.
Luo Zheng also took out Divine Crystals. They exchanged the money and the goods.
Just as Luo Zheng received the sword, Su Kuan said, "Can you let me take a look at this sword?"
"Su Kuan! This sword has been sold to a customer. It's not a Family Su's item anymore! Don't make trouble! "Su Qiao scolded.
Seeing the sorrowful look in Su Kuan's eyes, Luo Zheng was slightly moved. Only then did he pass the sword to him.

Chapter 2716
Mass Manufacturing
Seeing Su Kuan's expression, this Has Snow probably had some history.
However, this was Family Su's business, so Luo Zheng naturally didn't care.
He put away the sword. However, after Luo Zheng left the Famous Sword Tower, Su Kuan unexpectedly followed him.
"How long are you going to keep following me?" Luo Zheng frowned.
Su Kuan had already put away the grief in his heart. He suddenly said, "I've decided!"
"Decided what?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Join your banner!" Su Kuan said seriously.
"Join my banner?" Luo Zheng was slightly startled. "Aren't you also a Banner Lord?"
Su Kuan said, "Just give up the banner. I originally followed Banner Lord Chen. Now that he's been promoted to Alliance Lord, I naturally became a Banner Lord as well. However, Alliance Lord Chen is about to participate in the Tai Yiwei exam, so I can be considered a free person!"
According to Dragon City's rules, Banner Lords could indeed give up their banner, but this was very rare.
Especially since Su Kuan's cultivation was one level higher than Luo Zheng's!
"But if you give up your banner, won't you be unable to enter Dragon City?" Although Luo Zheng wanted to strengthen his banner, he still had some misgivings.
"Don't worry about this. I have my own ways of entering and leaving Dragon City," Su Kuan said with a smile.
"One last question. Why do you want to follow me?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Su Kuan's face revealed a crafty expression. He laughed mischievously and said, "I'm just following a local tyrant."
Luo Zheng didn't refuse Su Kuan's request. Since his Family Su was a family of blacksmiths, this person would be of great use to him. However, he faintly felt that Su Kuan joining his banner wasn't as simple as he said. It was very likely related to the sword he had bought.
After leaving Dragon City, Su Kuan really did as he said. He gave up his banner and registered it with the old man.
Afterwards, the two of them once again went to the market outside Dragon City.
This market wasn't small. This time, there was plenty of time, so Luo Zheng also strolled around.
Although this place couldn't compare to Dragon City, there were still some rare and strange things that were sold at very low prices. There were even hermit divine abilities that were sealed with talisman arrays for sale.
After leaving the market, Luo Zheng found a disciple of the school.
Yue Baicheng and the rest were overjoyed as they surrounded him. Luo Zheng introduced Su Kuan to them.
When they heard that a Nirvana Realm powerhouse had joined Luo Zheng's faction, the disciples of the academy were also rather shocked. However, the more powerful Luo Zheng's faction became, the better it would be for everyone.
At this time, Yue Baicheng lightly flipped his hand and took out a small bottle. He said to Luo Zheng with a smile, "Banner Lord, look at this."
Before Luo Zheng could examine it carefully, Su Kuan glanced at it from the side and immediately said, "This is Heaven Pure Liquid, the purest kind of spiritual liquid, but it is not rare. When refining magic weapons, it is used to lower the temperature. Because it is pure in nature, it does not bring any impurities. Some people also use it to make wine..."
"But this seems to be somewhat similar to Sword Comprehension Liquid," Luo Zheng said as he stared at the spiritual liquid. It was just that this Heaven Pure Liquid did not have the aura of true intent.
"Because Sword Comprehension Liquid is made from Heaven Pure Liquid. However, although Heaven Pure Liquid is cheap, there are too few people who can make it." Su Kuan shrugged his shoulders.
Yue Baicheng ignored Su Kuan. As if he had discovered a new continent, he said, "Banner Lord, this Heaven Pure Liquid is very cheap. You can buy such a big bottle for ten divine crystals!"
"And then? Buy it to make wine? "Su Kuan stretched his arms and said casually.
"Banner Lord, if you use it to make Sword Comprehension Liquid, the profit from the price difference is probably very rich," Yue Baicheng glanced at Su Kuan and continued.
"Cough cough cough..."
Su Kuan choked. He even suspected that he had misheard.
The reason why Sword Comprehension Liquid was so rare was because there were too few people who could make it!
In the entire Great Yi Heavenly Palace, only fourteen people could make it. Those were all people who comprehended dao with a single thought!
Among the fourteen people, some had fallen and some had high positions. The majority of them did not care about the income of Sword Comprehension Liquid. Therefore, only one or two people could make it.
Su Kuan was shocked that someone under Luo Zheng's banner wanted to create Sword Comprehension Spirit Fluid.
But Luo Zheng's answer made Su Kuan feel even more incredulous.
"If we can really open up a market for Sword Comprehension Liquid, we can indeed earn a lot of divine crystals," Luo Zheng took the bottle from Yue Baicheng's hands.
The price of the Heaven Pure Liquid was so low, and it wasn't difficult for Luo Zheng to convert it into Sword Comprehension Spirit Liquid by relying on the Eternal Sword Intent. Other than using the Soaring Serpent Sword to activate the Imperial Sword Seal, no other methods would be able to empty out his Eternal Sword Intent.
Su Kuan opened his mouth wide, saying, "No way, you can really make Sword Comprehension Liquid?"
Luo Zheng glanced at Su Kuan indifferently, reached out and covered the mouth of the bottle. As the true qi spiraled into the bottle, the Heaven Pure Liquid in the bottle also began to slowly rotate.
"The true qi vortex naturally fused together..." Su Kuan was dumbfounded.
The reason why Heaven Pure Liquid had so many uses was because of its purity. It almost did not react with any energy, and it was very difficult to change its characteristics.
Only a Dao intent with perfect compatibility could merge with the Heaven Pure Liquid!
If there was any flaw in the dao intent, the Heaven Pure Liquid would become turbid and could not be made into Sword Comprehension Liquid.
In other words...
"Brother Luo Zheng! You can comprehend dao with a single thought? "Su Kuan was suddenly enlightened.
No wonder this guy could pass the first floor of the Sword Practice Pagoda with such a cultivation level. No wonder he said he was not strong enough. Monsters that could comprehend dao with a single thought were almost all abnormal existences!
Luo Zheng handed the finished spirit liquid to Su Kuan, "Take a look and see if this is considered Sword Comprehension Liquid?"
Su Kuan felt the true qi in the bottle, and the complex expression on his face was hard to describe. After a while, he exclaimed, "I see, you can be so rich without any background. But a guy like you actually stayed in Dragon City!"
Luo Zheng shrugged, "I also find it strange."
...
Because of Luo Zheng's crazy buying, the 15 million divine crystals he got from Ear Mouse had almost been used up.
If he did not use the Chi You Race's inheritance, Luo Zheng would indeed be in a tight spot.
But Yue Baicheng's discovery solved his urgent need.
He had bought two hundred small bottles of Heaven Pure Liquid from the market. A bottle of pure Sword Comprehension Liquid was worth twenty thousand divine gems. Even if he sold it for fifteen thousand or even ten thousand divine gems, he could still earn two to three million divine gems. The cost was negligible.
That evening, Luo Zheng had finished making the Sword Comprehension Liquid.
Naturally, Luo Zheng did not need to personally sell the Sword Comprehension Liquid. He entrusted Yue Baicheng and the others to do the transaction in the market and use the market to circulate outside the sword wall.
Looking at the bottles of Sword Comprehension Liquid, Su Kuan also saw piles of divine crystals. He could not help but secretly click his tongue. His Family Su worked so hard and relied on blood to refine a divine weapon. It was not as much as what Luo Zheng earned in one night.
This was indeed very helpless. Not to mention a Family Su, even among the large families in the Center God Province, it was hard to produce a person with perfect compatibility.

Chapter 2717
Translator: Nyoi-Bo Studio Editor: Nyoi-Bo Studio
Luo Nian's Conflict
After crossing the fourth segment line, the difficulty of the Sea of True Intent increased greatly.
Crossing the Sea of True Intent was a process of tempering one's Yang Soul. The longer one stayed in the Sea of True Intent, the stronger their Yang Soul would become.
During this process, Dragon City's advantage was revealed.
Those silver swords pierced Luo Zheng's flagpole and continuously nourished everyone's souls. It was equivalent to continuously swallowing soul elixirs. Of course, the effect was much weaker.
After staying in the fifth segment line for more than ten days, his Yang Soul became stronger under the tempering of the cold intent. He and Ling Shuang once again set out to cross the sixth segment line.
The whole process was daunting but not dangerous.
After crossing the sixth segment line, Luo Zheng discovered that there were indeed many whirlpools in the Sea of True Intent.
Almost every time they advanced a certain distance, they would discover three to five whirlpools.
One could imagine how many people were trapped in these whirlpools and still unable to escape.
When they passed by the whirlpools, they could sometimes see people crying for help.
However, Luo Zheng was not a savior. Other than helping Lin Zhanting save his son, he did not plan to take the fourth candle. If the person who set the candle found out, his fate could be imagined.
After entering the sixth segment line, Luo Zheng stopped advancing and used his physical body to cross the Sea of True Intent.
This blazing red seawater also had a certain body-tempering effect, but it did not have much effect on Luo Zheng. As he swam through the water, the seawater began to boil like lava.
During this process, Luo Zheng could clearly feel the True Intent of Dao in his physical body. At the same time, he wondered if there was any True Intent Divine Ability that could unleash this True Intent of Dao …
However, Luo Zheng was the only one in the Mother World. It was probably impossible to find a related True Intent Divine Ability. Perhaps he could use it in the future after he figured it out.
When both his soul and physical body entered the sixth segment line, he began to prepare to open the giant whirlpool.
According to Ling Shuang, the Taiyi Mountain Family was also looking forward to it. Her cousin, in particular, was anxiously waiting every day.
Especially after Ear Mouse had delivered the fifteen million Divine Gems, the Lin family was completely convinced of Luo Zheng's ability. Ming Xuan was also brimming with energy. After all, Luo Zheng had given him the greatest hope.
The Sword Comprehension Liquid was handed over to Yue Baicheng, Qiu Siyuan, and the others. The initial price was only fifteen thousand divine crystals.
Originally, Luo Zheng thought that this price was a bit high, but Yue Baicheng and the others thought that since the other people were selling Sword Comprehension Spirit Liquid for twenty thousand Divine Crystals, the difference of five thousand Divine Crystals was enough.
Just as Yue Baicheng thought, 120 bottles were sold in the market on the first day of its release, and the remaining 80 bottles were preordered by some people.
After all, the Sword Comprehension Liquid was expensive, and it was difficult for them to buy it on the same day. They could only raise money overnight.
After selling a large amount of divine crystals, Yue Baicheng entrusted Lai Huabei to secretly purchase the Heaven Pure Liquid.
This was where Yue Baicheng was smart.
They weren't the only ones producing Sword Comprehension Spirit Liquid. Their actions were undoubtedly stealing other people's business and disrupting the market.
Therefore, Yue Baicheng didn't want to make a big fuss about producing Sword Comprehension Spirit Liquid for the time being. Perhaps it would be discovered by the other party in the end, but before that, it should be able to help Luo Zheng earn a considerable amount of Divine Crystals.
For more than ten days, there was a considerable amount of income coming in every day.
Luo Zheng didn't take it all for himself. He took out a million Divine Crystals and distributed them equally to his subordinates.
Su Kuan also got a share …
After Su Kuan was restricted by his family, it was very difficult for him to even take out a few hundred Divine Crystals. He was overjoyed to get ten thousand Divine Crystals. Following a tycoon was indeed the right choice.
As for his status as a banner master? He didn't have any plans to become a banner master at all. He was the only one under his banner.
Before undoing the Great Maelstrom, Luo Zheng once again returned to his inner world.
…
…
During this time, Luo Zheng would occasionally transform into his inner world to interact with the girls.
When he entered the immortal estate, he would adjust the flow of time back to normal. The flow of time in his inner world was faster, so Luo Zheng could spend more time with the girls. When he left, he synchronized the time in the immortal estate with his mother world, so that they wouldn't have to wait for him for a long time.
Ning Yudie often talked about it, and Luo Zheng would tell them about recent events.
These girls had once devoted themselves to cultivation in order to leave the immortal estate. Now that they had Luo Zheng's constant companionship, and their lifespans were unlimited, they naturally didn't have that state of mind.
Only Xun was devoting herself to cultivating the True Meaning of Dao, but her compatibility seemed to be very low.
Luo Zheng planned to send over other True Meanings of Dao. If she really had the willpower, she could also set foot in the Sea of True Meanings.
After returning to his mother world, Luo Zheng understood that entering the Sea of True Meanings wasn't as difficult as he imagined. The reason why the Divine Territory was so rare was entirely due to the cultural gap of the Jiuli Tribe, which led to the fragmentation of the True Meaning of Dao.
After staying in the immortal estate for a few days, Luo Zheng's inner world's incarnation flashed slightly and arrived on a continent in his inner world.
Tianjue Sect.
The number one sect on this continent.
A young man stood with his hands behind his back and a long spear in his hand, standing at the entrance of the sect.
Not far away, thousands of people surrounded him, all of whom were pointing and whispering.
"Where did this freak come from!"
"That Evil Deities from back then disappeared, and now another monstrous figure has appeared!"
"Challenging the number one sect on his own, too wonderful!"
This young man was Luo Nian.
As Luo Nian grew, he also became more mature.
Outside the immortal estate, time passed very quickly. His current bone age was actually about the same as Luo Zheng's.
After cultivating to Lower True God, he ultimately had a great conflict with the Tianjue Sect because of the continent's number two sect, the Flowing Flower Sect.
Although he understood that it was very simple to solve the matter in front of him — return to the immortal estate, tell his mother, and let his father come forward.
This was his father's world, who could contend against him?
Even those three old monsters in the Tianjue Sect would be annihilated with a thought from his father.
But Luo Nian wanted to solve it himself.
Whether it was magical power or talent, Luo Nian wasn't weak, not to mention that he also had a certain understanding of the True Meaning of Dao. In this round of battle, he had already defeated seven people.
These seven people were all peerless powerhouses of the present generation, but they were all defeated by Luo Nian in one move. The entire continent was practically shaken.
"Tianjue's Three Freaks! Get out! "Luo Nian pointed his halberd and shouted angrily.
The reason why he killed his way to the Tianjue Sect was because of the Flowing Flower Sect's Holy Maiden.
Compared to Luo Zheng, Luo Nian was much more devoted. After the Flowing Flower Sect was bullied by the Tianjue Sect, he was naturally furious for the sake of a beauty.
At this moment, a cold voice suddenly came from the Tianjue Sect.
"Humph! If we still let a brat like you run rampant here, do you really think there's no one in my Tianjue Sect? "The Tianjue Sect finally couldn't bear it anymore, and a red-haired old man shot out from the sect.
"Very good!" Luo Nian smiled coldly, and the long spear in his hand turned out traces of red light. His entire person also emitted an extraordinary aura, very stunning.
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly flew out of the crowd and landed behind Luo Nian.
Seeing Luo Zheng appear, Luo Nian was also stunned, and his face sank. "Father, why are you here, I don't need your help …"
Luo Zheng smiled slightly, glanced at the red-haired old man indifferently, and said, "I won't ask about your matters, but I need your help with something."
He came to find Luo Nian for the two paramita keys.

Chapter 2718
The Shadow of the Paramita
Luo Nian had risen to fame ten years ago, but then suddenly disappeared for three years.
Everyone on the continent thought that this genius had fallen like a meteor, but in fact, he had returned to the immortal mansion to cultivate during that time.
After returning, Luo Nian's cultivation had soared, and his strength was even more astonishing.
Everyone was speculating about the power behind Luo Nian, and what kind of family could cultivate such a character.
Now, the answer had been revealed. A mere Life and Death Realm cultivation was really shocking.
Luo Nian was about to challenge the Three Eccentrics of Heaven's End, but his father suddenly came out to interrupt. He was naturally a bit depressed, but fortunately, Luo Zheng had suppressed his cultivation to the Life and Death Realm.
If Luo Zheng had appeared with his original appearance, the people of Heaven's End Sect would probably have knelt down and kowtowed on the spot.
"Old Eccentrics of Heaven's End, just you wait! I'll be back in a moment! "
Luo Nian threw down these words, and together with Luo Zheng, he teleported to another desolate place in the blink of an eye.
"What's so important?" Luo Nian curled his lips and said.
Luo Zheng gently flipped his hand, and two octagonal copper plates appeared in his hand.
Luo Zheng had obtained one of these copper plates in the Heaven's Wall City. There were no other treasures in the Cenotaph, and only these copper plates attracted Luo Zheng's attention.
When he poured the Spirit Crystal into them, a map appeared on the copper plates, and there was a Sanskrit annotation next to it. Luo Zheng naturally couldn't understand it.
It wasn't until Luo Zheng rescued the Earthen Mouse and obtained the second copper plate that he understood that this copper plate was called the Paramita Key.
As the name implied, these two copper plates should be the key to open a place on the Paramita Key.
Luo Zheng believed that it wouldn't be long before he reached the Paramita Key, and it might be useful at that time.
That day, when Ling Shuang sent the Earthen Mouse's Paramita Key, she also told Luo Zheng that if he wanted to use the key in the Paramita Key, he must first decipher the Sanskrit in the key. If Luo Zheng wanted, she could help.
The Greater One Heavenly Palace employed translators who specialized in deciphering Sanskrit for a living, but it took a long time to decipher it. It could be as short as a few years, or as long as a dozen years. Moreover, it cost a lot of money.
Hearing Ling Shuang's words, Luo Zheng was speechless.
If Luo Zheng's Heavenly Palace, he would be able to deciphering Sanskrit, he would be able to see through Sanskrit in a glance.
Luo Zheng politely declined Ling Shuang's offer. Only then did he bring the two copper plates into his inner world.
"What the hell is this?" Luo Nian pinched the two pieces of copper and examined them. He could only see countless stripes. Where were the Sanskrit characters?
Luo Zheng gently extended his hand and took out a divine crystal.
Seeing the pure energy within the divine crystal, Luo Nian's face was filled with curiosity. "What is this? The energy in it is so amazing! "
Luo Zheng couldn't be blamed for his lack of knowledge. When Luo Zheng saw the Spirit crystal for the first time, he had the same expression.
"Ka!"
Luo Zheng squeezed it gently, dripping the energy in the Spirit crystal onto the Paramita Key.
The patterns on the Paramita Key began to flow slowly. Just like the first experiment, a map gradually emerged.
The Earthen Mouse's Paramita Key was the same.
Two maps. Two golden Sanskrit.
Luo Nian was excited when he saw the golden Sanskrit, but after a while, his expression became strange.
"This piece of copper has' One Layer of Heaven 'written on it. Behind it is a long string of sentences with unknown meaning, chirp …" Luo Nian pointed to the Earthen Mouse's copper.
"This piece of copper has' Nineteen Layers of Heaven 'written on it. Behind it is also a long string of sentences with unknown meaning, also recording …" Luo Nian continued.
"What do you mean by chirp?" Luo Zheng frowned.
Luo Nian shook his head. "I don't know. This is the first time I can't understand Sanskrit. This means it has no meaning."
In the past, Luo Nian could directly decipher the meaning of Sanskrit. But this time, even after deciphering it, the meaning was still unclear.
Luo Zheng thought for a while, then said, "Just repeat those sentences for me to remember."
"Okay." Luo Nian nodded, then recited according to the Sanskrit on the copper.
Sure enough, just like he said, he kept mumbling all kinds of unique syllables, which made Luo Zheng want to laugh.
But he still remembered every syllable without missing a word.
"I'm done." Luo Nian shrugged. He also felt that what he had deciphered was a little stupid. "Can I go back now?"
"Of course!" Luo Zheng patted Luo Nian's head and smiled. "You have good taste. If you really marry your sweetheart, you must tell me!"
"Got it. I'm still busy!" Luo Nian curled his lips. His figure flashed, and he disappeared in front of Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng stood in place, still holding the two Paramita Keys and scrutinizing them.
"That Ear Mouse gave me a First Heaven Paramita Key. That's good …" Luo Zheng muttered.
There was a big difference in value between the First Heaven and the Nineteenth Heaven. The Rat Alliance was full of unscrupulous merchants.
But Luo Zheng had to climb up step by step. When he had just stepped into the Paramita Realm, this First Heaven Paramita Key would be the most suitable.
What Luo Zheng didn't know was that the difference in value between the Paramita Keys was countless times greater than he imagined.
Take the Paramita Token in the Cenotaph for example. Once it was deciphered, it would be worth hundreds of millions of Divine Crystals. It was a world of difference from Ear Mouse's!
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng ascended and left.
As he left, he saw Luo Nian using his spear to defeat one of the three old monsters of the Tianjue Sect.
"This kid …"
Compared to when he was young, Luo Nian was much more arrogant.
But the two couldn't be compared. Although Luo Zheng also grew up in his father's inner world, he didn't know anything about the outside world. Luo Nian, on the other hand, was fearless when he knew everything. No matter how mischievous he was, there would be someone to back him up.
After figuring out the secret of the copper plate, Luo Zheng left the inner world.
A day later, he entered the Sea of True Intent again and helped Ming Xuan solve the third whirlpool and obtain the third candle.
Ming Xuan, who had been trapped for several years and was close to despair, wasn't in a hurry to advance in the Sea of True Intent. On the contrary, Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang planned to take a step further. After rushing past the sixth section line, they charged toward the seventh section line.
After the initial crazy sales of Sword Comprehension Liquid, the sales gradually stabilized. But they still earned millions of Divine Crystals every day. After all, even if the price was reduced to fifteen thousand Divine Crystals, there were still few who could afford it.
Su Kuan received a sum of Divine Crystals and often ran to Dragon City. Just as he said, he could enter Dragon City without using his Banner Master status.
Three months later …
Luo Zheng's Sun Soul became more and more resilient.
For a period of time, whether it was day or night, he stayed in the Sea of True Intent, neither advancing nor retreating. He quietly endured the invasion of the chill in the sea.
Now, Luo Zheng's Sun Soul didn't have much of an advantage compared to others. He could only spend more time cultivating. The tempering of the chill could also continuously strengthen his Sun Soul.
In these three months, he and Ling Shuang had crossed the seventh section line and the eighth section line.
When he crossed the eighth section line, he had already reached the end of the Sea of True Intent. In the azure sea, he could faintly see tiny crystals. These crystals floated in the sea and could actually corrode the Sea of True Intent boats. They undoubtedly formed a powerful obstacle and test for the Sun Soul.
After crossing the ninth section line, he would reach the other shore.

Chapter 2719

Giant Waves
Luo Zheng did not encounter many True Meaning boats between the first and sixth segment lines.
However, after passing the sixth segment line, there were more and more small boats floating on the Sea of True Intent. When they reached the ninth segment line, they looked around.
When he looked at the ninth segment lines, they were densely packed.

Ling Shuang giggled. "The first eight segments are child's play compared to the ninth segment. Most people stay there for a long time. What do you think they're doing?"
Luo Zheng looked around and realized that the True Meaning boats were quietly floating on the sea. The Sun souls were meditating.
Clearly, they were doing the same thing as Luo Zheng. They stayed in the ninth segment lines and received the baptism of the Sea of True Meaning to strengthen their Sun souls.
"The Paramita Realm is a huge hurdle. This hurdle is reflected in the soul. Some people stay in the ninth segment lines to cultivate for years," Ling Shuang said with a smile.
Although the crystals in the sea would corrode the True Meaning boats, they just had to leave the Sea of True Meaning before they collapsed. When they returned, the boats would reform and be brand new.
So in Luo Zheng's eyes, the True Meaning boats were constantly disappearing and condensing.
This was a huge training ground for souls.
"Let's begin," Ling Shuang said with a smile.
Luo Zheng nodded. Like the others, he adjusted the direction of the True Meaning boats and endured the corrosion of the sea.
The people under the banner of Dragon City had a natural advantage in this aspect.
In the process of tempering their Sun souls, there were constantly people buying Soul Pills.
At first, Luo Zheng was rather curious. After all, Divine Crystals could not be brought here. How were they going to trade?
After listening to Ling Shuang, Luo Zheng understood that they were using the Rat Alliance as a guarantor.
One party would give Divine Crystals to the Rat Alliance as a guarantor. Then, the Soul Pills would be traded in the Sea of True Meaning. The Rat Alliance would then pay the Divine Crystals to the other party as a third party.
During the entire process, the Rat Alliance would take a portion of the Divine Crystals as a commission.
And this kind of trade was not limited to the Sea of True Meaning. Even the Paramita trade relied on the Rat Alliance as a guarantor. There were countless such trades every day!
As the guarantor, the Rat Alliance reaped great benefits.
Listening to Ling Shuang, Luo Zheng clicked his tongue in amazement. This group of rats in the mother world was indeed smart …
…
Autumn left and winter came.
The entire Dragon City was covered in a thin layer of snow.
Most people only stayed in the nine-dash line for three to four hours, but Luo Zheng spent twice as much time in it.
In the rest of the time, Luo Zheng went to the Sword Training Pagoda to consolidate the [Fusion God Sword Scripture] and the [Nine Flash Diffraction Sword Scripture].
Luo Zheng knew everything that was happening in his inner world. Luo Zheng only needed to follow the method and ratio that the Evil Deities used to fuse with the Divine Dao. This wasn't difficult at all.
The only troublesome thing was that the Evil Deities weren't upper true gods at that time, so they had to pay special attention to certain aspects when fusing with the perfect divine dao again.

One of the true meaning boats in the nine-segment line suddenly moved. Soon after, hundreds and thousands of true meaning boats followed suit and sped forward.

"These people are planning to break through to the Nirvana Realm," Ling Shuang said with a smile.
"Are we going too?" Luo Zheng asked.
"If you're willing, I'll go with you," Ling Shuang replied.
If Ling Shuang relied on her soul necklace, it wouldn't be difficult for her to break through to the Nirvana Realm.
However, she also thought that she and Luo Zheng needed to train their Yang Souls. Breaking through to the Nirvana Realm too early wouldn't be a good thing even if they succeeded.
"Okay!" Luo Zheng nodded.
Two True-Intent boats also started cruising on the sea.
The nine-dash line was the final bottleneck, so one could imagine how difficult it was. Every time they tried to break through to the Nirvana Realm, there would be tens of thousands of True-Intent boats gathered together. It was a spectacular scene!
Since someone was taking the lead, Luo Zheng was happy to have some free time.
The whole team was divided into three, with Ling Shuang and Luo Zheng's True-Intent boats following behind.
"Whoosh, whoosh …"
As they continued to move forward, waves swept over, and the True-Intent boats that were linked together rose and fell with the waves.
"Everyone, be careful!"
"Before reaching the nine-dash line, the waves will get bigger and bigger!"
"If you can't stabilize your boat, please return on your own!"
During the process of moving forward, people constantly warned loudly, and these warnings were passed on to each other.
It was very common for people to die in the process of breaking through to the Nirvana Realm. This was also a tradition in the nine-dash line.
After these warnings were issued, some people left the team and turned their boats around to return. These people knew their own limits, so naturally, they didn't dare to force their way to the Nirvana Realm.
"I wonder if Dongfang Chunjun had always been here …" Luo Zheng remembered that Dongfang Chunjun was also a character in the nine-dash line, and he had been trapped there for a long time.
Cui Xie couldn't be blamed. He couldn't get a Soul Elixir in the Divine Territory, and he didn't have resources like Dragon City. It was indeed difficult for him to cross to the Nirvana Realm by himself.
After Luo Zheng swallowed a Soul Elixir, the chill was offset, and his state of mind calmed down.
It was also time for Ling Shuang to adjust her heartbeat and stand steadily at the bow of the boat.
Every time the waves of the Sea of True-Intent surged over, the two of them tried their best to keep the True-Intent boats stable. In a fleet of more than ten thousand people, perhaps only a hundred people could do what Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang did.
The fleet traveled for about ten miles, and the waves suddenly became bigger. Under the howling of the cold wind, it was as if it was going to freeze the soul.
The warnings were even more intense, one after another!
"I, I can't do it!"
A person on the left front of Luo Zheng shouted. In a moment of desperation, he directly withdrew from the Sea of True-Intent.
This was still considered lucky.
Not far away, two True-Intent boats were tossed by the waves, and the entire boat flipped over, turning upside down in the sea.
The Sun Soul entered the sea and turned into ashes.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly jumped, and he asked Ling Shuang, who was behind him, "Every time we attack the Nirvana Realm, so many people die?"
"More or less, it's still a little better now. I heard from Granny that a long, long time ago, the number of Soul Elixirs was extremely rare, and countless Sun Souls died at the nine-segment line. At that time, the nine-segment line was known as the Gates of Hell. Those who could enter the Nirvana Realm were one in ten thousand, and the other nine thousand people would all die …" Ling Shuang replied.
After people began to die in the fleet, more people began to feel fearful and began to retreat.
Two-thirds of the fleet of more than ten thousand people actually began to retreat …
The remaining three thousand people were all prepared to fight with their lives on the line. Even in the face of such a harsh environment, they had no intention of retreating!
After another ten miles, the waves doubled again.
A wave swept over, lifting Luo Zheng's True-Intent boat to a height of thirty to forty feet.
"Luo Zheng! Be careful! "Ling Shuang reminded.
She still had a lot of confidence in Luo Zheng's ability to adapt, but she still had some worries in her heart.
Luo Zheng stood at the bow of the boat, gliding along with the waves, and rather ingeniously dispelled this huge wave. But when he stood at the peak of the wave, his expression suddenly changed.
Five kilometers in front of them, there was a huge wave that was over a hundred feet in height surging towards them!

Chapter 2720

Pure Gold Sand
"There's a huge wave ahead!"
Luo Zheng said to Ling Shuang as he slid down with the wave.

Even Luo Zheng wouldn't be able to avoid such a huge wave. Once he was hit by the huge wave, he would also end up with his ship destroyed.

Everyone relayed the message. Ling Shuang also warned the people behind.
Then, Ling Shuang said to Luo Zheng, "There's a limit to the width of this wave. When it reaches us, retreat and come back in four breaths' time!"

After they left the Sea of True Intent, they would disappear along with the boat. But when they returned, they would appear in the same place. This was equivalent to hiding in space to avoid the huge wave.

Luo Zheng had already activated the illusion breaking spell. Just as the huge wave was about to drown him, he and the boat disappeared in front of the huge wave.
Luo Zheng, who was sitting under the banner, suddenly opened his eyes.
Earlier, he was still in the midst of the strange waves. Now, he was back outside of Dragon City. The surroundings were quiet. Everyone was cultivating quietly. He wondered if there was anyone outside of Dragon City who was trying to reach the Nirvana Realm with him.

The number of boats floating in the sea decreased too quickly.

"Because at that time, all the boats can only retreat and not move forward." Ling Shuang shrugged.
While they were talking, another huge wave rolled up in front of them …
As the group continued to move forward, people kept disappearing. They either left the Sea of True Intent or died in the huge waves.

Traces of warmth emanated from the seawater, and the originally biting cold had already disappeared without a trace. The Sun Soul stood on the True Intent boat, and only felt a warm feeling.
There was a carefree laughter in front of them. Some people were lying on their backs on the Sea of True Intent boat while others were jumping happily at the bow.
The Sea of True Intent became shallower and shallower …
A few hundred feet away, there was a golden beach.
"We … have already reached the Nirvana Realm?" Luo Zheng said as he gazed at the beach.
"Yes!" Ling Shuang replied with a faint smile on her face.
"It seems very simple …" Luo Zheng originally thought that there would be more troublesome things waiting for him. He didn't expect to reach the Nirvana Realm safely after going over these huge waves.
Ling Shuang tilted her head and thought for a while before replying, "It's not that difficult. But in the process of breaking through to the Nirvana Realm, as long as there is a slight mistake, you will die without a doubt!"
The Sea of True Intent posed a great threat to the Sun Soul. No one could afford to have a single mistake.
"Huala …"
"Huala …"

Someone jumped down from the boat. The Sun Soul stepped on the beach and ran wildly on it.

Luo Zheng jumped down from the Sea of True Intent boat and looked around. The beach was bare. Other than the golden sand, there were occasional bare rocks.
"I wonder where the Nirvana Realm token is?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.

"It's under your feet!" Ling Shuang smiled faintly.

Heavenly Sunflower Temple
Luo Zheng bent down and scooped up a handful of sand, then held it in his hand.

Ling Shuang pointed at the bare stones not far away and continued, "Those are Celestial Blue Stones. After fusing with them, you'll have one Jun of strength!"

When Yin Yuehuan brought him into Dragon City for the first time, someone used the red stones in Dragon City to test his strength. That person had two Jun of strength, and his Nirvana Token was a piece of Celestial Blue Stone.

Everything in the Nirvana Realm contained power. Even a grain of sand or a leaf could be fused with them. Walking along the beach, Luo Zheng could feel the rich energy aura with every step he took.

"It's coming again …"
This time, a little bit of black liquid overflowed from the two sides of the wall.
When Luo Zheng saw the liquid, he closed his eyes and sealed his Divine Sense, entering the Emotion Severing state.
Ling Shuang was the same as before. She had no way to deal with it. As the liquid turned into eyeballs, she fell into despair again.
"Little Soul Arrow!"
Since he couldn't see the target, Luo Zheng simply shot Little Soul Arrows forward.
"Swish swish swish swish …"
The opening of the depression was very small, and under such a barrage of bullets, it was bound to be hit.
However, despite the continuous barrage of soul arrows, none of them hit their target!
Just as Luo Zheng was puzzled, he heard Ling Shuang scream, "They … are coming … above … below … everywhere …"
Luo Zheng clenched his teeth. He had forgotten that the Eye Demon of Fear was a puddle of black liquid. It did not need to condense into a shape. It only needed to spread along the wall to become an eye.
Right at this moment, with a thought in his heart, an unquestionable aura emanated from his soul.
Most Divine Daos used True Essence and the momentum of the inner world, but some Divine Daos could affect the soul, or the use of energy at the soul level.
On this point, both the Great Ruling Art and the Emotion Severing Divine Dao were the same.
"Great Ruling Art!"
Luo Zheng's soul exuded an unquestionable majesty.
Under this pressure, it was actually directed at Ling Shuang!
Normally, Ling Shuang's Yang Soul wasn't much weaker than Luo Zheng's, and the Great Ruling Technique wouldn't have had any effect. But now, she was seized by fear, and had no ability to resist.
In a moment …
Ling Shuang had stopped screaming and crying.
She was like a puppet, staring blankly in front of her.
Luo Zheng carefully pulled Ling Shuang to prevent her from being dragged out. At the same time, he was looking for a way to activate the Sorcerer's Necklace in the depths of her soul.
A person's memories were very messy, not to mention that Luo Zheng had cultivated the Great Ruling Art. But he had not used it much, so he was not familiar with it.
Just as Luo Zheng was flipping through her memories, he saw a door, a sealed door.
"What is this?" Luo Zheng was quite puzzled.
Why was there such a thing in Ling Shuang's memories?
When Luo Zheng wanted to touch this door, a strong repulsive force came from the door.
Now was not the time to think about this door …
After a while, he finally found some memories related to the Sorcerer's Necklace …
… …
The strands of black liquid were like silk threads, binding Ling Shuang and Luo Zheng's bodies. Small eyeballs were attached to their shoulders, heads, and other body parts.
It seemed that this Fearful Eye Demon would not give up until it got the two of them out.
Just as it was strenuous, Ling Shuang's unconscious body suddenly moved.
She placed her hands on her chest and gently touched the Sorcerer's Necklace …
"Weng —"
A dark blue light flickered, filling every corner of this depression.
The black eyeballs became abnormally fragile under this light, and cracks quickly appeared on the surface of the eyeballs. Then, with a "pop" sound, they fought to be the first to explode.
Even the black liquid started to boil, and in the blink of an eye, it evaporated completely.
"Success …"
Luo Zheng opened his eyes and looked at the black marks on the wall, revealing a smile.
However, Ling Shuang still had no reaction, sitting in his arms without moving. Luo Zheng was stunned for a moment before he reacted. Ling Shuang was still under the influence of the Great Ruling Art, and was still his puppet.
After Luo Zheng released her, Ling Shuang slowly woke up. She stared blankly at Luo Zheng, completely unaware of what had happened.
"I just used the Great Ruling Art." Luo Zheng pointed at the black marks on the wall. "That thing was destroyed by your necklace …"
Even though Luo Zheng knew that the necklace on Ling Shuang's chest was not simple, the power of the Sorcerer's Necklace still exceeded his expectations.
"That thing was really destroyed?" Ling Shuang finally calmed down after seeing the black marks. However, when Luo Zheng mentioned the Great Ruling Art, she was slightly stunned. "Then, didn't you just …"
Since Luo Zheng used the Sorcerer's Necklace, he must have entered deep into her memories. Many of her thoughts were probably seen by Luo Zheng. For a woman, this was indeed a subtle and awkward feeling.
"I was just looking for how to use the Sorcerer's Necklace," Luo Zheng said seriously. Of course, he did not mention the sealed door.
Luo Zheng also had many secrets, and similarly, it was taboo for others to pry into them.
"Oh …" Ling Shuang calmed down a little.
Luo Zheng had no choice in the situation just now. After all, she could not face those eyeballs at all.
"But it's still a little strange." Luo Zheng looked at the black marks and said, "These things are spreading on the wall like flowing water, but the sound of them sliding across the wall was not like that. Other than these eyeballs, there should be something else outside."
Luo Zheng did not tell Ling Shuang that these things were called Eye Demons of Fear, lest he had to explain where he knew about them.
"There's something else outside?" Ling Shuang asked nervously.
"But that thing should not be able to move. At least, it hasn't moved for a long time now." Luo Zheng judged in a relaxed manner.

Chapter 2726

The Secret of the Temple
The Soul-melting Essence lasted for more than two hours before it gradually dissipated.
The entire temple was still very quiet …
"Let's go out," Luo Zheng said.
"But …" Ling Shuang felt a lingering fear when she thought of the black eyeball.
"Don't worry. That thing itself isn't powerful. It can kill people because of the Soul-melting Essence. Even if it doesn't die, it's not scary," Luo Zheng said.
If the Eye Demon was really powerful, they wouldn't be able to fight back while hiding in the depression.
Luo Zheng walked out of the depression first. Ling Shuang cautiously poked her head out and looked around.
There was a black mark on the wall. Luo Zheng followed the mark.
On the balcony on the other side of the thirteenth floor, there was a pale gold object.
Luo Zheng flipped around the corridor and jumped to the other side. When he reached the balcony, his pupils shrank. "A spider?"
Ling Shuang followed him. When she saw the object, her face lit up. "It's the Sky Weaver Spider! I understand now. The Sky Weaver Spider on the second floor must have been left behind by this thing! "
Ling Shuang had seen and heard of the Sky Weaver Spider before, so she could recognize it at a glance.
"Could it be that the thing that attacked us was the Sky Weaver Spider?" Ling Shuang asked.
"But the Sky Weaver Spider is already dead." Luo Zheng reached out and fiddled with it. The Sky Weaver Spider lay motionless on the ground. "What happened just now wasn't that simple …"
He flipped the Sky Weaver Spider over and examined it. There was a small hole between its head and abdomen. There were some faint black marks around the hole.
Just as Luo Zheng extended his hand towards the small hole, the dead spider suddenly retracted its claws, flipped over, and began to climb rapidly along the wall.
Clatter clatter clatter …
The sound of the Sky Weaver Spider climbing up the wall was exactly the same as before!
Ling Shuang shrank back in fright. Luo Zheng's eyes flashed sharply. He reached out and flicked his finger. A small soul arrow shot out.
"Crack!"
The soul arrow instantly nailed the Heavenweaver Spider to the wall.
Then, his hand slowly changed shape into a sharp knife. He peeled the Heavenweaver Spider off the wall and cut along its central axis from its head all the way to its abdomen.
"Zi …"
Black balls the size of pellets gushed out from the incisions.
"Ah! Again! " Seeing these little black balls, Ling Shuang's face changed again. It seemed like this encounter in the temple was something she would remember for the rest of her life.
"Zi …"
Those pellet-sized balls cracked open, revealing extremely small eyes.
These small eyes could also evoke fear, but they were much weaker than before. Luo Zheng didn't even need to close his eyes to resist.
"These are the offspring of those things. They haven't grown up yet, so they aren't dangerous …"
As Luo Zheng spoke, he stretched out his finger and pointed at those small eyes. With one finger, the small eyes exploded and turned into a pool of black liquid.
There were still quite a few small eyes that came out of the Heavenweaver Spider's body, and even more of them surged in a certain direction along the wall.
These descendants of the Fear of Eye Demons would be a disaster if left alive, so Luo Zheng naturally didn't intend to let them go.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng had killed a large number of them.
However, another group of descendants of the Fear of Eye Demons moved along the wall to the back of the largest statue.
"What's behind that statue?"
Luo Zheng followed, flipping over the huge statue in one fell swoop. When he saw what was behind it, he was slightly taken aback.
Behind this statue, there were three small pillars. And on these pillars, there were three spheres enshrined. The first two spheres were demonic red glazed eyeballs, and the last one was a pure white sphere!
Seeing that the small eyes were about to drill into the glazed eyeballs, Luo Zheng made a move and wiped them all out, not leaving a single one behind.
"What's behind the statue …"
Ling Shuang followed him. When she saw the white ball, her body suddenly stiffened.
"The soul aura in this white ball is very strong," Luo Zheng said.
"This should be a soul crystal! What a huge soul crystal! From the looks of it … I'm afraid that countless people died in the Sunflower Temple, and the Sun Souls that were melted by the Soul Melting Light were all concentrated in this ball, "Ling Shuang said after thinking for a while.
"Does this soul crystal have the same effect as a Soul Pill?" Luo Zheng stared at the white ball and asked.
The aura he felt from this white ball was very similar to a Soul Pill. At a glance, it looked like a large Soul Pill.
If this thing had the same effect as a Soul Pill, then its value in Luo Zheng's heart was greatly reduced. Soul Pills could be traded, and they could also be bought with Divine Crystals.
"No …" Ling Shuang shook her head, her entire body becoming excited. "A Soul Pill can only restore the power of the soul and temporarily strengthen the Sun Soul, but a soul crystal can increase the upper limit of the Sun Soul. It can make our Sun Soul even stronger!"
When she said this, Luo Zheng's gaze became sharp.
When one's soul was weak, there were still many methods to strengthen it, but after stepping into the Sun Soul, it became difficult to cultivate the Sun Soul. Other than tempering himself in the Sea of True Intent, he didn't find any other methods to strengthen it.
If he could absorb this soul crystal, it might be able to bring his Sun Soul to a higher level!
"Since soul crystals are so useful, don't other creatures refine them?" Luo Zheng asked.
Some experts could kill other Sun Souls and refine them for their own use. For some ambitious existences, wouldn't it be easy to increase the power of their Sun Soul?
"Someone once did this, but later he went crazy." Ling Shuang shrugged her slender shoulders. "Other than the Soul Melting Light, it's very difficult to purify the Sun Soul with other methods. Forcibly absorbing the Sun Soul of another person will only result in the fusion of countless memories. If the memories of hundreds of people are mixed together, the final result can be imagined …"
"Understood." Luo Zheng nodded.
Although he had figured out the white ball of light, what were those two demonic red glazed eyeballs?
Even the knowledgeable Ling Shuang didn't know, let alone Luo Zheng. In the end, Luo Zheng could only ask 9527.
9527 smiled faintly and said, "Can't you see? It's all because of your good luck that you were able to survive this time. "
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng asked.
"This is not a Sky Sunflower Temple at all. It's just a name that you humans made up. This temple originally belonged to the Eye Demons of Fear. That's why there are so many statues of Eye Demons on the walls …" 9527 said, "However, the temple has been ruined for so many years. The Eye Demons of Fear should have died out long ago. Perhaps some of the Eye Demons of Fear came back to life and controlled a Sky Weaver Spider that broke in. They then used the Spirit Melting Essence Light in the temple to harvest the Yang Soul so that they could reproduce rapidly and cultivate their offspring …"
The soul crystal was the "food" that the Eye Demons of Fear had painstakingly accumulated. If Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang hadn't come, this race would probably have revived after recuperating!

Demon Eye
Although 9527 had answered most of the questions in Luo Zheng's mind, he was still confused.
"Did the Eye Demon of Fear appear in the Chaos before?" Luo Zheng asked.
"You could say that. The Eye Demon was once a big clan," 9527 answered.
"Then all the clans in the Chaos can build temples on the Paramita? Are they also considered creatures of a higher level? "Luo Zheng asked again.
