"Strictly speaking, among the Eye Demons of Fear, only that Demon Eye is qualified as a creature beyond its level. The ones that you have encountered are merely its disciples and grand-disciples, and they are extremely weak. If it is a fully matured Eye Demon, you will not be its opponent."
At this moment, Ling Shuang said, "Luo Zheng, why are you in a daze?"
Ling Shuang naturally did not know that Luo Zheng was communicating with 9527.
After coming back to his senses, Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang discussed how to absorb the soul crystal.
They still had a long way to go in the Paramita. No one would give up the opportunity to strengthen their soul.
The two of them sat on one side, formed their consciousness, and went into the soul crystal.
The feeling of absorbing the soul crystal was similar to fusing with a soul. To some extent, they were devouring the Sun Soul of another group of people! It was just that these Sun Souls had been purified beforehand …
As the two of them kept absorbing, the white ball shrank at a visible speed.
At the same time, the auras emitted by the two Sun Souls were also visibly increasing at an explosive speed!
Half an hour later …
Ling Shuang had already retracted her consciousness. At this moment, the soul crystal had shrunk by half.
She and Luo Zheng discovered the soul crystal together, so they naturally had the right to share it. But Ling Shuang considered that she had other opportunities, so she gave up on absorbing the soul crystal. She just watched Luo Zheng quietly absorb the remaining half.
Half an hour later, Luo Zheng absorbed the last bit of the soul crystal.
When he opened his eyes, he felt a surge of energy!
"This Soul Crystal is indeed miraculous …"
Luo Zheng had painstakingly tempered himself in the Sea of True Intent for several months, and his soul force had only increased by two levels. But after absorbing this soul crystal, he felt that his soul force had increased by three times!
"Of course!" Ling Shuang smiled and said, "You can be considered to have directly merged with the Sun Souls of hundreds of people …"
When the Sun Soul was crystallized by the Soul Fusing Origin Light, there would definitely be an extremely great loss. Ling Shuang's words were naturally exaggerated, but the effect it brought was obvious.
After absorbing the huge Soul Crystal, the two of them looked at the demonic red glazed eyeball.
"I wonder if this thing can be fused into the Nirvana Token?" Luo Zheng did not forget that his purpose of entering the temple was to find the Nirvana Token.
The two glazed eyeballs in front of him also contained extraordinary energy.
Ling Shuang tilted her head and looked at it. Even with Ling Shuang's vast knowledge, she had no idea what it was. "No matter what it is, we can try to fuse it. As long as it's not a living thing, it shouldn't resist …"
"A living thing …" Luo Zheng thought of the dead Sky Weaver Spider.
This Sky Weaver Spider should have been dead for a long time, and had always been used as a puppet by the Fearful Eye Demon. "Can the dead Sky Weaver Spider be used as the Nirvana Token?"
"Yes and no. Anything in the Nirvana Realm can be fused, but fusing with a dead thing doesn't have much meaning. It's better to fuse with the Sky Weaver Silk that it spits out!" Ling Shuang replied.
Now that they had these two glazed eyeballs, the Sky Weaver Silk was naturally not a consideration.
Luo Zheng walked in front of the glazed eyeballs and reached out to touch them for a while before saying, "I … still don't know how to fuse with the Nirvana Token …"
Ling Shuang laughed out loud.
She had always felt that Luo Zheng was an anomaly. He knew things that he shouldn't know, but he didn't know anything that everyone knew.
"I'll teach you. Fusing with the Nirvana Token isn't too difficult, especially when it's a dead thing like this. It won't resist you, and you also need to use the True Intent of Dao when fusing with it …"
As Ling Shuang spoke, she held up the Nirvana Token.
Strands of the Sword Fate Eternal True Intent emerged from her body. The aura of the True Intent lingered on the surface of the glazed eyeballs. Just when she wanted to fuse it into her Sun Soul, a repelling force appeared.
She couldn't fuse with the glazed eyeballs!
"Eh?" Ling Shuang cried out strangely.
"What's going on?" Luo Zheng asked.
"This Nirvana Token doesn't match with the Sword Fate Eternal True Intent …" Ling Shuang pouted.
"Repelling? The Nirvana Token will choose the True Intent of Dao? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Ling Shuang exhaled and said, "It's very rare. I heard that only some very powerful Nirvana Tokens would have such a thing happen. I didn't expect it to happen with the glazed eyeballs!"
Such a situation showed that the glazed eyeballs were extraordinary. Ling Shuang was naturally very regretful that she couldn't fuse with them.
"Let me try!"
Luo Zheng followed suit and followed Ling Shuang's method to activate the Sword Fate Eternal True Intent.
The situation was exactly the same. When Luo Zheng tried to fuse it into his body, his Sun Soul and the glazed eyeballs repelled each other.
Luo Zheng thought for a moment. The aura emitted by his Sun Soul changed again. The aura of the Dao technique's True Intent of Nature was released again.
This time, the glazed eyeballs naturally fused into Luo Zheng's body.
"It's perfectly compatible with the Dao technique's True Intent of Nature!" Ling Shuang exclaimed.
This was also the benefit of cultivating two types of True Intent of Dao. Since it repelled one type of True Intent of Dao, it could be changed to another type of True Intent of Dao.
It was just that everyone's compatibility with each type of True Intent of Dao was different. Once they chose one, very few people would change their tune.
When Luo Zheng fused the glazed eyeballs into his body, he felt that there was something extra in his soul. At the same time, he could clearly sense its existence.
As Luo Zheng groped around, the glazed eyeballs slowly rose in his Sun Soul and moved to his eye.
Ling Shuang saw that on Luo Zheng's blurry face, a scarlet glow suddenly flashed!
Just by looking at each other, Ling Shuang let out a scream and kept retreating. She was extremely frightened.
Luo Zheng also closed his eyes. That demonic red glow also disappeared.
"Sorry …"
Luo Zheng was just trying it out. He didn't expect Ling Shuang to have such a big reaction.
The glazed eyeballs' ability was the same as those Eye Demons of Fear. This kind of method could be used to great effect at crucial moments.
Ling Shuang didn't mind. She said, "This ability to disturb one's mind … is very rare among the Nirvana Realm Tokens of the First Heaven. I just don't know how much Divine Power this demonic eye can unleash."

Chapter 2728
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
Awakening
Ling Shuang was a little resentful.
In the beginning, Ling Shuang did not take the Sky Sunflower Temple seriously. For someone of her status, the first two levels of heaven were just a transition.
The Nirvana Realm tokens found in the first and second levels of heaven were not much. This was only the entry stage of the Nirvana Realm. Most people would simply integrate a Nirvana Realm token here and start to advance.
However, there were exceptions to everything. For example, the Sky Weaver Silk and the Sky Weaver Spider existed in the third level of heaven. They rarely appeared in the first and second levels of heaven.
The exception this time was even more ridiculous. The dilapidated temple actually hid such a murderous intent. There were even two glazed eyeballs. Ling Shuang could not estimate which level of heaven the Nirvana Realm token belonged to.
The most unfortunate thing was that she could not integrate it. Naturally, she felt regretful.
"Since you can't integrate this Demon Eye, you should keep it," Luo Zheng said.
Ling Shuang shook her head. "It's useless even if you give it to me. You can sell it. It should be able to exchange for a lot of Soul Elixirs. I'll go and integrate the Sky Weaver Silk …"
The Sky Weaver Silk was very suitable for the Sword Fate Eternal True Meaning.
The Taiyi Sword Technique that Ling Shuang cultivated was divided into three sects. One of them was the Coiling Sect. After integrating the Sky Weaver Silk, she could maximize the power of the Coiling Sect. The two were quite compatible.
"How can that be? Since we're going into the Sky Sunflower Temple together, we'll split the things we find equally. Even if you can't integrate it, you should keep it," Luo Zheng said seriously.
However, Ling Shuang still declined. She only smiled and said, "It's a pity that you're also cultivating the Sword Fate Eternal True Meaning. If you're cultivating other True Meaning, you can integrate two Nirvana Realm tokens …"
"One person can have two Nirvana Realm tokens?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Ling Shuang nodded. "Not only two. The powerful people in the Sky Palace can even have three or even four Nirvana Realm tokens. For every True Meaning of Dao that they cultivate, they can have one more token …"
Most of the powerful people in the Origin World had more than one Nirvana Realm token. They would usually cultivate the True Meaning of Dao according to their needs.
Everyone had a True Meaning of Dao that was most compatible with them. The Nirvana Realm token that was fused with this True Meaning of Dao would occupy the main position while the other Nirvana Realm tokens would be subsidiary.
"I see … In that case, I can still fuse with another Nirvana Realm Token," said Luo Zheng.
"Of course." Ling Shuang nodded and smiled.
"Since accommodating one more Nirvana Realm Token means one more supernatural power, and even the power of Shenjun will increase exponentially, why do the people of Dragon City only cultivate one True Meaning of Dao?" Luo Zheng asked again.
Ling Shuang blinked her eyes and said, "Not everyone is like you, able to comprehend another True Meaning of Dao in a short period of time. When they truly cultivate to a higher level, they will also choose the second and third True Meaning of Dao."
Hearing this, Luo Zheng suddenly felt that he should pick up the True Meaning of Good and Evil.
Back when he received Yuan Chengtian's inheritance in the Land of Dao Battle, Luo Zheng also comprehended the True Meaning of Good and Evil. However, after that, Luo Zheng did not put any effort into this True Meaning of Dao.
Luo Zheng's compatibility with the True Meaning of Good and Evil should not be as good as the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate. However, it should be stronger than the True Meaning of Dao Technique. It should not be too difficult to master it through a comprehensive study.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to go down with Ling Shuang to fuse with the Heaven Weaving Silk, he looked back and saw that there was a small crack on the wall at the top.
This wall was in a straight line with the two glazed eyeballs and the soul crystal.
Just now, when those small black eyeballs moved in this direction, Luo Zheng thought that their target was the two glazed eyeballs. However, the glazed eyeballs could not be hidden. Could it be that there was something hidden in the crack on the wall?
No matter what, Luo Zheng's current Yang Soul Body could not destroy the temple. He could do it again after his physical body crossed the Nirvana Realm.
He always felt that this matter was not as simple as what 9527 said.
Since the Sky Sunflower Temple had been dilapidated for so many years and the Eye Demon of Fear had disappeared for so many years, it was very strange that a few of them suddenly came back to life.
After the two of them stored the Heaven Weaving Spider's body, they returned to the second floor. Ling Shuang used the True Meaning of Dao to store the Heaven Weaving Silk for her own use. Their first trip to the Nirvana Realm came to an end.
After leaving the Sky Sunflower Temple, the two of them left the Nirvana Realm one after another.
When Luo Zheng woke up, the sky was already bright. Most of the people who were cultivating had also stopped.
"Hong Tian, Hong Tian! Wake up! "
Under Yin Yuehuan's flag, there was a person sitting upright. Someone beside him was calling out to him.
After calling out a few words, Hong Tian fell to the ground with a plop. He had actually lost consciousness ….
Seeing this scene, Yin Yuehuan frowned. After she went forward to check, her face darkened. "Hong Tian has been wandering around the nine-dash line. I'm afraid he failed to break through to the Nirvana Realm last night …"
Seeing Hong Tian lying on the ground, Luo Zheng also shook his head slightly. He went forward and said, "The storm that broke through to the Nirvana Realm this time was indeed very strong. There were many casualties …"
After the Sun Soul was swallowed by the Sea of True Meaning, only a soulless body was left.
Every time one tried to break through to the Nirvana Realm, they would walk through the gates of hell. Many people like Hong Tian never woke up again. Whether it was in Dragon City or other places of cultivation, this kind of thing was not uncommon.
After Yin Yuehuan ordered people to move Hong Tian's body away, her eyes focused on Luo Zheng again. "You've passed the Nirvana Realm?"
Luo Zheng had been in Dragon City for less than a year. In the beginning, he was almost different every day. Later on, it was different every month. Now, he had directly broken through to the Nirvana Realm.
Thinking of the years when she was trapped in the nine-dash line, Yin Yuehuan could not help but feel that fate was unfair.
"Last night, I did break through to the Nirvana Realm," Luo Zheng replied.
"Banner Lord has crossed to the Nirvana Realm!"
"Banner Lord has finally reached the Nirvana Realm …"
Qiu Siyuan, Yue Baicheng, and the others also noticed the changes in Luo Zheng.
Actually, in their eyes, reaching the Nirvana Realm was not uncommon. However, Luo Zheng entering the Nirvana Realm still made them look forward to it. In their eyes, Luo Zheng was definitely the most special one.
"You've just reached the Nirvana Realm, and your Sun Soul has actually been reborn?" Yin Yuehuan stared at Luo Zheng and said.
Although Luo Zheng's Soul Power had been growing steadily in the past few months, that kind of growth was normal. As long as he was immersed in the Sea of True Meaning every day, his Sun Soul would grow steadily.
However, Luo Zheng's growth this time was too shocking. Even Yin Yuehuan felt inferior.
This guy had just entered the Nirvana Realm. How did he do it?
"My luck is not bad this time. I found some opportunities in the First Heaven," Luo Zheng replied casually.
"Some opportunities …" Yin Yuehuan pondered over Luo Zheng's words and was suddenly speechless. What kind of opportunities did it take to make the Sun Soul grow so much? Unless it was to devour the Soul Crystal …

Chapter 2729
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
Testing Again
Dragon City.
It was particularly lively in front of the trial red rock. Last night, more than ten people broke into the Nirvana Realm outside Dragon City.
After fusing with the Nirvana token for the first time, these people couldn't wait to test their strength.
A long-haired woman stood in front of the red rock.
A small seed appeared on top of the woman's head.
"It's a spirit fire seed …"
"She's lucky. It's hard to find a spirit fire seed!"
"Someone must have helped her and prepared this seed for her!
Envy appeared on everyone's faces.
The woman held a green short sword in her hand.
When she raised the short sword, the spirit fire seed emitted a trace of fire. A little flame also burned on her short sword.
"Go!"
The woman suddenly rushed forward. When she arrived in front of the red rock, she suddenly slashed with the short sword.
"Boom!"
Under this slash, all the power was absorbed by the red rock.
After a while, the red rock flashed with light. A strange pattern appeared, and the word "three" appeared in the middle.
This result immediately attracted a burst of cheers.
"It's within my expectations …"
"The spirit fire seed's power can't reach the strength of three Jun. Together with this woman's cultivation, it's almost there."
"For someone who just entered the Nirvana Realm, this is already a very good start."
The woman was obviously very satisfied with her result. She put away the short sword, stepped back, and stood in front of a tall man.
This man was carrying a large flag. Obviously, he was a Banner Lord.
Luo Zheng, who was in the crowd, saw this scene. His thoughts were not on the woman's result.
This woman had just entered the Nirvana Realm. Obviously, she was not a Banner Lord, but she could enter and leave Dragon City freely.
It seemed that the rules of entering and leaving Dragon City were not as strict as he thought.
He didn't know how Su Kuan got in and out without a flag. It seemed that he had to catch him and ask him.
After the woman stepped back, another man stepped forward.
The Nirvana token that this man fused with was the most common celestial stone. It could be seen everywhere on the beach in the Nirvana Realm. It was very common.
Of course, this man's result was also very common.
Even though he used all his strength to attack the red stone, only the word "one" appeared on the red stone. This meant that his strength was below the strength of two Shen Jun …
However, the man was still very satisfied. He had finally set foot on the Nirvana Realm. Fusing with the celestial bluestone was just a transition. If there was a chance in the future, he could change it again.
The following few people's Nirvana Realm tokens were very ordinary. There were celestial blue stones, white angelica grass, and so on. They were all ordinary items. Most of the strength of these people was between one to two Godly Jun.
However, there was a man with a delicate appearance who took out a very unique Nirvana Realm token. It was a shiny crystal chip.

Yue Baicheng was a very cautious person. When he first started selling the Sword Comprehension Liquid, he knew that it would definitely have a conflict of interest with others. Naturally, he had also secretly inquired about it.
That Situ Xiu was someone Luo Zheng absolutely could not provoke at this stage!
If Yue Baicheng hadn't fainted, he would have definitely tried his best to stop it. He didn't expect that after he woke up, Luo Zheng would have already smashed his shop.
"Banner Master, this time … I was too rash …"
Seeing that Luo Zheng was venting his anger for him, Yue Baicheng was naturally moved. However, provoking an alliance master that he shouldn't have pushed Luo Zheng into the abyss.
Qiu Siyuan, on the other hand, didn't change his bad habits as a hedonistic disciple. "What's there to be reckless about? Banner Master did it for us! Moreover, those guys should have been ruthlessly taught a lesson! "
"What do you know?" Yue Baicheng unhappily retorted.
Seeing Yue Baicheng's distressed appearance, Luo Zheng lightly smiled and said in a sincere tone, "You should properly recuperate. I know these things. Although Qiu Siyuan doesn't use his brain to speak, he's right. There are some things that as long as we make concessions, the other party will press on step by step."
Yue Baicheng was silent. It could be seen that he was still very worried.
Today, it was very lively outside the area where Luo Zheng had planted his flag. Everyone knew that Situ Xiu wouldn't leave the matter at that. Some people already faintly knew what was going to happen tonight.
The setting sun was like blood, outlining the enormous shadow of Dragon City.
A group of more than ten people left Dragon City and flew towards the wilderness.
Wang Xiao was especially happy today. After sending away Alliance Master Liu, he could become a true Alliance Master. The key was that Situ Xiu would help him get rid of Luo Zheng!
Within this range of several hundred li, there were many flag poles densely packed together. Most of the people under these flags were already prepared to cultivate after nightfall. When they saw Situ Xiu and the other alliance masters flying over, most of them revealed curious expressions.
"These Alliance Masters can plant their flags outside Dragon City, why did they run so far away?"
"Didn't you hear what happened during the day?"
"Anyway, someone is going to be unlucky …"
Outside Dragon City wasn't a peaceful place. In any case, the strict rules of Dragon City didn't extend to here. Killing people was too common.
"It's over there." Wang Xiao saw a flag with the word "Luo" written on it in the distance. The corner of his mouth slightly raised as he spoke to Situ Xiu.
Situ Xiu's fat body shuttled through the air and arrived in the sky. He coldly scolded, "Who is Luo Zheng!"
Yin Yuehuan raised her head and saw Situ Xiu. Her heart sank slightly.
This Alliance Master Situ was just like the rumors said. He couldn't tolerate overnight grudges!
She glanced around and saw Alliance Master Tong. With a thought, she stepped forward bravely in front of Luo Zheng.
Yin Yuehuan obtained her current status by following Alliance Master Tong all the way. She wanted Alliance Master Tong to intercede for her. "Alliance Master Tong, I'm afraid this is a misunderstanding. Luo Zheng is also new and doesn't know the rules …"
Alliance Master Tong looked a little embarrassed. He knew that Situ Xiu was furious today. He couldn't quench his anger at all. Instead, he would offend Situ Xiu. So, he simply pretended not to hear.
"Who are you! Get lost! "
Without seeing how Situ Xiu attacked, an invisible force hit Yin Yuehuan heavily. This force was so strong that Yin Yuehuan was smashed to the ground as if she was hit by a heavy hammer.
"Banner Master!"
"Banner Master!"
Yin Yuehuan's people under the flag screamed when they saw this scene.
As a Banner Master, Yin Yuehuan wasn't weak. Although she was severely injured by this blow, she still struggled to get up from the ground.
"You're not dead?" Situ Xiu was a little surprised. He clenched his fist and swung it at Yin Yuehuan.
An invisible force smashed toward Yin Yuehuan again like a heavy hammer. Under this blow, it was likely that her body would be smashed into a pulp.
However, before the force hit her, a figure suddenly appeared beside Yin Yuehuan.
Then, everyone heard a muffled "clang". Situ Xiu's blow seemed to have hit a reinforced iron body.
The person who blocked this blow was Luo Zheng.
Wang Xiao saw Luo Zheng blocking the attack, so he reminded Situ Xiu from behind.
Situ Xiu sized him up and was a little surprised. "You're Luo Zheng? Tell me, where did you get the Sword Comprehension Spiritual Liquid! "
Today, Situ Xiu came here not only to kill, but also to find out where the Sword Comprehension Spiritual Liquid came from.
He helped that person sell the Sword Comprehension Spiritual Liquid. It seemed that he was rich, but in fact, 99% of the income was in that person's pocket. If there was a cheaper source, he was naturally willing to take a batch.
Luo Zheng lowered his head to check. The blow just now didn't seem to be very powerful, but it caused a lot of damage to Yin Yuehuan. All her bones were broken, and her Dantian was probably damaged as well. However, she was still alive.

Chapter 2734
Infuriation
As soon as Luo Zheng said this, everyone's face was filled with astonishment.

Situ Xiu's slightly chubby face was already deathly pale. After being in Dragon City for so many years, his violent temper had calmed down a lot. However, no one had ever dared to speak to him like this.

After sending Wang Xiao flying, Situ Xiu could no longer control his anger. He charged toward Luo Zheng like an angry beast.

He could also tell that this Situ Xiu was immensely strong. He should have mainly cultivated the Divine Dao of Strength and borrowed the Divine Force of the Paramita Token.

Although the people under the banner had already scattered in all directions, after feeling the power, they still found it difficult to stabilize their bodies. Some people even sat down on the ground.
Those Banner Masters even pulled out the flags they had planted and moved them to another place.

"That guy is so scared that he won't run?"

Since ancient times, the Celestial Palace had been filled with sword cultivators. A physical contest like Luo Zheng and Situ Xiu's was extremely rare … Of course, in the eyes of most people, this couldn't be considered a contest. They only thought that this was a one-sided slaughter by Situ Xiu.

As for the other people under the banner, when they heard the words of the academy disciples, they almost wanted to vomit blood. What did they mean by "As expected of Banner Master"? Which Banner Master had the qualifications to contend with the Alliance Masters?
Luo Zheng's slightly thin figure slowly floated up from the dust. He paced in the air and looked at Situ Xiu on the ground with an indifferent gaze. "Those who don't take their opponents seriously will often suffer a great loss. Overestimating one's own strength is exactly what people like you are …"
Situ Xiu naturally had the strength, but he indeed didn't take Luo Zheng as an opponent, so he didn't even use his weapon. He only wanted to tear Luo Zheng apart with his ferocious strength.
Luo Zheng didn't have any other intentions. He also didn't use his weapon.
Since the Power Divine Path and the Power of Shen Jun could be compatible, the moment Situ Xiu rushed towards him, he also mobilized the Power Origin and fused it with the Power of Shen Jun from the Nirvana Realm Token to counterattack.
Situ Xiu's body was thick and tough, so he wasn't injured. Even so, his fists were trembling non-stop. An unprecedented feeling of suffocation attacked his heart.
In his eyes, Luo Zheng was just an ant that he could casually crush. He clearly wanted to crush him to death just now …
But the strength that this guy exploded with completely exceeded Situ Xiu's expectations!
That guy's strength was probably 20 Shen Jun! How was this possible! It didn't make sense at all!
"Your strength … How did you do it?" Situ Xiu asked with a serious expression.
"There are some secrets that I can only tell the dead. You want to know? Give me your life in exchange, "Luo Zheng said indifferently.
In less than ten breaths of time, the two's identities seemed to have been completely switched.
Even the alliance masters were looking at Luo Zheng with serious expressions. Seeing Luo Zheng's indifferent expression, their hearts were pounding.
This situation didn't seem right no matter how they looked at it.
A little guy in the First Heaven had such a Power of Shen Jun and unlimited potential. According to the rules of Dragon City, he should have been sent to the Heavenly Palace long ago …
Unless this Luo Zheng had absolute confidence in winning, how could he be so calm?
Alliance Master Tong and the others looked at each other. They even wanted to remind Situ Xiu.
Looking at the situation, Luo Zheng obviously provoked Situ Xiu on purpose. This guy probably had other motives. But Situ Xiu couldn't possibly listen to them now.
"Arrogant brat, I was just careless!"
Situ Xiu was extremely prideful. Today, he actually suffered a small loss at the hands of a mere Banner Lord. This was a huge loss of face.

Chapter 2735
Heart Flow Sword
After half a year of bitter cultivation in Dragon City, Luo Zheng wanted to test his strength.
Since Situ Xiu took the initiative to challenge him, Luo Zheng didn't want to waste this opportunity.
Situ Xiu held the Wild Snow Sword with both hands, and his ferocious True Intent aura began to surge. His eyes were like a wild beast waiting to pounce.

Next were Alliance Master Tong, Alliance Master Yu, and the others. This time, they had successfully passed the test and joined Tai Yiwei. Everyone said that Alliance Master Liu was the one who everyone wanted. If the number one alliance master couldn't join Tai Yiwei, then no one else could.
Situ Xiu had always been the weakest among the alliance masters. Everyone respected him not because of his strength, but because of the person behind him.
Now, Alliance Master Liu realized that he had really underestimated Situ Xiu!
"Alliance Master Situ hid his strength so well …"

Alliance Master Tong and the others had bitter smiles on their faces. To think that they had always looked down on this person …
Facing Situ Xiu's rising aura, Luo Zheng's expression gradually became serious.
He didn't know much about the alliance master, but he was roughly at the level of a one-star Tai Yiwei, and Tai Yi Heaven Palace was a major force on par with the You Xiong Clan. He judged that this person should be at the level of a one-star Xuan Yuan Guard, which was similar to Huyan and Pu Zhe.
However, the aura that Situ Xiu displayed now was somewhat beyond his expectations.
"Since you're deliberately provoking me, then I'll do as you wish …"
Situ Xiu wasn't as stupid as he looked. He obviously felt that Luo Zheng was deliberately provoking him.
However, there was one thing that Situ Xiu couldn't understand. Even if this Luo Zheng was born with divine strength, would a guy at the First Celestial Layer really have the guts to challenge him?
"No matter what you're relying on, there's no room for regret now." Situ Xiu's expression was ferocious.
After saying that, he suddenly took a step forward.
The soft layer of soil cracked and collapsed.
However, he still maintained his standing posture, as if his entire person had stopped moving.
Everyone was silent. Some of the more cowardly ones quietly retreated. Although they had already retreated to a considerable distance, when this kind of elite made a move, the earth would shake and the mountains would shake. It would be an injustice if they were accidentally injured.
"Shua!"
In the next breath, Situ Xiu had already disappeared from where he stood and charged towards Luo Zheng.
From slow to fast, there was almost no time to connect. This method was very sudden, swift, and impossible to guard against!
Even the Alliance Masters didn't expect this to happen so suddenly …
The only one who reacted was Luo Zheng.
Although Luo Zheng had been irritating Situ Xiu with his words, it didn't mean that he looked down on him.
In the blink of an eye, the Wild Snow Sword was already slashing towards his head.
There was nothing special about this unrestrained sword technique, but it was this ordinary sword that gave Luo Zheng a misconception.
He had no way to retreat and could only take it head-on!
No matter what method he used to dodge, he would be cleaved in two by this sword.
"This sword technique …"
A strange glint flashed through Luo Zheng's mind.
"It's the Heartflow Sword!"
"Alliance Master Situ can actually cultivate the Heartflow Sword!"
"With such talent, why is he holed up in Dragon City?"
After seeing Situ Xiu's sword technique, many of the Alliance Masters were even more baffled.
Luo Zheng had come into contact with the Heartflow Sword before. On the third floor of the True Intent Sword Pavilion, the Heartflow Sword was located in the third-to-last pillar of light and was known as the Heartflow Sword Codex.
After hearing the pavilion guardian's introduction, Luo Zheng became somewhat interested in the Heartflow Sword Codex.
It was said that this sword could read the opponent's state of mind through subtle changes and make perfect judgments. Not only that, once the user entered the state of mind, they would be able to do as they pleased. Even if they were facing an extremely powerful opponent, they would still be able to make the best decision.
The Heartflow Sword was actually a major school of the Greater One Heavenly Palace, but the Heartflow Sword Codex was the most powerful sword codex in this school.
However, the pavilion guardian also said that the threshold to cultivate the Heartflow Sword Codex was very high. One couldn't cultivate just one sword codex and had to enter the Heartflow Pagoda on the upper level of the Heavenly Palace to comprehend it. At that time, Luo Zheng felt that it wasn't suitable, so he skipped over this supreme sword codex.
When Luo Zheng faced Situ Xiu's sword, he finally understood the feeling of the Heartflow Sword …
It couldn't be dodged, but it also couldn't be avoided.
One sword was enough to force the opponent into a desperate situation!
As expected of a supreme sword codex!
Any other alliance head would be in dire straits, but Luo Zheng might not be in such a predicament.
Even if Situ Xiu had boundless strength, Luo Zheng still didn't need to dodge …
"Triple Burying Sword!"
After extracting the power of the demonic eye, the Snow Sword in Luo Zheng's hand emitted strands of mixed colors. Three strands of Dao Fusion energy coexisted and shrouded the surface of the Snow Sword.
Then, the two swords clashed together …
"Boom!"
When the Triple Burying Sword exploded, it released a dazzling white light that once again enveloped the two of them.
When he felt the power erupting from Luo Zheng's sword, Situ Xiu's face twitched slightly. The strength of 20 Shen Jun just now wasn't this kid's limit. The power of this kid's sword move was even greater!
However, once the Heartflow Sword was activated, Situ Xiu himself also fell into a state of Heartflow. No matter how his emotions fluctuated, these fluctuations would be quickly smoothed out. Like a puppet, he would attack again until he killed the opponent!
Therefore, the moment Situ Xiu was repelled by Luo Zheng's Triple Burying Sword, he turned around and slashed again. This slash also contained that strange feeling, making it impossible for Luo Zheng to dodge.
Before the light of the first Triple Burying Sword dissipated, Situ Xiu's second slash followed.
"Boom …"
"Boom …"
"Boom …"
The people in the distance only saw huge balls of light shining one after another on the plains of Dragon City, making deafening sounds like thunder.
The people nearby were either lying on the ground or staring in shock.
They could only see two faint shadows crossing each other before the light completely dissipated. As for the situation of the battle, they still couldn't tell.
"Alliance Leader Situ is so strong!"
"Everyone says he's a fake alliance leader. Where did this rumor come from? I think he's even stronger than Alliance Leader Liu … "
"Hey, don't forget … who's Alliance Leader Situ's opponent?"
While admiring Alliance Leader Situ, these people suddenly realized that they seemed to be paying attention to the wrong person.
The one fighting with Situ Xiu in the white ball of light was Luo Zheng, who had just reached the Nirvana Realm not long ago. Before yesterday, this kid wasn't even at the Nirvana Realm!
He had just reached the Nirvana Realm, but he could already fight with such a strong Situ Xiu …

A glance.

The Heavenly Palace wasn't stupid and Dragon City wasn't just for show.

The Solitary Sword Sect was particular about following one's heart. Everyone could comprehend the most suitable divine ability for themselves in the Heart Flow Pagoda.

Faced with the ice sword shadows that were closing in on him, Luo Zheng's figure became unusually agile as he shuttled back and forth.

These ice sword shadows refracted with each other. When Situ Xiu entered, thousands of images were projected. It was impossible to distinguish which was real and which was fake.
The ice prison was only a method to trap the enemy. Situ Xiu himself was the greatest killing move!

Fine cracks also appeared on the surface of the Frostthorn Jadeskin floating above his head.
The Paramita Token also had a limit. After excessive extraction of power, it would require a large amount of Shen Gems to make up for it!
In the Fifth Heaven, even the most ordinary Paramita Token could reach 15 Shen Jun of strength. If his Frost Thorn Jade Bone was fully squeezed out, it could reach 40 Shen Jun of strength! Coupled with his Heart Sword, the power of this sword was extremely terrifying.
"Swish —"
Situ Xiu entered the state of Heart Sword again. Under the cover of the ice sword shadows, he charged towards Luo Zheng.
He had overdrawn all his strength for this sword. He wanted to cut Luo Zheng into pieces.
"This sword is very troublesome!"
Seeing the fat figure in the ice sword shadows, Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
If it was purely a competition of Shen Jun of strength, Luo Zheng would not be weaker than this person. However, in the ice prison, he could not distinguish the real person.
If he could not find the real person, the other party had the ability to kill him.
Seeing that those figures were getting closer and closer to him, Luo Zheng's right eye narrowed slightly. A faint red glow was brewing within …
When Situ Xiu saw Luo Zheng's right eye close, his heart skipped a beat. For some reason, he faintly felt that there was a great threat hidden in that eye.
However, since he had already used Heart Sword, he naturally had no intention of retreating.
"Clang …"
All the ice sword shadows shattered. At the same time, Situ Xiu rushed out and slashed at a strange angle with his Wild Snow Sword.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's right eye suddenly opened. A demonic red glow was released.
When Situ Xiu saw this glow, his entire body trembled.

Heart Killing Sword
Luo Zhengdong didn't use this devil eye to take advantage of the situation. He only wanted to break the illusion of the Sword Prison.

When Situ Xiu fell into the state of mind, he affected not only himself but also his opponent.

When cultivating the Fusion God Sword Technique, Luo Zheng often sighed. Donghuang was indeed a genius.

Guarding Jade Cloud City was already a five-star mission. This meant that Jade Cloud City itself was in danger!
"You still plan to go?" Luo Zheng asked.
"I'll go!" Su Kuan instantly made up his mind.
Luo Zheng sized up Su Kuan and asked, "This Jade Cloud City has some relationship with you, right?"
Su Kuan did not answer.
Luo Zheng shrugged. Since Su Kuan was unwilling to say, he did not pursue the matter.
A huge transportation array was built in the north corner of Dragon City. After showing the token, the guards allowed the two to enter.
The long-distance transportation in the mother world was somewhat different from that in the divine realm.
In the divine realm, as long as one's cultivation reached a certain level, they could travel freely in the entire divine realm. However, in the mother world, each prefecture was a different world. When one reached the border of a large prefecture, one would need to leave the transportation array and cross the barrier between the prefectures before being transported again. It was quite tedious.
Luo Zheng and Su Kuan headed north. After passing the Center God Province, they arrived at the North Qi Province and entered the Shan Ming Province.
Along the way, it was the homeland of the human race. There were no great demons invading. The human race had built countless cities, forces, and sects.
Seeing these large prefectures, Luo Zheng sighed in his heart.
The Jiuli race used to be the orthodox human race, just like the Heavenly Palace. However, those great rivers and mountains were destroyed just like that. Moreover, it was the human race that killed each other. Thinking of this, Luo Zheng also felt a faint burden in his heart.
Reviving the Jiuli race was not something that could be solved in a short period of time.
"Guanshan Province is next door. When the time comes, Tai Yiwei will take you away." The guard guarding the transportation array looked at Luo Zheng's token and chose to let them pass. At the same time, he warned, "Guanshan Province is much more chaotic than here. You have to be careful."
Luo Zheng and Su Kuan were not the only ones who passed through the transportation array. There were many people behind them.
Among them, a considerable number came from the sects under the Heavenly Palace.
Qiu Yin River had set up many sects in other prefectures. In the large prefectures controlled by the Heavenly Palace, there were tens of thousands of sects standing tall!
The Dao Sword Palace set up the "Tian Luo Guard". These sects also had various "guards", such as the Qing Ping Guard, the God Guarding Guard, the Deep Sea Guard, and so on. Luo Zheng also met many of them along the way.
Of course, the value of these "guards" naturally could not be compared with Tai Yiwei. These forces themselves were imitating Tai Yiwei.
These sects also had a large number of people who needed to gain experience. The sects themselves also needed to prove themselves to the Heavenly Palace. They would also send experts to assist the Heavenly Palace.
Now that Guanshan Province was in trouble, naturally there would be a large number of reinforcements coming here.
When these "guards" saw the token in Luo Zheng's hand, there was a faint look of envy.
Although Luo Zheng was not Tai Yiwei, he was after all a person from Dragon City. Only people from there were qualified to enter Tai Yiwei, representing the orthodox Heavenly Palace.
"Thank you for the reminder." Luo Zheng took back the token. After the guards checked it, the others also followed behind Luo Zheng and boarded a giant airship. This giant airship would send everyone through the space barrier.
There were about a thousand people on the airship. Some were worried about the future, and some were very excited. The airship was noisy.
Luo Zheng and Su Kuan leaned at the back, not saying a word.
Half an hour later, the airship passed through the space barrier.
Before everyone could see the scenery of Guanshan Province, they saw a small black dot in the distance flying over.
The giant airship's space barrier rippled and was instantly penetrated by this black dot.
"Pata …"
The black dot had landed in the center of the airship.
Luo Zheng glanced at the black dot and saw that it was a black three-legged bird.
This bird was pitch-black all over. Only its three legs shone like pure gold.
Luo Zheng observed and felt that the energy in this bird was very violent. It was very similar to the moment when the energy of the Dao Fusion exploded. His face suddenly changed and he cried out in alarm, "Be careful!"

No way to retreat

When two alien races fought, it was rare for them to use the Heaven and Earth Cage. This was because the cage not only trapped the opponent, but also their own people.
However, the cage could greatly shorten the duration of the battle. Both sides would not be able to use Greater Teleportation to fly around, so the battle would become very intense.
Jinwu's use of the Heaven and Earth Cage could only mean that they were determined to obtain Guanshan State.

Looking at the dark clouds in the sky that were getting lower and lower, Su Kuan's mouth was full of bitterness. He had just arrived in Guanshan Province, and he was going to give up his life here?

Faced with this situation, Luo Zheng couldn't give a second answer.

Looking at the miserable state of these people, Luo Zheng's eyes twitched slightly.

Luo Zheng's eyes were like torches. With a flip, the long sword in his hand had already swept out a white light!
"Nine Diffraction Flashes!"
The Nine Flash Diffraction Sword Technique was a sword technique of its own. It pursued extreme speed.
The nine sword gleams did not spread out like a fan. Instead, they were like nine cold stars, sprinkling a field of stars above his head. They were like meteors that streaked across the sky, fleeting and disappearing.
"Pu pu pu …"
Those strange black birds that were flying towards Luo Zheng were instantly cut in half!
While killing these strange black birds, Luo Zheng's entire body suddenly sank down.
The power of one strange black bird's explosion was probably hundreds of Godly Jun! With so many strange black birds exploding, wouldn't it be enough to destroy the world? This was what Luo Zheng was worried about.
He never expected that those strange black birds that were cut in half would just fall down. They did not explode like Luo Zheng imagined!

Just kill them before they get close. If you fight and retreat, you will still have a chance to survive!"
The power of these strange black birds' explosion was too shocking. No one could withstand it. They all ran away in fear. Their speed was not as fast as the strange black birds. They just had to wait for these strange black birds to explode one by one. Then, all of them would probably die here.
Luo Zheng told everyone about his discovery. Suddenly, everyone was excited. Maybe they could still fight for a way to survive!
"Whoosh —"
While flying forward, these thousands of people also performed their own supernatural powers.
The power of the strange black birds' explosion was shocking, but their defense was extremely weak. Those who had entered the Nirvana Realm could easily kill these strange black birds by fusing with any token.
"Sword of Wind and Rain!"
"Sword of Huaitian!"
"…"
These people belonged to the Heavenly Palace. Most of them were sword cultivators.
Countless sword gleams rushed towards the sky. All the strange birds that came into contact with them were killed. Black feathers flew everywhere, and limbs sputtered in all directions. They fell down like raindrops.
Just like Luo Zheng's situation, these strange birds needed a long time to accumulate energy. After being killed, they could not explode. Naturally, the threat was greatly reduced.
Even so, the number of strange birds was too great.
A considerable number of strange birds swept in front of the team and covered them like a pot lid. They actually formed an encirclement!
"Boom!"
Dozens of strange black birds landed in advance and exploded again. The spreading energy flow once again engulfed the dozens of people in front of them.
The energy flow produced by the explosion of each strange black bird was as high as hundreds of Godly Jun. It was about twice as strong as Situ Xiu's full-powered attack. All the Nirvana Realm cultivators who were engulfed in it died.
Even Luo Zheng was greatly shocked when he felt the power of this explosion!
"Descend!"
Luo Zheng ordered. Then, he and Su Kuan descended to a height of 300 feet.
When the others heard Luo Zheng's words, they immediately understood what he meant. They also descended.
After suddenly descending, they won some time for everyone. However, after a few breaths, the "dark clouds" above their heads also sank!
"Descend again!"
Luo Zheng gritted his teeth and said helplessly.
He led everyone to descend to a certain height. The countless strange black birds also followed.

Mountain River Fortress

The large dark cloud above their heads began to "collapse", and the densely packed black birds fainted one by one and fell from the sky.

Not only did their flying speed decrease greatly, but they also stumbled along the way. They could not even tell the direction and flew randomly.

The people floating in the air looked at each other, and their faces showed joy.

"What was that sound just now?"
Su Kuan rubbed his ears. He felt that he could not hear clearly.
"I don't know, but it must have a miraculous effect on these black birds," said Luo Zheng.
Birds were extremely sensitive to sound, and some special sounds could indeed make them faint. The people of the Guanshan State had probably done everything they could to fight against the Jinwu.
"Let's go quickly …"
"If these strange birds wake up, I won't be able to escape death."
After experiencing this disaster, they just wanted to get away from this place.
However, at this moment, a few white boats suddenly appeared in the distance.
The flying boats flew from afar at an extremely fast speed. About ten people alighted from the boats. These people were dressed in martial attire and exuded a cold, murderous aura.
Feeling the murderous aura from these people, Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. Only people who killed all day could give off such a feeling.
They did not look at Luo Zheng and the others. They took out palm-sized beads from their backs and threw these beads into the small mountain of strange birds.
"Whoosh!"
The fainted black birds immediately turned into a sea of fire. In the blink of an eye, hundreds of thousands of black birds were burned to ashes.
"The Mountain River Fortress is in front. Follow us!"
After one of them said this, they returned to their respective flying boats and sped forward. They did not seem to have any intention of giving them a ride.
Everyone followed closely behind him with sullen expressions on their faces.
They had traveled thousands of miles to the Guanshan State to fight for the human race. Almost half of them had died in the hands of these black birds, but there was not even a word of consolation.
Luo Zheng's expression remained unchanged.
No matter what, they were still alive.
However, judging from the situation, the Guanshan State was much more troublesome than he had imagined.
Although Su Kuan had just walked through the gates of hell, he still remained calm. He lowered his head and seemed to be thinking about something.
After flying for 500 miles, a majestic fortress appeared in front of them.
However, the most attractive thing was not the fortress itself, but the nine giant swords behind the fortress!

Chapter 2744
Soul Catcher Golden Crow
The Mountain River Fortress was the rear of the Human Race in Guanshan Province, so it was heavily guarded.
When the five hundred or so people entered the fortress, they were stopped by a dozen or so armored guards.
The leader of the armored guards pointed at the crowd and said coldly: "All of you, follow me!"
Under the lead of this armored guard, the five hundred or so people were arranged in a spacious drill ground.
"All of you wait here. You are not allowed to cross the red line. Anyone who does will be killed without question!" the armored guard said sternly.
These five hundred or so people had gone through life and death situations to reach the Mountain River Fortress. They didn't expect to be treated like this, so they were somewhat indignant.
"Why?"
"We are working for the Human Race! Are you treating us like prisoners? "
"This is an insult!"
"You want to stop us with just a red line?"
The crowd said indignantly.
Luo Zheng stood in the group and looked at the red line with a strange expression.
It was indeed too much to treat his colleagues like this.
However, the armed guard did not pay any attention to him. He crossed the red line and left.
This action caused the five hundred plus people to become even more restless. Two of them even ignored the guard's words and strode out of the red line with large strides.
Just as these two people crossed the red line, a towering sword erected in the distance suddenly emitted a faint luster, and an invisible force came straight toward them.
This force was so oppressive that it almost suffocated everyone.
Luo Zheng's expression changed, and he retreated like a conditioned reflex. But he remembered the armored guard's warning and only approached the edge of the red line, not leaving it.
"Pu!"
"Pu!"
The two people who had stepped out of the red line were directly killed by an invisible sword ray. Their entire bodies were cut into several pieces.
What was even stranger was that although these two people were killed, not a single drop of blood flowed out!
While everyone was still in a state of shock, a pair of wings extended out from the two men's heads. A three-legged black bird the size of a fist drilled out from the corpses of the two men. It flapped its wings as if it wanted to fly away.
"Pu pu!"
Without any suspense, the invisible force descended once again, and the two little black birds were also chopped into pieces.
"What's going on?!"
"When did these birds get into their heads?!"
"Don't tell me I'm like this too!"
When everyone saw this scene, they were instantly terrified. They were afraid that this kind of bird would drill into their heads as well. Each and every one of them was extremely nervous.
Luo Zheng glanced at Su Kuan subconsciously. Su Kuan smiled at Luo Zheng and said in a relaxed manner, "I'm fine …"
"Yes, those black birds must have drilled in when they covered the ground. It seems that there are quite a lot of them," Luo Zheng said with a nod.
He had a strange feeling just now.
The rules of the Mountain River Fortress were strict, and routine inspections were common. However, there were quite a few people in the crowd who were clamoring, encouraging everyone to walk out of the red line. This was a typical case of seeking one's own death.
However, most of them were still rational. No one dared to cross the red line and become cannon fodder.
Thus, these birds that had burrowed into the brain could only use two people to test the waters first. Naturally, the outcome was what everyone had seen.
The armed guard who had just left turned back. When he saw the broken corpses outside the red line, he sneered and said loudly, "These damn birds have done this more than once or twice. For the sake of safety, I can only inconvenience you brothers!"

Some people in the crowd were a little uneasy. They looked around in panic.

Their bodies were cut into pieces, but there was no blood dripping. These people were also parasitized by the strange birds.
The other dozens of people inside the red line were as anxious as ants on a hot pan. They ran here and there, and their mouths made strange "ga ga" sounds.

As the red light in the Drill Ground became brighter and brighter, those people suddenly screamed strangely and fell to the ground, motionless …

"When did these strange birds burrow into their brains? Why didn't they notice it at all?" someone asked.
The armed guard grabbed one of the bird corpses and said, "There are about 50 branches in the Jinwu. Each of them has different talents. The first group that attacked you can explode is called the Exploding Golden Crow, and this one is called the Soul-taming Golden Crow … This little thing has brought us a lot of trouble!"
The Soul-taming Golden Crow could be a parasite without anyone noticing. Not only could it seize all the memories of the other party, but it could also devour the other party's soul and blood.
13 years ago, a Soul-taming Golden Crow lurked in the body of a five-star Tai Yiwei and deliberately issued a wrong command. It directly killed more than 300 Tai Yiwei and indirectly caused an immeasurable loss.
Since then, the Tai Yiwei had been highly vigilant against the Soul-taming Golden Crow. People like Luo Zheng who entered the Mountain River Fortress had to be routinely checked.
"You can report over there." The armed guard pointed to the rear.
Under the guidance of the armed guard, Luo Zheng and the others moved to another part of the fortress. After a round of registration, the people in the fortress checked the information in their tokens one by one.
"You are from Dragon City?" The young man in charge of registration looked at Luo Zheng curiously.
Luo Zheng nodded. "Yes, what's the matter?"
"Nothing." The young man smiled and said, "You can wait over there. You are only assisting in the mission of Jade Cloud City. When the time comes, there will be Tai Yiwei going with you. They will take you away."
The defense of Jade Cloud City mainly relied on Tai Yiwei. Missions that involved Tai Yiwei directly were very difficult. Seeing that Luo Zheng had just entered the Nirvana Realm, the young man naturally felt strange.
Guan Shan Prefecture was not Dragon City. Although the competition in Dragon City was cruel, it was like playing house compared to Guan Shan Prefecture. The bloodshed here was not something that Dragon City could compare to. He could even imagine the scene where Luo Zheng and Su Kuan were scared witless.
Other than Luo Zheng and Su Kuan, there were seven or eight other people waiting at the side.
The mission issued by Tai Yiwei was not only for Dragon City, but also for all the forces under the Heavenly Palace. Those seven or eight people had obviously accepted the same mission as Luo Zheng.
After waiting for about an hour, a young man in military uniform walked in quickly.
The young man in military uniform had a token hanging on his waist with the word "Tai Yiwei" written on it. He was a Tai Yiwei.

Make things difficult

Other than those Tai Yiwei, the cultivation of the others was at the third or fourth level of the Nirvana Realm.
Luo Zheng and Su Kuan were especially eye-catching in the crowd.

With the Heaven and Earth Cage binding them, even the flying boat could not enter the space tunnel. They could only drive the flying boat to maintain a high speed.

However, during the flight, they could be attacked by the Jinwu at any time. No one dared to use this time to enter the Nirvana Realm to cultivate. Everyone was fully focused to deal with the danger that could appear at any time.

What appeared in front of everyone was a dilapidated city.
At a glance, the entire city was in ruins. Many buildings had collapsed and turned into ruins. Not far away, there were wisps of black smoke rising.

Luo Zheng had long seen that Su Kuan had a connection with Jade Cloud City. However, if Su Kuan did not take the initiative to say it, Luo Zheng would not expose him.
As the flying boat slowly descended, the sound of sobbing entered everyone's ears.

The originally prosperous Jade Cloud City was now a dangerous city. These people naturally fought to be the first to leave.
These Tai Yiwei did not even look at them. They each led their own people and left separately.

"Caw, caw …"
Three to five black Golden Crows hovered in the air. From time to time, they would descend and peck the corpses with their beaks.

"After Jinwu attacked, the alert day was lifted. You don't have to stay here for the time being. You can receive ten Divine Gems with the tokens that I gave you." Lin Hui turned and left after he finished speaking.
After such a large-scale attack, Jinwu would not launch a second attack. However, Lu Qi and the others did not intend to leave. They chose to sit down and cultivate.
Hearing that they could move freely, Su Kuan's heart skipped a beat. He looked at Luo Zheng and said, "Master Banner Leader, I want to take a look!"
Perhaps Su Youxue was already dead. However, before he saw her again, Su Kuan would not give up.
Luo Zheng patted Su Kuan's shoulder and said, "I'll go with you!"
The two of them passed through the south of the city. Along the way, they saw dilapidated buildings.
Messy feathers and bird corpses could be seen everywhere on the ground. Some of the Golden Crows were red and taller than a person. These three-legged Golden Crows were not weak. They were probably the main force of Jinwu.
Fortunately, these red Golden Crows did not attack the south of the city last night.
Amidst the broken tiles and ruined walls, the sobbing sounds of women and children could be heard. Although the Golden Crow Clan had targeted the four great formations, Jade Cloud City had still suffered considerable losses. Many people had died from an undeserved calamity.
Luo Zheng furrowed his brows when he saw the miserable scene.
A war between races was even more terrifying than a war between humans. There was no mercy to speak of between two different races. There was only a battle to the death.
After arriving in the city, several light green lights suddenly appeared in front of them. These lights contained a powerful energy aura.

Chapter 2750
Family Su's Fifth Uncle
The two of them walked forward for a while, and a wall that was more than ten feet high appeared in front of them.
Around the wall, Tai Yiwei dressed in martial attire stood erect. Their expressions were unusually solemn as they patrolled back and forth.
Just as Su Kuan was about to approach, one of the Tai Yiwei stepped forward and blocked the two of them.
"This is an important area protected by formations. Ordinary people are not allowed to approach," the Tai Yiwei said in a stiff manner.
Su Kuan could only retreat with Luo Zheng.
The two of them circled around the wall, and no matter which direction they went, they were tightly blocked by the wall.
When those Tai Yiwei weren't paying attention, Su Kuan even tried to use his Divine Sense to probe. However, just as his Divine Sense was about to climb over the wall, a yellow light screen flashed on the surface of the wall, blocking his Divine Sense outside. This action even attracted the scolding of one of the Tai Yiwei.
"Let's go take a look at other places …" Su Kuan shrugged his shoulders.
The degree of damage to the buildings in the center of Jade Cloud City was relatively light. Some of the buildings were temporarily requisitioned for the use of Jade Cloud City's soldiers.
Some people even opened a market square here to trade some necessities.
The two of them strolled around aimlessly. Most of the things they bought in the market square were long swords of various standards, as well as scattered runes and some unidentifiable ores.
Su Kuan was able to identify them one by one. However, the quality of these things were not presentable, so Luo Zheng naturally had no interest in buying them.
However, when they turned a corner, they heard a shout coming from ahead.
"The World's Most Famous Swords Come From Family Su. Everyone, don't miss this opportunity …"
Not far away, a bar-browed middle-aged man had three long swords stuck in front of him. A layer of faint green light floated on the surface of the three long swords, and a luxurious gem was embedded in each of the swords. At first glance, the three swords were all of extraordinary quality. They were peerless swords!
When Su Kuan saw this middle-aged man, his eyes suddenly lit up.
"You know him?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes, that's …" Su Kuan stammered, "My fifth uncle …"
Luo Zheng said with a smile, "Since your Fifth Uncle is here, we can ask him for some information. As an elder of the Family Su, he should know something, right?"
Seeing Su Kuan's expression, Luo Zheng felt a bit strange.
"This Fifth Uncle is also unreliable in my Family Su …" Su Kuan gritted his teeth and said, "Look at those broken swords he sold …"
Su Kuan's Fifth Uncle was called Su Youqi. He was talented since he was a child, but he liked to play tricks and often caused trouble. The whole Family Su was worried about him.
After Su Youqi became an adult, he didn't make any progress. When he ran out of divine crystals, he would resort to swindling others.
Family Su's miraculous skill in refining tools had been completely deceived by him.
The three swords in front of him were all ordinary items. They couldn't even be classified as Third-Rate Mystique Venerable Level Dao Treasures.
However, when he was refining them, he would inject all sorts of unknown runes and some unpopular gadgets into the swords. He would forcefully elevate these ordinary items to a higher level.
Once, he had sold an unclassified longsword for the price of a First-Rate Mystique Venerable Level Dao Treasure. It was worth more than ten million divine crystals. Moreover, he had sold it to a young master of a big family in the Center God Province.
This kind of thing could be deceived for a while, but it couldn't be deceived forever.
When that young master took out that longsword to show off, he was seen through immediately. Therefore, he brought the elders of the family to the Family Su to denounce him.
In the end, the Family Su could only compensate him …
As for Su Youqi's divine crystals, he had already lost them all.
Although Su Kuan looked down on Fifth Uncle, after wandering around Jade Cloud City for such a long time, he really did find someone from the Family Su. Now, he could only count on him.
Su Kuan and Luo Zheng walked forward. Fifth Uncle didn't even look to see who it was. He began to peddle fluently, "My two friends, the Family Su's famous swords are famous in the Center God Province. These three longswords are the painstaking work of the head of the Family Su. To tell you the truth …"
Before Fifth Uncle could finish, Su Kuan interrupted him. "Fifth Uncle, it's me!"
Only then did Su Youqi raise his head to size up Su Kuan. His face revealed a shocked expression. "Su Kuan? Why are you in Jade Cloud City? Aren't you in Dragon City? "
Su Kuan laughed dryly. "I can also come out to earn merits in Dragon City."
"Come to Jade Cloud City to earn merits? Aren't you afraid of death? "Su Youqi's expression became serious.
He had stayed in Jade Cloud City for quite some time. After all, he came with the Family Su and had Tai Yiwei's strict protection.
However, the soldiers guarding Jade Cloud City had all died tragically. Su Kuan was being reckless!
Back then, Su Kuan's father didn't allow Su Kuan to go to Dragon City. Because of this, the father and son had a huge dispute. If his father knew about this, he would probably fly into a rage.
Su Kuan was very respectful towards the elders of the Family Su. He wasn't afraid of this Fifth Uncle.
He avoided this topic and stared at Fifth Uncle. "Fifth Uncle, where is Youxue?"
"Why are you asking this?" Su Youqi waved his hand. "I don't know anything. Don't block me from selling!"
He didn't even look at Su Kuan. He actually continued to shout.
"Fifth Uncle! Fifth Uncle … "
No matter how Su Kuan called out, Fifth Uncle ignored him. It was as if Su Kuan was air.
Just as Su Kuan was feeling disappointed, a cold voice suddenly sounded. "Old man! What kind of trash did you sell me yesterday?! "
Not far away, three Tai Yiwei dressed in martial attire came over aggressively. The leader was a three-star Tai Yiwei.
He held a few pieces of a broken sword in his hand. Looking at the quality of the broken sword, it was obviously sold by Su Youqi.
This three-star Tai Yiwei really thought that he had bought a Mystique Venerable Grade treasure. He thought that he had gotten a great deal and flaunted it in front of his colleagues. The longsword actually broke, so he came looking for them!
When Su Youqi saw this, he hurriedly put on a smile. "My friends, let's talk nicely. I …"
"Swoosh!"
These Tai Yiwei were all hot-tempered. The three-star Tai Yiwei grabbed the longsword in front of Su Youqi and slashed it. A sword mark that was several feet deep slashed across his body. Then, the extraordinary longsword in his hand suddenly broke into several pieces.
"Using this kind of imitation to trick our Old Man Fan …"
"You've got guts!"
The leader Tai Yiwei threw the broken sword in front of Su Youqi and sneered at the same time. "I know you're from the Family Su. I won't make things difficult for you. Spit out the 300,000 Divinity Crystals that I paid you yesterday."
It was normal for someone to pay for the counterfeit goods. However, Su Youqi smiled bitterly. "I've already lost all the Divinity Crystals from yesterday. I really don't have any money on hand!"
"Then what do you think we should do?" The three-star Tai Yiwei stared at Su Youqi and said, "Should I go to the Family Su directly, or do you want to gather the money now?"
The bitter smile on Su Youqi's face deepened. With his reputation, how could he gather a few hundred thousand Divinity Crystals? If the Family Su found out, it would be even more troublesome …
Under the stares of the Tai Yiwei, Su Youqi became anxious. He regretted that he should have hidden after he succeeded yesterday. He did not think that he would be exposed so soon.
At this moment, Luo Zheng and Su Kuan looked at each other. Luo Zheng understood Su Kuan's thoughts and nodded at him.

Chapter 2751
Translator: Nyoi-Bo Studio Editor: Nyoi-Bo Studio
Seal Stone Sign
Su Youqi was still thinking about how to get away with this, but he couldn't take out the godly crystals right now.
When he heard Su Kuan's words, he was surprised. "Su Kuan … where did you get the godly crystals?"
Fifth Uncle knew that Su Kuan had cut off all his sources of income after fighting with his father.
"Don't worry about that. I only have one request. Tell me about Su Youxue's situation," Su Kuan said coldly. "If you don't agree, these Tai Yiwei brothers won't be easy to talk to."
The Tai Yiwei brothers sneered. If Su Youqi didn't spit out the godly crystals today, he would lose a layer of skin.
Su Youqi hesitated for a moment, but he realized that this was the only thing he could do, so he nodded.
However, he was still doubtful about whether Su Kuan could take out a few hundred thousand godly crystals.

Luo Zheng flipped his hand, and his Sumeru Ring flashed. A pile of godly crystals appeared in front of everyone.
The Tai Yiwei brothers didn't hesitate. They took the godly crystals and left.
After the crisis was resolved, Su Youqi's expression returned to normal.
Su Kuan obviously didn't plan to let Fifth Uncle go. He stared at him and asked, "Youxue, is she okay?"
Su Youqi hesitated for a moment, then said calmly, "Her bloodline has been overly squeezed. She hasn't woken up in the past few days …"
"They really did that." Su Kuan's expression turned cold. When he thought of Su Youxue's pale face, his heart ached.
"I'm afraid my brothers won't let her go until they squeeze her to death." Su Youqi also let out a long sigh.
The Su Family's Weapon Refiners were all very cold-blooded. In order to refine a Divine Weapon, they were willing to pay any price. Although Fifth Uncle was a good-for-nothing, he was also an exception in this aspect. He did not approve of the Su Family's actions.
"Can't it be delayed for a few days?" Su Kuan asked.
If the bloodline was completely drained, the body would eventually wither.
If given enough time, Su Youxue could take more Life Blood Spirit Pills, it could be remedied.
"Jinwu's army is pressing against us. Time is of the essence, and we can't move the stone away. What else can we do but sacrifice her?" Su Youqi shook his head and said.
Whatever Su Kuan could think of, Family Su could naturally think of as well.
"Let me talk to my father!" Su Kuan's eyes were resolute.

"Take me there! Otherwise, pay me back three hundred thousand Divinity crystals! "
Su Kuan's words made Su Youqi choke. Where would he get the divine crystals to return to Su Kuan?
Su Youqi thought for a while and said, "I can let you take a look, but you can only take a look. You can't make a big deal out of it. How about that?"
In the face of Fifth Uncle's condition, Su Kuan could only agree.

Three hundred thousand Divinity crystals was a small amount for Luo Zheng, but Su Kuan could not afford it in a short time.
"Don't worry about this little amount of divine crystals," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
Although Su Kuan had only joined him for a short period of time, he had put in a lot of effort in many things. Moreover, he relied on the Sword Comprehension Liquid, which was a profitable business. A few hundred thousand Divinity crystals was nothing to him. If other disciples of the academy needed divine crystals, Luo Zheng would not hesitate at all, let alone Su Kuan.
Su Kuan did not reply. He was silent all the way.
After a while, Su Youqi brought the two of them back to the city wall.
In the face of the Tai Yiwei's obstruction, Su Youqi showed a token, and the Tai Yiwei immediately let them pass.
However, before entering the gate, Luo Zheng saw a magic array formed by red lines. This magic array should be used to prevent the Soul-absorbing Golden Crows from seizing a person's shell.
After entering the high wall, Luo Zheng saw a few green light pillars erected not far away!
In the center of the light pillars was a black square stone.
Although this stone was floating in the air, at first glance, it seemed to be absolutely still. It was as if it was not floating in the air, but should have appeared in this space. It looked very magical. This should be the Sealing Stone that Su Kuan mentioned.
"After so many years, another Sealing Stone has appeared. Hehe, I'm afraid there's not much time left …" Suddenly, the voice of 9527 sounded in Luo Zheng's mind.
"What do you mean there's not much time left?" Luo Zheng asked.
However, 9527 did not answer this question. It only said, "I just don't know what kind of Nirvana Realm token is hidden in this Sealing Stone …"
"Even you are interested in this thing?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
All these years, nothing could catch the eye of 9527. It did not even show any interest in the Sword of Tengshe.
According to Su Kuan, this Sealing Stone could only be used to refine a first-rate Nirvana Realm treasure. Although it was said to be comparable to a third-rate Innate Realm treasure, Luo Zheng was skeptical of this statement.
"I'm interested in this thing, but it has nothing to do with what it can be refined. This Sealing Stone is only used to refine weapons. In fact, it's a sign," 9527 answered.
"What sign?" Luo Zheng was a little confused.
"You can think of it as a timer. It's a grain of sand that falls from the hourglass of time. It's a sign from order to chaos," 9527 continued.
"Can you explain it more clearly?"
"No."
"Okay …"
Luo Zheng shook his head. It was not the first time that 9527 was so mysterious.
The three of them walked around the huge Nine Edges Refining Array. For a while, a simple building appeared in front of them.
Su Youqi took the lead to enter and then retreated. He waved at Luo Zheng and Su Kuan and whispered at the same time, "You should leave after taking a look. If your father knows about this, you will be in trouble, and so will Fifth Uncle!"
Su Kuan and Luo Zheng also sneaked in quietly. In the corner of the room, they saw a small blood-colored Talisman Array. In the center of this Talisman Array lay a woman in white.
This woman in white had a beautiful face. She had a simple and quiet appearance. However, her face was as pale as paper, and her breath of life was very weak. It was obvious that she had lost a lot of blood.
Following a flash of crimson light from the formation, round beads of blood began to peel off from her body.
"There's snow!"
Seeing this, Su Kuan suddenly took a few steps forward and wanted to pounce.
Seeing this, Su Youqi grabbed Su Kuan and growled in a low voice, "You're crazy!"
"There's snow in this state, and you're still squeezing her blood. You're the crazy ones!" Su Kuan's eyes were red. He clenched his fist and said angrily.

Chapter 2752

Conflict in the house
Su Kuan choked at Fifth Uncle's words.
The security here was so tight. Not to mention the Family Su, there were also so many Tai Yiwei.
So what if he found Su Youxue? He couldn't even speak to her, let alone take her away.
"Both of Youxue's parents died after she was born. Fifth Uncle's heart aches for her to end up like this. What can we do?" Su Youqi sighed. "You should leave first. Yesterday, Jade Cloud City blocked another wave of Jinwu's attack. Perhaps we can take a breather …"
Ever since Su Kuan's father took over as the head of the Family Su, he pursued an iron-blooded policy. Everyone in the Family Su was willing to sacrifice themselves for the family.
However, the Family Su didn't want Su Youxue to die unless it was absolutely necessary. After all, she was the purest descendant of the Family Su.
"But …"
Su Kuan couldn't accept that there was nothing he could do now that he was already here.
Just then, the door was suddenly pushed open.
A group of three people came in.
The three of them were dressed in white. On the shoulder of their clothes was a pattern of flames and a sword. That was the Family Su's emblem.
The leader was a middle-aged man with broad eyes and long brows. His gaze was deep.
The three of them were slightly stunned when they saw someone in the building. The leader saw Su Youqi first and scolded, "Fifth Brother, what are you doing here …"
Halfway through his sentence, the middle-aged man saw Su Kuan. The shock in his eyes intensified. At the same time, a sharp glint appeared in his eyes. "Su Kuan! What are you doing here? "
The middle-aged man was Su Kuan's father, Su Yu-jin.
If it was in the past, Su Kuan would be extremely fearful and submissive when he met his father.

This father and son had originally quarreled over Su Kuan's decision to go to Dragon City. It relied on the efforts and sacrifices of thousands of Family Su members. Otherwise, who do you think you are? I'm afraid you won't even be able to enter Dragon City! "Su Yu-jin said coldly.
Over the years, the Family Su's position in the Heavenly Palace had been rising steadily. His style of doing things was indeed very important.
"Whether I can enter or not is my own business. Today, I must take Su Youxue away!" Su Kuan said loudly.
"How dare you! Don't even mention taking Su Youxue away. Since you're here, don't even think about leaving! "Su Yu-jin scolded.

Weapon Forgemasters also had requirements for their cultivation. This Su Yu-jin was even a figure of the Seventh Heaven of the Nirvana Realm!

If this slap landed on Su Kuan's body, even if he did not die, he would be crippled!

Su Yu-jin was originally in a fit of anger. He just wanted to teach Su Kuan a lesson.
He did not expect that his slap would be blocked by the young man beside Su Kuan. He was stunned.
Not only him, but Su Youqi and Su Kuan's Second and Third Uncles were also surprised.
They saw that Luo Zheng was beside Su Kuan. In terms of cultivation, Luo Zheng was weaker than Su Kuan. They thought that he was a friend that Su Kuan made in Dragon City. He was only at the First Heaven of the Nirvana Realm. Who would carefully size up Luo Zheng?
However, Luo Zheng casually blocked Su Yu-jin's slap. The meaning was different.
Not to mention the First Heaven of the Nirvana Realm, even those at the Third or Fourth Heaven of the Nirvana Realm would not be able to do it as casually as Luo Zheng.
"Who are you?! What does my Family's internal affairs have to do with you? "Su Yu-jin said coldly.
Although he was not afraid of Luo Zheng, no one would believe that someone at the First Heaven of the Nirvana Realm did not have a powerful background.
"Su Kuan is a member of your Family Su. He's also under my banner. As a Banner Master, it's my duty to protect him," Luo Zheng said seriously.
Su Yu-jin stared at Luo Zheng for a while. He smiled coldly and said, "A mere Banner Master in Dragon City dares to stop us from refining the Seal Stone. No matter which noble family you're from, I'm afraid you're courting death!"
As he spoke, Su Yu-jin's body emitted a scorching energy. His Nirvana token was a piece of Devil Fire Dragon Scale from the Seventh Heaven.
As the scorching energy dissipated, everyone felt an intense burning sensation on their faces.
Su Kuan's Second and Third Uncles also took a few steps back.
This building was originally built temporarily. How could it withstand the erosion of the scorching wave?
Following a series of popping sounds, the walls of the entire building collapsed from the heat.
Outside the building, the Tai Yiwei also felt the commotion. They originally wanted to come over to investigate, but then they saw this scene in the building.
Luo Zheng stood on the spot. His pupils contracted slightly.
Seventh Heaven of the Nirvana Realm …
Even if he went all out, he would probably only be able to barely put up a fight.
However, he accompanied Su Kuan here today and did not intend to make a move. Moreover, this person was Su Kuan's father.
At this moment, Su Kuan, who had already crossed the blood-colored talisman array, turned back and blocked in front of Luo Zheng. "Father, if you want to kill someone, then kill me. I'm not important to you anyway. Don't make things difficult for my Banner Master!"
Su Kuan was already very grateful that Luo Zheng brought him all the way to Jade Cloud City. Luo Zheng had also taken care of him along the way. How could he put Luo Zheng in danger?
Su Yu-jin stared at his son. The corners of his eyes kept twitching.
No matter how useless Su Kuan was, he could not really kill him.

Chapter 2753
A Helpless Choice
"Bang!"
The door was tightly shut.
Luo Zheng, Su Kuan, and even Su Youqi were "invited" out by Tai Yiwei.

Su Kuan stood in place, looking as if he had nothing left to live for.
