Hearing the conversation between uncle and nephew, Luo Zheng smiled and shook his head. "This matter can't be rushed. Besides, your Fifth Uncle is right. Since Su Youxue's bloodline is so rare, how can they bear to let her die?"
That blood-red formation array not only squeezed Su Youxue's bloodline, it also constantly replenished her blood. As long as it wasn't squeezed too hard, it wouldn't kill her.
Tai Yiwei and Family Su were heavily guarded here. If they took her away, not only would they not be strong enough, they wouldn't have the right to do so.
Su Kuan naturally listened to Luo Zheng, but he didn't have much hope in this matter.
Just as Su Kuan and Luo Zheng were about to leave, they suddenly heard a sharp whistling sound.
This whistling sound seemed to come from an extremely high place...
Luo Zheng looked up and saw a faint shadow appear in the sky above Biyun City.
He immediately frowned.
The entire Guanshan Province was confined by the Prison of Heaven and Earth. How could someone use Greater Teleportation?
Under Luo Zheng's gaze, he suddenly saw that the shadow gradually solidified. Two ten-zhang-wide golden wings spread out. In the center of the wings, there was actually a half-naked woman?
The woman's eyes were tightly shut as if she hadn't woken up yet. Her entire body was emitting a faint golden light.
She flapped her wings lightly, and the huge wings enveloped her entire body. Then, the golden light suddenly shot out in four directions.
"The direction of the four golden lights …" Luo Zheng's eyelids suddenly twitched.
Not only Luo Zheng, but several senior executives of Tai Yiwei, including Su Yu-jin, who had just driven Luo Zheng and Su Kuan out, were also shocked.
The direction of the four balls of golden light was the four Myriad Underworlds Sealing Sword Formations in Jade Cloud City!
"Buzz buzz …"
Every Ten Million Serene Sealing Sword Array was guarded by more than ten Tai Yiwei, and there was even a five-star Tai Yiwei among them.
When these Tai Yiwei saw the golden light heading towards them, they immediately raised the alarm.
"Woo woo woo …"
However, it took a long time for the array to merge with the Godly Crystal.
After the Prison of Heaven and Earth was released, the human race and the Jinwu were unable to use Greater Teleportation. This was also the reason why the Tai Yiwei's judgment was affected.
The four balls of golden light hovered above the four arrays. In the blink of an eye, they were ready to strike.
The winged woman standing in the center of the city softly uttered a few words.
"Holy Word, Golden Punishment Annihilation."
"Swoosh!"
The golden light poured down from the sky like a flood.
"Back off!"
When the Tai Yiwei guarding the Myriad Underworlds Sealing Sword Array felt the power of the golden light, they only had one thought in their minds, and that was to escape.
They were completely unable to resist the attack of such terrifying energy. If they stayed here, they would be buried together with the array. There was no other choice!
"Boom boom boom boom!"
Following the four violent explosions, the entire Jade Cloud City was shaking.
As the golden light poured down, the four Myriad Underworlds Sealing Sword Arrays were destroyed instantly …
In the sky, the winged woman looked down at the entire Jade Cloud City. She smiled faintly, revealing an exceptionally charming smile. As she flapped her wings, she turned into a faint shadow and disappeared into thin air.
In the face of the sudden attack, the entire Jade Cloud City was in a mess.
"The space is confined by the Prison of Heaven and Earth. Isn't it impossible to use Greater Teleportation? How did that woman do it? "Luo Zheng asked.
Su Kuan and Su Youqi were both confused. Naturally, they did not know …
They were all thinking about the same thing. Since the four Myriad Underworlds Sealing Sword Arrays were destroyed, the Jinwu would definitely not let go of this opportunity. The outcome of Jade Cloud City was imaginable.
"The Prison of Heaven and Earth is a good method. However, the True Meaning of Void Disillusionment combined with a special Nirvana Realm token naturally has a way to break through it. Moreover, that woman's actual body did not teleport over. That was just a projection," 9527 explained.
Unless it involved information from the depths of the Nirvana Realm, 9527 was usually able to answer Luo Zheng's questions.
"It's just a projection, but it has such power …" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
The power contained in those golden lights was probably the power of tens of thousands of kilograms!
Such power could be achieved by a mere projection. It was too terrifying!
Just as Luo Zheng was thinking, a loud voice suddenly rang out in Jade Cloud City. This voice directly spread throughout the entire Jade Cloud City.
"All Tai Yiwei and Tai Yiwei's subordinate soldiers, retreat to the center of Jade Cloud City!"
The soldiers in Jade Cloud City relied on the four Great Arrays. Originally, they could have guarded the Jinwu outside the city.
Now that the four Great Arrays were destroyed, the Jinwu would definitely take advantage of the situation and attack. There was no point in guarding the entire Jade Cloud City. The higher-ups of Tai Yiwei decisively gave the order for all combat forces to retreat into the city and guard the Nine Edges Refining Array.
From all directions, people were constantly gathering …
Looking at these people, Su Kuan panicked. "Banner Lord, what do we do now …"
"Let's stay here and wait," Luo Zheng replied.
…
…
Inside the Nine Edges Refining Array.
Su Yu-jin and the others naturally saw this scene.
"What do we do now?" A weapon refiner in the Heavenly Palace asked anxiously.
"There's definitely not enough time!" Su Kuan's Second Uncle looked at the sealing stone and said.
The sealing stone was extremely sturdy. Even if thousands or even tens of thousands of kilograms of force were to hit it, it would not be destroyed.

Qiu Yinhe said, "There are two ways to survive. The first is to stop the needle from piercing the chaos. The Jiuli, including the Li Mountain, and a considerable number of alien races have all chosen this Dao. This is the Dao of the End."
Luo Zheng blinked.

"What Dao did the You Xiong Clan choose?" Luo Zheng asked.
As Qiu Yinhe listened, he reached out and gently pinched the gravel on the ground. Energy flowed out from his fingertips and swallowed all the gravel. In the end, only a small stone was left. "There's only one stone left. It's clean. Isn't it not as messy?"

"The Dao of Order is to kill all the other races." Qiu Yinhe shrugged.

This was indeed a crazy plan.

Tacit Understanding

After that, he made various attempts, but still, there was no reaction.
"9527, how should I deal with this thing?" Luo Zheng asked.
It was a complete accident that the sealing stone was absorbed into his body …
Now, Luo Zheng could not do anything about it.
"I don't know … Sealing stones are usually used to refine weapons. This thing has a special characteristic. It can completely integrate with Nirvana tokens. Therefore, a piece of sealing stone is equivalent to a first-rate Nirvana treasure," 9527 answered.
The reason why Nirvana treasures and Mystique Venerable treasures had a qualitative leap was because of the difference in the integrated Nirvana tokens.
The more powerful the Nirvana token, the higher the requirement for the refining materials!
If a Mystique Venerable treasure was integrated with a Nirvana token, it would collapse during the integration process. The treasure itself could not integrate Nirvana tokens.
For example, a third-rate Nirvana treasure could not integrate Nirvana tokens from the first to the tenth level of heaven. A second-rate Nirvana treasure could integrate from the eleventh to the twentieth level of heaven. As for a first-rate Nirvana treasure, it could integrate from the twenty-first to the thirtieth level of heaven.
However, there was a detail that 9527, Su Kuan, and the rest did not mention.
Most first-rate Nirvana treasures could only integrate Nirvana tokens up to the thirtieth level of heaven, which was the Yu Sheng-tian level.
However, only sealing stones and some rare items could overcome this limitation and integrate Nirvana tokens above the thirtieth level of heaven.
"If that's the case, my body is also considered a Nirvana treasure?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked.
Luo Zheng's body was a complete anomaly.
There was once an Ancient God Body Tempering Technique that could cross the Sea of True Intent, but the Ancient God Body Tempering Technique cultivated another level of True Intent of the Dao.
This layer of True Meaning of Dao was known as the "True Meaning of Huangshen Xuan Body". Following the disappearance of the Ancient God Body Tempering Technique, the people who cultivated this layer of True Meaning of Dao naturally disappeared as well.
Luo Zheng, on the other hand, had actually blazed a new trail, relying on the calcination of the True Fire to enter the sea of True Intent.
As for the True Meaning aura emanating from his body, it did not exist in the past. Even 9527 could not tell where it came from. Whether or not he could fuse with the Nirvana Token was also unknown.
Hearing this, 9527 paused for a while and then said, "Oh right... this is interesting!"
At first, 9527 wasn't interested in Feng Shi, but now he took it seriously.
The Nirvana Realm was a complicated and mysterious world. There were a large number of Nirvana Tokens that could be fused, which could greatly increase the strength of the True Gods. However, there were also a considerable number of Nirvana Tokens that could not bear it.
For example, in the Sixth Heaven, there was a Nirvana Token called "Poison Heaven Grass". The poison contained in it was extremely ferocious, but no one dared to fuse with this Nirvana Token. After the fusion, the poison of the Nirvana Token would also destroy the user. Therefore, "Poison Heaven Grass" was very suitable to create Nirvana Taoist Treasures. Many third-class Nirvana Taoist Treasures preferred "Poison Heaven Grass".
There were many Nirvana Tokens similar to "Poison Heaven Grass". It all depended on whether these things were compatible with Luo Zheng's True Meaning.
After hearing 9527's words, Luo Zheng had a faint expectation in his heart. However, after thinking about it, he smiled bitterly and said, "The problem now is how to deal with these black spots …"
9527 thought for a while and then said, "Try burning it with fire …"
The Stone Sealing Solution was absorbed into Luo Zheng's body when he was being burned by the Golden Crow Divine Fire. Since he could not get it out, he could try to completely fuse it into his body.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. "I can try!"
Then, he gently pinched his hands together. A wisp of red flame quietly rose, and the raging flames immediately enveloped him.
As the flames burned, golden vortexes appeared on the surface of his body again.
Just as the golden vortexes were rotating, the Stone Sealing Solution did not move at all …
"Again!"
Seeing this, Luo Zheng continued to urge the Fledgling Fire Divine Path.
The raging flames continuously entered Luo Zheng's body.

The true intent of Dao itself was a special kind of "rhythm". By chanting according to this "rhythm", one could touch upon the power of the sea of true intent. As long as one didn't think according to this "rhythm" and dissected the words individually, there wouldn't be much of a problem.
"What's there to write … you almost lost your life!" Ning Yudie said discontentedly.
"That won't do. It wasn't easy for me to decipher it!" Luo Nian had spent a great deal of effort deciphering this Sanskrit. He naturally wasn't willing to let his efforts go to waste.
Then, Luo Nian dipped the brush in ink and continued to write word by word according to the Sanskrit on the yellow paper.
For safety reasons, Luo Nian used a piece of black cloth to cover every word he wrote. This was to prevent himself from associating the words with each other and releasing that sharp aura again.
Two hours later, Luo Nian finally finished writing. The entire process was very smooth.
He carefully rolled up the paper and handed it to Luo Zheng. At the same time, he cautiously said to Luo Zheng, "Father … although I don't know what this is, if Father wants to cultivate, you must be very, very careful!"
Hearing his son's advice, Luo Zheng was also moved. This kid had indeed grown a lot while cultivating in his inner world.
"Nian 'er, you've spent so much effort. Don't you want a reward this time?" Luo Zheng suddenly asked with a smile.
"Reward …" Luo Nian blinked and suddenly said, "If Ling Ya and I reach the Great Perfection stage, can you let us leave your inner world?"
Ling Ya was the Saintess of the Flowing Flower Sect.
Luo Nian's current strength in his inner world was already at the peak …
If he wanted to take another step forward, he could only choose to leave this place.

Transferring the Alliance Leader

Chapter 2781
Going up the mountain

"Come with me," Wen Si said.
Under Wen Si's lead, the two left Dragon City and went straight to Greater One Mountain.
This huge mountain was like a giant beast that had been creeping for countless years. It had been guarding this place for countless years, and its height was almost impossible to measure.
The two climbed along the mountain path. Although their speed was extremely fast, after climbing for a long time, when they looked back, although Dragon City was so small that it was negligible, they still seemed to be at the foot of the mountain.
After several hours, the two finally reached halfway up the mountain.
Luo Zheng followed Wen Si into a spotless palace. Wen Si had him wait at the door while he went in first.
When Wen Si came out, there was a white-robed middle-aged man at his side.
This person was called Guchen. All the disciples that went up the mountain were examined by him, and Wen Si was only responsible for the recommendation.
Guchen glanced at Luo Zheng's token, then nodded and said, "It really is the sword token of the Tian Jiedushi!"
Then he said to the path leading up the mountain, "From here, you need to go up the mountain on your own. If you encounter any trouble on the way, you need to deal with it yourself. At the top, there will naturally be someone to receive you."
The path up the mountain was never easy to walk. Wen Si had recommended many people, but those that could truly climb up were no more than ten or twenty.
"With your ability, you just need to be a little more careful on the way," Wen Si said with a smile.
"Thank you for your reminder, Elder Wen." Luo Zheng clasped his hands.
Guchen looked at Wen Si in surprise and said, "Wen Si has such confidence in this person?"
Although Luo Zheng had Qiu Yinhe's sword token, it was only because Qiu Yinhe favored this person. Since ancient times, there had been many people who went up the mountain. Who could guarantee that they would definitely be able to reach the top?
"Someone recommended by Qiu Yinhe is naturally not ordinary," Wen Si said with a smile. After being warned by Qiu Yinhe, he naturally would not reveal too much information about Luo Zheng.
Guchen shook his head. "I don't think so. Several Tian Jiedushi have recommended him. Those people who are related to him shouldn't perform too poorly. Many of them have rolled down the mountain. Their vision can't compare to Wen Si's …"

Comparison

The formation disk in Guchen's hand was only used to control the "Refining Cloud Mountain Formation". This formation was only built to test the climbers.

If one wanted to enter the Heavenly Palace, one had to go up the mountain directly.

The difficulty on the disk array had become a decoration at this moment?

Immediately after, he observed again, and his expression froze again, as if he had seen a ghost.
Wen Si looked at Guchen's expression and forced a smile, not saying a word.

Wen Si already knew that this would be the result …

Under the Third Level of difficulty, Luo Zheng's climbing speed had indeed slowed down.

No matter how these flying swords hacked at him, they couldn't hurt his body at all.

As for the "weights" that Luo Zheng saw in his left eye, they naturally disappeared one by one.
After the weights disappeared, Luo Zheng immediately felt relaxed. He gently stretched his body. His entire body once again turned into a streak of light, speeding along the Coiling Dragon Ancient Path.
Luo Zheng saw the real world with one eye. Talismans were floating towards him. With the other eye, he saw an illusory formation, and flying swords were shooting towards him.
"Break for me!"
A snowy long sword gently pointed out, turning into a series of fine sword rays.
These sword rays tumbled, touching the talismans and instantly twisting them into pieces …
This illusory formation had almost no effect on Luo Zheng. Therefore, the second level of difficulty was easier for Luo Zheng than the third level of difficulty. He climbed the mountain like this as if he was walking on flat ground.
"Speed, Luo Zheng's speed …"
In the Cloud Mountain Palace, Guchen and Wen Si were both dumbfounded.
From the time Guchen activated the second level of difficulty to the time Luo Zheng escaped with his recovery speed, it took less than the time it took for two incense sticks to burn.
The Heavenly Stem Illusory Formation did not trouble him at all!
There was an essential difference between the Heavenly Stem Illusory Formation and the Cloud Mountain Refinement Formation.
The Cloud Mountain Refinement Formation was only used for assessment, but the Heavenly Stem Illusory Formation was used to defend against enemies!
Since the establishment of the Heavenly Palace, very few people had broken the illusory formation with their own strength. Not to mention a guy like Luo Zheng who was only at the first layer of Heaven.
"How did he do it?"
"Could it be that Luo Zheng can also use his mind to control swords?"
However, the two of them could only probe through the formation plate. They did not know how Luo Zheng did it.
"Should we … adjust it to the first level of difficulty?" Wen Si could not hold back and asked.
Guchen hurriedly shook his head, as if he was afraid that he would not be able to resist Wen Si's temptation. He covered the formation plate. "The first level of difficulty may cause death. With Luo Zheng's performance, if something happens to him on the Coiling Dragon Ancient Path, I'm afraid Lord Qiu will kill me. Brother Wen Si, don't waste my time!"
"I'm just giving a suggestion." Wen Si shrugged and smiled.

Chapter 2784
Treatment
After coming out of the Coiling Dragon Ancient Road, a mountain gate appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
Seeing this mountain gate, Luo Zheng was somewhat astonished.
This mountain gate was made up of two weather-beaten pieces of wood, with a plaque covered in cracks hanging from them.
It had been in disrepair for a long time, and the words on the plaque had already disappeared, hanging there bare …
Who would have thought that the gate of the dignified Greater One Heavenly Palace would be so dilapidated.
Luo Zheng passed through the plaque and continued forward. The mountain road branched into three paths.
These three paths were neatly paved with stone bricks.
What was strange was that the bricks on these five paths were all different …
The path on the left was made of wide and thick blue stone bricks. The stone bricks were covered with carvings of mountains and rivers, giving off a majestic feeling.
The path on the right was made of black stone bricks. It was unknown what these bricks were made of, but they were very solid and exuded an ancient aura.
The path in the middle was made of fine red stones. It was brisk and agile, faintly containing a sharp intent.
But looking at the path in front of him, Luo Zheng was somewhat hesitant.
Didn't they say that once he went up the mountain, there would naturally be someone to welcome him? Why didn't he see a single person?
Looking at the three paths and pondering for a while, Luo Zheng had an answer in his heart.
The test of going up the mountain was not over …
These three paths should be a choice, a choice that did not give him any hints.
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng made his choice. He chose the red stone brick path.
After going up this path, Luo Zheng walked up the steps and jogged all the way.
After walking for more than twenty li on this path, he arrived at a mountain ridge. After crossing this ridge, another plaque appeared in front of him.
The plaque was impressively engraved with the words "Heart Flow Sword Sect".
Seeing this plaque, Luo Zheng's eyebrows unconsciously raised.
His choice was indeed not wrong …
After fighting with Situ Xiu, Luo Zheng also developed a trace of interest in the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
Some of the aura of the Heart Flow Sword Sect was extremely similar to the Emotion Severing Divine Dao that Luo Zheng cultivated. This was indeed a path that suited him.
He stood under the plaque for a while. Behind the plaque, two women in white leisurely walked over.
One of the women in white clasped her hands towards Luo Zheng, "Sir, are you Luo Zheng gongzi Luo?"
Luo Zheng nodded slightly, "That's right.
"If gongzi Luo gongzi chooses the Heart Flow Sword Sect, you can come with us. But now, you still have a chance to change your mind and choose one of the other two paths," the woman in white said.
"The other two paths are …"
"The one on the left is the Murdering Sword Sect, and the one on the right is the Absolute Formation Sword Sect," the woman in white said.
The three forks in the road were just for those ascending the mountain to consider as they pleased. However, if they really didn't like it, they could still turn back.
Two Luo Zheng Sword Sect, Luo Zheng, and Luo Zheng Luo Zheng Of Of Sword sect.
"What's the difference between the three sword sects?" Luo Zheng asked.
This was not the first time the white-robed woman was faced with this question. Her pretty face had a slight smile as she said, "The Murdering Sword Sect mainly cultivates the sword of slaughter. They pursue the strongest method to injure the enemy in order to quickly exterminate the foreign races. The Absolute Formation Sword Sect mainly cultivates the sword array …"
"What about the Heart Flow Sword Sect?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"As you wish." The white-clothed woman's answer was very simple.
Luo Zheng had dabbled in both sword formations and slaughter before. But after hearing the woman's answer, Luo Zheng did not hesitate and walked past the plaque.
The two women in white led the way. On both sides of the path were rows of straight trees. It was silent all around. There was not a single person in sight.
"There don't seem to be many people from the Heart Flow Sword Sect?" Luo Zheng asked.
"There are quite a few, but everyone is focused on cultivation. Gongzi naturally cannot see anyone," the woman in white replied.
Luo Zheng nodded and quietly followed behind the two women in white.
The path was ten li long. After crossing the path, there were many courtyards in the shape of a well. These courtyards were connected to each other and densely packed together, forming an exquisite little city.
It seemed that there were indeed many people from the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
The distance between these courtyards was only about the width of a person. The two women led Luo Zheng through the narrow alleys. Along the way, Luo Zheng also saw a few other women in white. Most of them were pretty and beautiful, and their clothes were plain and clean.
But Luo Zheng found it strange that apart from these women, there was no one else …
"Gongzi Luo, we've arrived." One of the women in white arrived at the entrance of a courtyard. She turned and bowed slightly to Luo Zheng.
"This place …" Luo Zheng was somewhat surprised.
"This is Gongzi Luo's courtyard," the woman in white said.
Surprised, Luo Zheng pushed open the door to the courtyard.
There was a long table in the courtyard. On the table were neatly folded white clothes, a sword, a token, and even shoes and hats.
Luo Zheng glanced at the token and saw his name engraved on it. He smiled and said, "The Heart Flow Sword Sect is so well-prepared. If I don't choose, won't all of this be wasted?"
"That is Gongzi Luo's choice." The woman in white smiled faintly.
Luo Zheng looked along the table, and he noticed that there were two spatial rings on the table. Luo Zheng picked up the first spatial ring, and when he inspected it, he discovered that there were actually a million Divine Crystals inside.
He looked at the second Sumeru Ring. There were ten golden square tokens in it. He took out a square token and asked, "This is …"
"The entrance token of the Heart Flow Tower. One entrance token will be used every two hours in the Heart Flow Tower. This item is very rare in the Heart Flow Sword Sect. Gongzi must take good care of it," the woman in white said.
The long sword on the table was not of a low grade. It was a second-rate Mystique Venerable Dao Treasure. There were a million Divine Crystals in the ring, but the two women in white did not seem to care about it. They did not even take the initiative to introduce it.
The only thing they emphasized was the entrance token. It seemed that this item was indeed very important.
"The Heart Flow Sword Sect is very generous. Does everyone who joins the sect get such treatment?" Luo Zheng asked.
The woman in white smiled faintly. "Those who can climb the mountain are considered members of the Heavenly Palace. This treatment is just a small gift …"
Looking at this exquisite courtyard, Luo Zheng felt a little emotional.
It turned out that the difference between the foot of the mountain and the top was so great.
One must know that regardless of whether it was the Banner Lord or the Alliance Lord, they did not even have a cave residence, let alone a courtyard like this. There was only a bare flag. Everyone cultivated with the earth as their seat and the sky as their blanket. It was extremely simple.
"Gongzi Luo must be tired from climbing the mountain. I can help you bathe and change your clothes." The woman in white said and walked to the side of the courtyard.
The other woman in white went to tidy up the clothes, shoes, and hats on the table …
Luo Zheng was stunned. He looked at the two women in white and asked curiously, "You don't have to do these things, do you?"

Chapter 2785

An Essay on Leisure
Luo Zheng was taken aback when he heard the woman in white call herself a maid. "Ya, maid?"

Luo Zheng shook his head. "I want to ask, why are you maids?"

Luo Zheng had not encountered any other disciples on the way to the Heart Flow Sword Sect, but he had encountered many women in white like Xiaoyin and Xiao Xin.
He had been a little curious at the time, but now he realized that these people were the maids of the Heart Flow Sword Sect disciples.
"Can I not?" Luo Zheng asked.
Since the Heart Flow Sword Sect had given him such a large courtyard, Luo Zheng could even invite Ning Yudie and the others to come out. He himself did not need anyone to take care of him.

When in Rome, do as the Romans do. It would be unsatisfactory if he drove them away.
"In that case, you can stay. As for bathing, you don't have to." Luo Zheng shook his head.

These maids were all selected from the Central Divine Province by the Heart Flow Sword Sect. Their cultivation wasn't low either, basically at the True Immortal Realm.
Every time someone joined the Heart Flow Sword Sect, these maids would be assigned to the new disciples.
If the disciples they served performed well, they would be rewarded by the Heart Flow Sword Sect. Some of the maids would even develop feelings for the disciples during their service. They could even become Daoist companions, which was considered climbing up the social ladder.
In the room, steam rose from a large vat and a strong medicinal smell spread out.
"Do as you wish … no taboos … What a Heart Flow Sword Sect." Luo Zheng smiled helplessly.
This Heart Flow Sword Sect was far more strange than Luo Zheng had imagined.
No one preached or guided them. They only provided these things to show their sincerity.
Luo Zheng would have to find out the reason bit by bit …
Bathing and changing clothes.
Luo Zheng, who had changed into a white robe, seemed to have changed his appearance. He looked even more upright and bright.
After leaving the bathroom, the two maids came up to him again.
Xiaoyin said, "If Young Master Luo is tired, I'll go take care of the tent. It's only noon now, so it's time for lunch …"
"Cough cough cough …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng almost choked.
He was just trying to get used to this place, but the atmosphere here was far beyond his imagination!
Not to mention his current cultivation, even warriors who were full of energy every day didn't need to take lunch breaks, right?
Was this a place for cultivation, or was it a place for a vacation?
"Are the others like this too?" Luo Zheng asked.
Xiaoyin nodded with a smile. "Yes."
She had expected Luo Zheng to react this way.
In fact, every Nirvana Realm cultivator who entered here had the same reaction as Luo Zheng …
"They don't need to cultivate?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"For the Heart Flow Sword Sect, this is cultivation," Xiaoyin answered seriously.
"Okay, I'll see what kind of tricks this is …" Luo Zheng said.
According to Luo Zheng's original plan, he should have started cultivating.
He should have either studied the sword pattern or entered the Nirvana Realm, but he did neither.
Just like a loafer at home, he walked around the small courtyard. When it was time for lunch and night, he would go to sleep alone.
Of course, he didn't need to sleep. Even lying down was meditation.
If it was during the day, he would have just stepped over the threshold and walked around the alleys of these courtyards. The only people he met were the women in white who shuttled back and forth.
Luo Zheng wanted to find a disciple of the Heart Flow Sword Sect to ask, but one day, two days, a week had passed, and he hadn't met a single one!
Were these guys all in seclusion? Or was he the only one in the entire Heart Flow Sword Sect?
As time went by, he became more and more puzzled.
Therefore, Luo Zheng decided to knock on the doors of other courtyards and go in to ask. However, when he walked to the door, the women in white closed the door as if they were guarding against a thief …
Of course, he couldn't knock on the door with his outstretched hand.
Shaking his head, Luo Zheng could only walk back through the alleys.
Along the way, he could hear the laughter and whispers of women in the courtyard. He didn't need to look to know what was going on in the courtyard.
"Whew … What the hell is this place?!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng felt that this place was even more dangerous than the dangerous places he had been to!
He curled his lips and went back.
Luo Zheng couldn't waste this good time. He even missed the days in Dragon City.
Since no one was paying attention to him, he found a quiet room in his courtyard and set up a restriction array. He started to study the sword pattern art in seclusion.
After cultivating like this for half a month, Luo Zheng came out and gave a Sumeru Ring to Xiaoyin. He asked Xiaoyin to give it to Yin Yuehuan in Dragon City.
The Sumeru Ring was filled with Sword Comprehension Liquid. Before coming up the mountain, Luo Zheng had agreed that Yin Yuehuan would take 30% of the profits from the Sword Comprehension Liquid. The other 70% would be given to Luo Zheng and would be replenished once a month.
Xiaoyin was very responsible. It only took a day for her to bring back the Sumeru Ring.
Luo Zheng counted it. There were nearly 20 million divine crystals in it.
"Young Master Luo, what's in the Sumeru Ring? It seems so mysterious …" Xiaoyin asked.
"Just some ordinary things." Luo Zheng shrugged. He naturally wouldn't tell her about the Sword Comprehension Liquid.

Chapter 2786
A Strange Evaluation
Luo Zheng's mind was shaken by the sound of the bell.
It's finally here …
If this continued, he might have to return to Dragon City!
"That bell, is it summoning the disciples?" Luo Zheng asked.
Xiaoyin saw Luo Zheng's hopeful expression and pursed her lips. "Gongzi Luo, that's just the sound of the bell at the beginning of the month. It's rung once a month …"
"And then?" Luo Zheng asked.
"There's nothing else. It's just to remind everyone that a new month has arrived," Xiaoyin replied.
"… …"
Luo Zheng did not say a word and silently closed the door.
Then, Xiao Yin clicked her tongue and looked at Xiao Xin. Both of them shrugged their shoulders.
After avoiding Luo Zheng, Little Xin said, "It seems that Gongzi Luo is also an impatient person …"
Xiaoyin shook her head. "According to them, Gongzi Luo won't be able to achieve much in the Heart Flow Sword Sect."
"No matter what, I think that Gongzi Luo is quite good. He treats others with courtesy and restrains himself," Little Xin said.
Despite Little Xin's words, Xiaoyin's expression was still grave.
She had been willing to become a servant girl in the Heart Flow Sword Sect because she had a few things to take care of. She was more utilitarian than cautious. If Luo Zheng's performance was extremely poor, Xiaoyin would naturally be disappointed.
But she had no right to choose who to serve, so she could only hide her disappointment in her heart.
When night fell …
The small doors to the courtyards opened one after another.
A white-clothed woman walked out of each courtyard and walked through the alleyways.
The moment Xiao Yin stepped out of the door, Luo Zheng had already sensed it.
His eyes flickered slightly. Then, he released a wisp of divine sense and followed behind Xiao Yin.
Xiaoyin and the other women silently gathered in one direction. The moment Xiaoyin left the alleyway, Luo Zheng felt a vast power sweep over him and annihilate his Divine Sense.
These white-robed women quickly gathered in a palace.
Inside the palace, there were five golden round bamboo tubes, and all the white-clothed women were standing in front of these round bamboo tubes.
Not long after, several women walked into the palace.
The leader was a beautiful woman. She smiled at the girls and said, "A month has passed. Have you girls been serving your young masters?"
Such mocking words were not serious at all. However, the Heart Flow Sword Sect did not know any manners and did not have any taboos.
The white-clothed women also burst into laughter.
"My family's Young Master is amazing. He's always, always …" A woman in white said.
"Always what?" The beautiful woman asked.
"It's always been like this …" the woman in white replied embarrassedly.
Another round of bell-like laughter followed.
Then, the beautiful woman asked about what her young master had been doing during this period of time, how many times he had bathed, how many times he had been under the bed, and so on.
However, he didn't ask about cultivation.
According to the answers of the women in white, the seductive woman placed a tag with a name on it into the round bamboo tube.
The five bamboo tubes were divided into Top, Top, Middle, Bottom, and Bottom.
Depending on the answer, the bamboo tubes would be different.
For four to six hours, the seductive woman had been asking questions. She had also placed a large number of tags into the bamboo tubes.
Only a very small number of people had placed their tags into the Top, Top, and Top. Most people had placed theirs into the Top …
If the name of their young master was placed into the Top, the white-clothed women would be overjoyed. If the name of their young master was placed into the Top or Bottom, they wouldn't be so happy.
Their young master's performance every month directly determined their salaries, and the difference was very big.
If it was the Bottom or Bottom, they would basically have nothing. Fortunately, after so long, no tags were placed into the Top or Bottom.
"Loraine, your young master is a new disciple. How is he doing?" The seductive woman looked at the white-clothed woman beside Xiaoyin.
Loraine answered honestly, "My young master has been on the mountain for more than 20 days. Every day, he plays the zither, paints, and sings poems with us. He's very talented and has a very good temperament …"
After hearing Loraine's answer, the seductive woman tossed her tag into the Top.
Then she looked at Xiaoyin. "Xiaoyin, you've been serving the young master for a month."
The white-clothed women all looked at Xiaoyin. Xiaoyin's expression became unnatural. She stammered, "My young master … set up a barrier on the seventh day and cultivated by himself. Of course, I couldn't see it …"
After Xiaoyin's report, the other white-clothed women laughed again.
"Xiaoyin, did he choose a path?"
"Heart Flow Sword Sect isn't suitable for him to cultivate …"
"Someone like him will be kicked out, right?"
Xiaoyin gritted her teeth and continued to report with a frown. The more she said, the louder the laughter became.
These white-clothed women were all proud of their young masters. If they were blessed, they would treat it as an honor. They didn't have the restraint of ordinary women.
After the flirtatious woman finished listening to Xiao Yin's report, a tag appeared in her hand. The tag just happened to have the two words "Luo Zheng" on it. Then, without hesitation, she threw the tag into the pot. This was the only tag that was thrown into the pot.
At the same time, the seductive woman's casual smile disappeared. She stared at Xiaoyin and said, "The young masters of Heart Flow Sword Sect are the ones who can do whatever they want, not you. If your young master can't get rid of his obsession with cultivation, you should be punished too! I don't want this person to be tossed into the Top next time! "
Looking at the tag on the Top, Xiaoyin's face turned pale. She clenched her fists.
The gazes that were directed at her from all directions were like sharp swords, causing her cheeks to feel like they were on fire.
"Disperse." The seductive woman ordered someone to put away the Top and left with her people.
The white-clothed women also dispersed …
When the women gathered together, there would always be a lot of gossip and discussion.
Walking alone in the crowd, Xiao Yin felt that her head was heavy and her feet light.
Occasionally, someone would call out to Xiaoyin and say, "Xiaoyin, it's a good thing there's no exam this month. There's a problem with the Heart Flow Tower. After the Heart Flow Tower is repaired, you have to be careful. If your young master is expelled from Heart Flow Sword Sect, we will also be expelled …"
These women's words sounded like they were concerned, but they were actually mocking her.
"Yes, I understand …"
Xiaoyin replied, but she felt even more depressed.
When she returned to Luo Zheng's courtyard and met with Xiaoyin, Xiaoyin had already guessed the result.
"Middle?"
"Bottom?"
"It can't be bottom, right?"
Xiaoyin looked like she had fainted. After a while, she said with a worried expression, "Then what should we do …"
"We have to think of a way." Xiaoyin's expression became serious.
"What way?" Xiaoyin asked.

Sword Pattern

Fortunately, this enchantment was not very strong. Xiaoyin was bounced out, but she was slightly injured. She put on a thin layer of makeup and deliberately lowered her skirt. Her slender and tender legs were exposed. She looked weak and pitiful.
However, Luo Zheng did not give her a second glance. He reached out and flicked his finger. A wisp of green light enveloped her. The Dao of the Divine Dao of Life healed her.
Just as Luo Zheng turned around, Xiaoyin said, "I thought that Young Master Luo would need some nourishment for his daily cultivation. Therefore, I made soup for Young Master Luo … It seems that I have to get another pot."
The pot of soup had been knocked over and was emitting fragrant steam.
Looking at the pot of soup, Luo Zheng just shook his head. "I have been fasting for many years. You don't have to worry about it."
"But …"
Xiaoyin was about to speak again, but Luo Zheng had already returned to the room. With a gentle wave of his finger, another enchantment enveloped the room.
She clenched her teeth and retreated resentfully.
"Sister Xiaoyin, how is it …" Seeing Xiaoyin's appearance, Little Xin had already guessed the answer. However, she could not help but ask.
"What an unreasonable guy. Unfortunately, we can't tell him …" Xiaoyin curled her lips.
This was the rule of the Heart Flow Sword Sect. These maids could not speak of the secret of the Heart Flow Pagoda.
"Once the Heart Flow Pagoda is repaired, Young Master Luo will not be able to pass through it. I'm afraid he will be driven away immediately. We only have one month," Little Xin said dejectedly.
"I won't give up!" Xiaoyin said seriously.
In the next few days, Xiaoyin did her best.
One day, Little Xin was proficient in music. She brought a guzheng over and played it on the second floor of the courtyard. Luo Zheng cast a Silent Enchantment to isolate the sound.
Another day, taking advantage of Luo Zheng coming out of his closed-door training, Xiao Yin took the initiative to ask Luo Zheng about her painting. She asked Luo Zheng to comment on it, but Luo Zheng flatly refused with the reason that he was not proficient.
Another day …

Heart Flow Tower
Xiaoyin was someone who wouldn't give up until she got what she wanted, but she couldn't do anything to the stubborn Luo Zheng.
A few days later, Xiaoyin finally despaired when she heard that the Heart Flow Tower had been repaired.
On the first day of the month, the Heart Flow Sword Sect's bell rang again.
Luo Zheng didn't seem to care about the bell at all.
Since the Heart Flow Sword Sect was so strange, Luo Zheng could just treat this courtyard as a cave residence.
He would continue cultivating after he mastered the sword pattern technique. After all, he wasn't in a hurry.

Xiaoyin went to the palace with the other women again.

"The Heart Flow Tower has been repaired. Tomorrow, the disciples of the Heart Flow Sword Sect will go to the Heart Flow Tower to train. You should leave the sect on your own when this person leaves." The beautiful woman didn't look at Xiaoyin kindly anymore.
The other white-clothed women were also looking at Xiao Yin with pitiful expressions.
Being expelled from the Heart Flow Sword Sect was probably the greatest humiliation for these women in white.
Xiaoyin returned to the courtyard with a look of despair.
Naturally, Xiao Xin had expected this outcome. She had also made plans to leave.
"Go tell Master Luo that the Heart Flow Tower has been opened." Xiaoyin looked lonely.
"It's better if Sister Xiaoyin says it herself …" Xiao Xin said.

Xiao Xin sighed helplessly and went upstairs to pass the news to Luo Zheng.

Luo Zheng didn't think that he would stay like this after joining the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
Otherwise, why would the Heart Flow Sword Sect give out those entry tokens?
But Luo Zheng couldn't figure out the Heart Flow Sword Sect's carefree way of thinking.

"Gongzi Luo, please come with me." Xiao Xin bowed slightly in front of Luo Zheng.
Xiaoyin felt gloomy and shut herself in her room. She could only rely on Xiao Xin to guide her.

The women in white led the disciples of the Heart Flow Sword Sect through the alleys.
The cultivation of these disciples wasn't weak. Almost all of them were around the sixth level of the Nirvana Realm. They glanced at Luo Zheng intentionally or unintentionally with faint disdain between their brows.
Those women in white recognized Little Xin and naturally understood that Luo Zheng was Xiaoyin and Little Xin's servant. Some of the women in white stuck close to their young masters' ears and whispered.

"What a pity. Xiaoyin worked so hard, but she still has to face the fate of being kicked out."
Although the women in white spoke very softly, Luo Zheng still heard them.

Little Xin was very sure in her heart, but she still shook her head and said, "This is your own choice. As maids, we can't interfere."
Luo Zheng felt that Little Xin didn't mean what she said. He just smiled and said, "I won't disappoint you."
"As if …" Little Xin retorted in her heart.
They followed the crowd and left the courtyard. After passing through several palaces, a tall pagoda appeared in front of them.
This pagoda was shaped like a gourd with a total of 19 floors. There was a door on the surface of each gourd.
Around the pagoda, there were a dozen independent courtyards. These courtyards were the same as the one Luo Zheng lived in, but they were built around the Heart Flow Pagoda. It was unknown who built them.
As Luo Zheng approached, he felt a strange feeling coming from the quaint Heart Flow Pagoda.
His originally calm state of mind seemed to have been disturbed, and he felt somewhat restless.
Luo Zheng originally thought that the others were like this, but the disciples of the Heart Flow Sword Sect were either chatting and laughing wittily or looking normal. They didn't seem to be affected by the Heart Flow Pagoda.
Little Xin sighed in her heart when she saw Luo Zheng's behavior.
Since these disciples of the Heart Flow Sword Sect had the strength to go up the mountain, they weren't true good-for-nothings.
The backlash of the Heart Flow Pagoda's obsession was very terrifying …
Before entering the pagoda, they had to completely release their inner desires.
Greed, Obsession, Caution, Lust, Acceptance, Enjoyment, Action, Knowledge …
Everything had to be satisfied as much as possible.
Of course, this kind of satisfaction wasn't absolute.
For example, some people dreamed of getting a first-rate Nirvana Realm treasure, but they couldn't have so many Divine Gems, so they naturally couldn't be satisfied. Of course, the Heart Flow Sword Sect wouldn't really find a first-rate Nirvana Realm treasure as a gift. This was unrealistic.
He had given her a courtyard and two maidservants. That was all she had.
The more satisfied they were, the more they could suppress their obsessions to the minimum. For the Heart Flow Sword Sect, this was an alternative way of cultivation.
When the disciples gathered, Luo Zheng's gaze was also looking around.
He was looking for Mo Yijian.
This person was in the Heart Flow Sword Sect, so he would meet him sooner or later.
After searching for a while, he didn't see him.
But Luo Zheng seemed to be possessed, and he had to find him …
If it was the Luo Zheng of the past, he wouldn't be so anxious to find Mo Yijian, even though he cared a lot about him.
After searching for a while, Luo Zheng suddenly realized something, and he frowned quietly. This tower had quietly affected his thoughts!
"I'm afraid I've underestimated the Heart Flow Tower," Luo Zheng thought.
At this time, a dozen people flew out of the courtyard and landed in front of the Heart Flow Tower.
The coquettish woman waved her hand, and someone placed a row of golden cylinders in front of her. "Every disciple who enters the Heart Flow Sword Sect will only get ten entrance tokens in a year, and they can stay in the Heart Flow Tower for 20 hours. So every opportunity to enter the Heart Flow Tower is extremely rare, and I hope you cherish it!"
"Those who enter the Heart Flow Tower for the first time and can't pass the second floor will be expelled from the Heart Flow Sword Sect immediately, and their entrance tokens will be divided among the others. If someone can climb to the seventh floor, another courtyard will be sealed, and an additional 100 entrance tokens will be given every year."
The coquettish woman's words were naturally targeted at newcomers like Luo Zheng.
The independent courtyards behind the Heart Flow Tower were prepared for the elite disciples of the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
After she finished speaking, she pulled out a tag from the golden cylinder and shouted, "Zhu Chaoyun, you can enter the tower."
Hearing the coquettish woman call his name, a pale-faced young man stretched and walked leisurely towards the Heart Flow Tower.
Then the coquettish woman pulled out another person's tag from the golden cylinder and called his name. That person also entered the Heart Flow Tower.

The Last Person
The time spent in the Heart Flow Pagoda was calculated by hours.
Every hour was very precious, and the people who entered the pagoda would seize every second.

Although she knew that there was no hope, she couldn't give up until she saw it with her own eyes.

These spots of light paused on the first floor for a while before climbing up to the second floor in the blink of an eye.
"So fast … These young masters are the most outstanding. Unfortunately, I don't have the luck." Xiaoyin sighed as she stared at the golden spots of light.
These golden spots of light could accurately mark the position of each person.
The first batch of people to enter were all from the top. They were well-prepared and were naturally like fish in water.
The beautiful woman looked at the movement of the spots of light with satisfaction.
"The Heart Flow Pagoda has been closed for two months, and the disciples have made great progress. I wonder if anyone can pass the seventh floor?" someone beside the beautiful woman asked.
"Zhu Chaoyun's limit last time was the sixth floor. If he can reach the seventh floor, he'll be qualified to move," another person said with a smile.
They were also looking forward to someone moving into those independent courtyards, but in the past year, no one from the Heart Flow Sword Sect had obtained this qualification.
The golden spots of light kept jumping on the surface of the Heart Flow Pagoda. In a dozen breaths of time, seven or eight spots of light had jumped to the third floor.
After the time it took for an incense stick to burn, the 10 specks of light stopped at the fifth level.
"The fifth floor of the Heart Flow Pagoda is very important."
"Our young master is accumulating strength on this floor. Maybe he can reach the seventh floor in one go!"

"It must be our Young Master Zhu!" A woman in white with two ponytails exclaimed in surprise.

Two months ago, Zhu Chaoyun was just a little bit short of reaching the seventh floor, so he regretfully left the Heart Flow Tower. This time, the possibility of reaching the seventh floor was indeed very high.
As for the other nine golden light spots, they were still struggling on the fifth floor. From the looks of it, it would be very difficult for them to reach the sixth floor.

The maids in the Heart Heart Translation Pagoda were getting more and more nervous. Success or failure would depend on this. The golden spot of light on the sixth floor suddenly rushed up.
Buzz ….
Under everyone's gaze, the golden spot of light really reached the seventh floor.
Most people who reached a certain floor would try to consolidate their strength if they had time. After all, time in the Heart Flow Pagoda was too precious.
However, this person would not be able to withstand the pressure of the seventh floor. The moment he reached the seventh floor, he broke through the door and flew out.
"Young Master Zhu, our Young Master Zhu …" The maids in the Heart Heart Translation Pagoda shouted excitedly, but she suddenly stopped.
The person who walked out of the Heart Flow Pagoda was not Zhu Chaoyun, but Li Can ….
The maids in the Heart Heart Translation Pagoda were disappointed. Not far from her, the two maids in the Heart Heart Translation Pagoda started cheering.
In the last few hours, three more people reached the sixth floor of the Heart Flow Pagoda. Zhu Chaoyun was one of them.
However, the sixth floor and the seventh floor were completely different.
When the two hours were up, Zhu Chaoyun and the other two unhappily opened the door to the sixth floor. The other six people also came out from the fifth floor.
These people were aiming for the seventh floor of the Heart Flow Pagoda. It was indeed a great pity that they could not reach the seventh floor.
After these ten people left the Heart Flow Pagoda, the coquettish woman selected another ten people to enter.
The strength of the second batch was obviously not as good as the first batch. Whether it was the speed or the height, they were weaker. Only one person stayed on the sixth floor, while the others stayed on the fourth and fifth floors.
Then came the third, fourth, and fifth batches ….
The disciples who left the Heart Flow Pagoda did not choose to leave. They were still waiting here.
"What are these people waiting for?" Luo Zheng asked Xiao Xin, puzzled.
Xiao Xin thought for a moment and smiled. "They are waiting for you to enter the Heart Flow Pagoda."
"They are waiting for us?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
"Yes, you are a new disciple. It is very likely that you will not be able to climb to the second floor. When that time comes, you will be expelled from the Heart Flow Sword Sect. In that case, the remaining nine entry tokens will be divided among these people. How can they be willing to leave?" Xiao Xin said.
Obtaining an entry token meant one more chance to enter the Heart Flow Pagoda. No one was willing to miss it.
"I see …."
Luo Zheng suddenly realized that these people were waiting for him to be expelled from the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
Batches of people entered the Heart Flow Pagoda. Those who came later were not as talented as those who came before. Their results were getting worse and worse. The first few batches were able to enter the fifth and sixth floor. Most of the people who entered later stayed on the fourth, third, and even second floor.
However, until the last batch of ten people entered, no one was trapped on the first floor. Even the new disciples who entered the Heart Flow Sword Sect at the same time as Luo Zheng struggled to climb to the second floor. No one was expelled from the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
This was a disappointing result for the disciples who wanted to get an entry token. Some of them were ready to leave.
At this moment, the coquettish woman said, "There is still one person who has not entered the Heart Flow Pagoda today."
Saying this, she took out Luo Zheng's label from the cylinder. "This disciple called Luo Zheng has been on the bottom floor for two months in a row. It is very rare. Now it is his turn to enter the Heart Flow Pagoda."
The number of people entering the Heart Flow Pagoda was a round number of ten. Luo Zheng happened to be the only one left.

Fire of the Soul

Back then, when he fought against Situ Xiu, his sword art contained a strange feeling.
This feeling was similar to the Emotion Severing Divine Dao. At that time, Luo Zheng had a trace of interest in the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
And Luo Zheng understood another layer of the situation.
Back in Guanshan Prefecture, when Qiu Yinhe showed up, he also acted like an old pervert, touching Phoenix Maiden from head to toe. A dignified senior like him was so impulsive that he didn't have the slightest bit of grace.
It seemed that Qiu Yinhe was also an expert from the Heart Flow Sword Sect.

Qiu Yinhe didn't show up, which meant that he was confident in passing the Heart Flow Tower.

He suppressed this anxiety and stepped into the Heart Flow Tower.
"Bang …"
The door of the Heart Flow Tower slammed shut.
The space inside the tower wasn't big, and there was no illusion array.
However, there was a column in the center of the tower. There seemed to be a flame burning inside, emitting a hot aura, which made the temperature inside the Heart Flow Tower much higher.
However, for Luo Zheng, who had been calcined by all kinds of flames, this temperature was nothing.
Just as Luo Zheng was about to move away, a voice came from the Heart Flow Tower. "Show your entry token."
Luo Zheng shook his sumeru ring gently and took out a golden square token.
"Swish …"
This golden square token seemed to be made of ice and instantly melted in the Heart Flow Tower.
"You can stay on the first floor or climb up. It depends on your strength and ability …" The voice also understood that it was Luo Zheng's first time entering the Heart Flow Tower and gave him a small hint.
The moment the voice disappeared, the surface of the column in the center of the Heart Flow Tower began to glow red. There really were flames coming out of the column.
"So, so hot …"
At the same time, Luo Zheng felt his chest boiling.
He wasn't afraid of flames. Even if the Heart Flow Tower was filled with flames, it couldn't stop him at all.
However, the situation this time seemed to be different.
Luo Zheng endured the pain in his chest and took a few steps into the tower.
As he moved forward, the burning pain became more and more obvious. At the same time, countless distracting thoughts came and began to affect Luo Zheng's mood.
"Anxiety, failure, fear, greed, lust …"
Everyone had these negative emotions, but everyone had a lid that covered these emotions. If one's mind was strong enough, they could completely ignore them. Luo Zheng could do it.
But ignoring them didn't mean they didn't exist. In the Heart Flow Tower, all the emotions were ignited by the restless flame.
Even if Luo Zheng forced himself to move forward, the pain would continue to increase …
After taking seven or eight steps, Luo Zheng's chest suddenly hurt. A red flame was burning in his chest!
"The backlash of the Heart Flow Tower can swallow your heart. Young man, the stronger your obsession, the stronger the backlash of the Heart Flow Tower. If you can't bear it, you can retreat on your own." The voice in the Heart Flow Tower sounded again.
"Heart Flow Tower?"
Luo Zheng endured the pain and stopped.
He reached out to the red flame in his chest. This flame didn't burn Luo Zheng's hand.
It didn't have a high temperature, but the pain caused by the burning was definitely not an illusion.
"If it's just climbing to the second floor …" Luo Zheng looked up.
There were no stairs in the Heart Flow Tower. If he wanted to go to the second floor, he could just jump.
Luo Zheng made up his mind. With a stomp of his feet, he flew up into the air.
But the moment Luo Zheng jumped up, the red Heart Flow Tower in his chest suddenly swelled up like a huge cocoon, wrapping Luo Zheng up. The pain it brought was magnified hundreds of times!
Along with the pain came unsuppressable irritation, loss, and despair.
At the moment when countless negative emotions broke out, he almost felt that he was an orphan abandoned by the whole world. There was no value in his existence.
"Plop …"
He only jumped up half a zhang before falling to the bottom of the tower.

Otherworldly Magical Powers

Two incense sticks …
Three incense sticks …
Time passed quickly. More than an hour had passed, and the people outside the Heart Flow Tower were still waiting.
"After trying twice, he doesn't dare to move anymore. What a good-for-nothing …"
"He's thinking about life at the bottom of the tower!"
"It's okay. It's almost time."
Just as the women were whispering, a door of one of the independent courtyards behind the Heart Flow Tower quietly opened.

Chapter 2792
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
Up
Everyone was bored to see the golden light spot in the Heart Flow Pagoda motionless.
Therefore, the appearance of the young man and the woman in yellow immediately attracted everyone's attention.
"It's Mo Yijian, Young Master Mo …"
"He actually came out today!"
"He's the top disciple of the Heart Flow Sword Sect. It's said that he can comprehend Dao with a single thought. He can make as much Sword Comprehension Liquid as he wants!"
"Sword Comprehension Liquid is so expensive. Won't he earn an astronomical amount of Divine Crystals by selling it?"
The women in white looked at Mo Yijian with bright eyes.
Geniuses were always the center of attention and were admired.
As for Zhu Chaoyun and the other disciples of the Heart Flow Sword Sect, they felt inferior when they saw Mo Yijian.
Those who lived in the independent buildings were the elites of the Heart Flow Sword Sect, and Mo Yijian was the most outstanding of them all.
The Heart Flow Pagoda was only open for twenty hours a year for ordinary disciples, but it was open for a hundred hours for elite disciples, which was ten times the number. However, it was said that Mo Yijian had special privileges and could enter the Heart Flow Pagoda as many times as he wanted!
This was probably the greatest privilege of the Heart Flow Sword Sect.
As for the woman beside Mo Yijian, she was also someone with a strong background in the Heavenly Palace.
The beautiful woman smiled when she saw Mo Yijian. "Yijian, you have the time to go out today?"
"It's too boring to be locked up in the courtyard." Mo Yijian shrugged casually and looked at the Heart Flow Pagoda with a strange expression. "Why is there only one person in the Heart Flow Pagoda? And he's at the bottom level? "
"This person has been evaluated as' inferior 'for two months in a row, and he's the last person. Naturally, there's only one person left," the beautiful woman replied.
"Oh?"
Mo Yijian's interest was piqued and he halted his steps.
"Yijian, what's there to see …" the woman called "Han 'er" beside Mo Yijian said coquettishly.
"I just want to see how long he can last. He's been evaluated as' inferior 'for two months in a row. His heart is deeply rooted in the Heart Flow Pagoda …" Mo Yijian said.
"But …" Han 'er still wanted to pull Mo Yijian away.
At this time, the beautiful woman said, "This person has been in the Heart Flow Tower for more than an hour. He will be kicked out in at most two incense sticks' worth of time."
"That's right, we're not in a hurry." Mo Yijian stood still and reached out to hold Han 'er's hand.
Not far away, Little Xin sized up Mo Yijian from head to toe. Then, she said to Xiaoyin, "Sister Xiaoyin, if we serve Young Master Mo, how glorious would that be?"
Xiaoyin shook her head and said, "For someone like Mo Yijian, how could the woman by his side be ordinary? That yellow-clothed woman is not his maid. Rumor has it that she is his fiancée. Her father is also a high-ranking member of the Heavenly Palace, unlike us … "
"Fate is decided at birth." Little Xin curled her lips.
"It's a pity that Young Master Luo has persevered for long enough. It seems that he won't be able to break through." Xiaoyin looked at Luo Zheng again.
Xiaoyin was different from other women. She did not have so many boring fantasies. Everything depended on the path under her feet. Luo Zheng had been at the bottom for so long, but he still did not have the confidence to make the final leap. His hope was getting smaller and smaller.
While everyone waited, another incense stick's worth of time passed.
Luo Zheng was still meditating with his eyes closed.
The Dao of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao was constantly dissecting in his mind.
At this moment, he no longer saw the Emotion Severing Divine Dao as a killing Theurgy. Instead, he saw it as a special Theurgy of the Soul, similar to the Great Ruling Art.
"The Emotion Severing Divine Dao's power lies in severing one's emotions and erupting with a decisive sword …"
"The Fire of the Heart can attack this point …"
"I don't need to continue severing my emotions. If distracting thoughts grow like weeds, I can just sever them myself!"
"Understood!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly opened his eyes.
"Rise!"
Having made a decision, Luo Zheng jumped up again.
"Whoosh!"
Just as Luo Zheng rushed to the second floor of the Heart Flow Pagoda, the Fire of the Heart burned in his chest again, and his anxious emotions were stirred up again.
"Emotion Severing …"
Luo Zheng immediately used the Emotion Severing Divine Dao.
But the Fire of the Heart also had a response. They instantly gathered at one point and went straight to Luo Zheng's glabella.
Previously, Luo Zheng was defeated by this move. But this time, Luo Zheng came prepared!
The Fire of the Heart could attack this point, and Luo Zheng could also resist this point.
When it broke through the Emotion Severing Divine Dao and used Luo Zheng's obsession to arouse endless emotions, Luo Zheng also gathered all his Dao Connotation at this moment.
"Buzz …"
The confrontation between the two was only for a moment, but it was actually extremely dangerous.
The emotions aroused by the Fire of the Heart had been completely extinguished …
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng had reached the second floor of the Heart Flow Pagoda.
"Huh?"
The face transformed by the Fire of the Heart was shocked when it saw this scene. "He comprehended the One Thought Severing Emotion so quickly?"
Although the status of Divine Dao in the mother world was not high because of the existence of the Nirvana Realm, a supernatural power outside of Dao was not a cabbage that could be easily comprehended. Moreover, Luo Zheng had to bear the pressure of the Fire of the Heart in the Heart Flow Pagoda!
Without any hesitation, Luo Zheng jumped up again!
Similar to the situation on the first floor, the Fire of the Heart rose from Luo Zheng's chest again and spread toward his head.
"Again!"
Luo Zheng did not know what the name of this supernatural power was. He only knew that he could only use this method to break the Fire of the Heart.
"Buzz …"
With a flash of white light in his mind, the Fire of the Heart penetrated his mind again, but the negative emotions of anxiety, failure, and fear were still decisively extinguished.
"The third floor!"
Luo Zheng jumped again!
Outside the Heart Flow Pagoda.
When Luo Zheng jumped to the second floor, everyone had different expressions.
"Young Master Luo! Young Master Luo went to the second floor! "Little Xin screamed.
"I saw it." Xiaoyin stared at the round light spot. As long as Luo Zheng did not fall and could walk out of the door of the second floor, it meant that he would not be expelled.
Therefore, Xiaoyin was still a little worried. However, not only did the golden light spot not fall, but it also went straight to the third floor!
"That kid …"
"He rushed to the third floor in one go?"
"How can he be so fast!"
The disciples of the Heart Flow Sword Sect were also dumbfounded.
They had waited here for so long, and no one expected this result.
"Didn't you say that this person is just a lower floor? Jumping to the third floor at this speed, his talent is not bad, "Mo Yijian said with a smile. This had nothing to do with Mo Yijian. He was just watching the show.
The coquettish woman and her entourage were dumbfounded when they saw this.
"He's going up again!" someone exclaimed.
The golden light spot on the surface of the Heart Flow Pagoda jumped again and reached the fourth floor.
Before anyone could react, the golden light spot jumped again. The fifth floor …
"The fifth floor? This guy … "
The disciples of the Heart Flow Sword Sect also understood that they would not be able to get Luo Zheng's entry token, but the result of the fifth floor was still unacceptable.

Chapter 2793
Translator: Henyee Translations Editor: Henyee Translations
It's him!
The disciples of the Heart Sword Sect widened their eyes and held their breaths.
The white-clothed women also stared at the golden specks of light in a daze. No one could say a word. The entire place was silent.
What kind of person was trying to reach the seventh floor on his first time in the Heart Flow Tower?
Xiaoyin and Xiao Xin were also speechless.
Their eyes were shining with excitement, as if they were about to cry. It was a very moving sight.
Mo Yijian originally had the intention of watching the fun. But after seeing this scene, the expression of the woman beside him also changed.
"This person's obsession is very deep, yet he can still reach the sixth floor. Like Yijian, this person also entered the Dao through the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, right?" Han'er looked at the Heart Flow Tower and whispered to Mo Yijian.
"Not necessarily. I heard that using the Soul Control Divine Dao to control one's own mind can also resist the Heart Fire," Mo Yijian said.
The Soul Control Divine Dao was a great ruling technique in the divine realm.
It was an extra-dao ability comprehended from the Divine Dao. It could create another self consciousness and complete the strange method of "controlling" oneself.
"But I'm afraid he won't be able to break Yi Jian's record …" Han 'er smiled.
The performance of the person in the tower was amazing, but when Mo Yijian entered the Heart Flow Tower for the first time, he directly jumped to the eighth floor.
Therefore, in the eyes of Mo Yijian and Han 'er, this performance was still acceptable.
But as soon as Han 'er finished speaking, the person in the Heart Flow Tower had reached the seventh floor!
The seventh and sixth levels were difficult to pass. Most of the Heart Sword Sect's disciples would be trapped here. Therefore, the Heart Sword Sect had set up a reward on the seventh level. Only by passing this level could one enter the ranks of the elites.
While everyone was still guessing the possibility of Luo Zheng reaching the seventh floor, he had already reached the seventh floor.
Inside the Heart Flow Tower, the Heart Fire had turned blood red.
As he climbed higher and higher, the Heart Fire's power grew stronger.
Even if he used the methods of the Emotion Severing Divine Dao, it was still difficult to completely cut off the negative emotions that were like weeds.
But he clenched his teeth and endured.
At this moment, Luo Zheng had lost track of time. He didn't even know he was on the first floor.
He only knew that if he hesitated for even a moment, those chaotic emotions would fill his heart and push him into the abyss of pain.
This pain was too special. It wasn't a wound to the soul, nor was it a physical pain. it was a direct attack on the heart!
"Charge!"
Luo Zheng jumped again.
"The eighth floor …"
Han 'er glanced at Mo Mo Yijian.
Mo Yijian didn't say anything, but his bright eyes was cautious.
Someone who was able to reach the eighth level on their first try was definitely qualified to be his opponent. However, who was this person?
If he came from the bottom, he couldn't be a nobody.
Could it be that some hidden genius had been sent to the Heart Flow Sword Sect?
"Whoosh …"
When Luo Zheng reached the eighth floor, the silence of the Heart Flow Sword Sect was finally broken.
When Luo Zheng reached the fifth floor, everyone was stunned. When he reached the sixth floor, they were still stunned. Even so, they were still somewhat unconvinced. People like Zhu Chaoyun, Li Can, and the others could still compare this person to them.
But when Luo Zheng reached the seventh and eighth floors, they had subconsciously removed Luo Zheng from their scope of comparison.
This guy was not the same kind of person as them.
So at this moment, most people changed their minds. Instead, they were looking forward to seeing how far this weirdo Luo Zheng could go.
"Can he reach the ninth floor?"
"He's already tied with Mo Yijian's record when he first entered the Heart Flow Tower …"
"It'll be interesting if he breaks Mo Yijian's record!"
Many people looked at Mo Yijian, wanting to see what expression Mo Yijian would have.
It was impossible for them to catch up to Mo Yijian, but they still hoped that someone could surpass him …
However, Mo Yijian's expression was still very calm. He just stared at the Heart Flow Tower without saying a word.
Immediately afterward, the golden light in the Heart Flow Tower began to jump again and then steadily landed on the ninth floor.
"A new record … has appeared …" the coquettish woman said.
For a moment, the coquettish woman began to doubt the evaluation system of the Heart Flow Sword Sect. After all, this was a "bottom" person. Did something happen to the Heart Flow Tower again? It had clearly just been repaired!
But even when the golden light reached the ninth floor, it showed no signs of stopping.
In the tower, Luo Zheng felt that he had reached a limit.
The Emotion Severing Divine Dao was not omnipotent, even though it was the best way to resist the Heart Fire.
However, Luo Zheng's obsession was too strong. Those negative emotions that grew crazily were like tentacles that could not be shaken off, strangling his emotions. At the instant he leaped up, the might of the tenth level's Flame of Heart doubled again!
After passing the seventh floor, the Heart Fire seemed to have a strange magic power that could cause people to hallucinate.
These hallucinations were extremely realistic, making it impossible to distinguish between reality and illusion. Luo Zheng seemed to have fallen into a crazy scene.
In a single thought, he seemed to have experienced several months.
And during these several months, he saw the divine realm being destroyed by an invisible black hand, saw Luo Nian die tragically under the sword of someone else, saw Ning Yudie perish, saw the immortal residence shatter …
Even though he knew that these were all hallucinations, he could not extricate himself from these hallucinations. Instead, he felt that they were more and more real, and he could not extricate himself from them!
"I can't do it! Kill! "
Luo Zheng knew that if he held on for one more breath, he would probably sink into this hallucination forever.
He expended almost the last of his spiritual power, gritted his teeth, and rushed to the tenth floor, smashing open the door of the Heart Flow Tower.
"Bang!"
Everyone watched as the door to the tenth floor was smashed open, and a wet person fell out.
Luo Zheng was sweating profusely as if he had just been fished out of the water.
However, when he left the Heart Flow Tower, the Heart Fire also disappeared completely, and everything returned to normal. He landed steadily on the ground, and his eyes, which were full of confusion and regret, gradually regained their clarity.
Mo Yijian and Han 'er stared at the door of the Heart Flow Tower, wanting to see who this person was.
When they saw the person in front of them clearly, they were also stunned.
"Young Master … It's him!"
Han 'er covered her mouth, and her face was full of disbelief.
However, she should be aware of this. After all, she had spent time investigating this person.
Defeating Situ Xiu with the strength of the First Sky of the Nirvana Realm was originally an extremely abnormal thing. However, after Mo Yijian cut off one of Luo Zheng's swords, Luo Zheng inexplicably disappeared for a period of time.
